<html lang="es"><head><meta charset="utf-8">
<meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1, viewport-fit=cover, user-scalable=no">
<title>Las aventuras del Stickman · v32.0 (versión pública)</title>
<style>
:root{--bg:#10101a;--fg:#fff;box-sizing:border-box;padding-top:env(safe-area-inset-top,0px);padding-bottom:env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px)}
@media (prefers-color-scheme:dark){:root:not([data-theme="light"]){--bg:#10101a;--fg:#fff}}
:root[data-theme="dark"]{--bg:#10101a;--fg:#fff}
html,body{margin:0;height:100%;background:var(--bg);color:var(--fg);font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,"Segoe UI",Roboto,sans-serif;overflow:hidden;touch-action:none;user-select:none;-webkit-user-select:none}
#box{height:100%;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:8px}
canvas{width:min(100vw,calc((100vh - 110px) * 800 / 448));aspect-ratio:800/448;background:#5c94fc;display:block}
#tc{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;width:min(100vw,720px);padding:0 14px;box-sizing:border-box}
#tc div{display:flex;gap:12px}
#tc button{width:72px;height:60px;border-radius:14px;border:0;background:#2b2b44;color:#fff;font-size:26px;touch-action:none;box-shadow:0 4px 0 #14142a}
#tc button:active,#tc button.on{background:#ff4fa3}
#hint{font-size:12px;opacity:.7;text-align:center;padding:0 8px}
.tb{position:fixed;top:6px;background:#0008;color:#fff;border:0;border-radius:8px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:16px}
#mu{right:8px}#mn{right:56px}
</style></head><body>

function move(b,dt){
 b.vy=Math.min(900,b.vy+GR*dt);b.hx=false;b.bonk=null;
 b.x+=b.vx*dt;let r0=Math.floor(b.y/T),r1=Math.floor((b.y+b.h-.01)/T);
 if(b.vx>0){const c=Math.floor((b.x+b.w)/T);for(let r=r0;r<=r1;r++)if(solid(c,r)){b.x=c*T-b.w;b.hx=true;break}}
 else if(b.vx<0){const c=Math.floor(b.x/T);for(let r=r0;r<=r1;r++)if(solid(c,r)){b.x=(c+1)*T;b.hx=true;break}}
 b.y+=b.vy*dt;b.ground=false;const c0=Math.floor(b.x/T),c1=Math.floor((b.x+b.w-.01)/T);
 if(b.vy>0){const r=Math.floor((b.y+b.h)/T);for(let c=c0;c<=c1;c++)if(solid(c,r)){b.y=r*T-b.h;b.vy=0;b.ground=true;break}}
 else if(b.vy<0){const r=Math.floor(b.y/T),cc=Math.floor((b.x+b.w/2)/T),cs=solid(cc,r)?cc:(solid(c0,r)?c0:(solid(c1,r)?c1:-1));if(cs>=0){b.y=(r+1)*T;b.vy=0;b.bonk=[cs,r]}}
}

  if(K.ld===0){if(s%8!==7)sfxM(N(12,ARP[K.ar][s&7]+(bar&1?2:0)),sec*1.3,K.w,.024);if(bar>=2&&(s===0||s===3||s===6||s===8||s===10||s===12))sfxM(N(24,[0,2,4,2,5,4,7][(s+bar)%7]),sec*2.2,"square",.016)}
  else if(K.ld===1){if(s%2===0){const d=HK[K.ar][(s>>1)+(bar&1?8:0)];if(d>-9)sfxM(N(12,d),sec*1.9,K.w,.03)}if(s===0)sfxM(mf(ro),sec*15,"triangle",.016);if(s%4===3)sfxM(N(24,ARP[K.ar][s>>2]),sec*.8,"square",.01)}
  else if(K.ld===2){if(s===2||s===6||s===10||s===14||(s===11&&bar===3)){[0,2,4].forEach(d=>sfxM(N(12,d),sec*1.1,"square",.02))}if(s%2===0&&bar>=2)sfxM(N(24,ARP[K.ar][s>>1&7]),sec*1.2,"square",.012)}
  else{if(s%4===0)sfxM(N(12,ARP[K.ar][(s>>2)+(bar&1)*4&7]),sec*3.2,K.w,.03);if(s%4===3)sfxM(N(12,ARP[K.ar][(s>>2)+(bar&1)*4&7]),sec*1.6,K.w,.012);if(s%8===4)sfxM(N(24,4),sec*2,"triangle",.015)}
  if(s===0&&bar===0&&hv)sfxM(4800,.3,"square",.01,-3000)}

// ---- estados
function runEnd(){if(!inRun)return;inRun=false;if(Object.values(ups).some(v=>v>0))plays++;if(plays>=15){plays=0;Object.keys(ups).forEach(k=>ups[k]=0);noticeTxt="¡Pasaron 15 partidas con mejoras! Tus mejoras de la tienda desaparecieron. Tus monedas se quedan.";noticeUntil=performance.now()+9000}save()}
function newGame(n){wld=n>=14&&n!==99?1:0;if(wld)n-=14;inRun=n!==99;lvl=n;lap=0;score=0;coinsN=0;lives=DIFS[dif].lv+ups.lf+petLives();startLevel()}
function startLevel(){build();S="play";time=0;sfx(660,.15,"square",.06,400)}
function toMenu(){S="menu";shake=0;runEnd();save()}
const act={play:()=>newGame(clamp(gi(),0,unlocked-1)),next:()=>{if(!wld&&lvl===13){wld=1;lvl=0}else lvl++;startLevel()},retry:()=>newGame(gi())};
// ---- entrada
let KB={l:"a",r:"d",j:" ",at:"j"},CFG={sfx:1,shk:1},bindWait=null;try{const o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stk_cfg")||"null");if(o){Object.assign(KB,o.kb);Object.assign(CFG,o.c)}}catch(e){}
const inp={l:false,r:false,j:false,jp:false,at:false,mb:false,ck:false};
const KM={ArrowLeft:"l",a:"l",A:"l",ArrowRight:"r",d:"r",D:"r",j:"at",J:"at",x:"at",X:"at",k:"at",z:"at",Z:"at"},JK=["ArrowUp","w","W"," "];
addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.tagName==="INPUT")return;if(KM[e.key]){inp[KM[e.key]]=true;e.preventDefault()}else if(JK.includes(e.key)){if(!inp.j)inp.jp=true;inp.j=true;e.preventDefault()}
 else if(e.key==="Enter"){if(S==="menu")act.play();else if(S==="over")act.retry();else if(S==="win"&&gi()<23)act.next()}
 else if(e.key==="Escape"&&S!=="menu")toMenu();else if(e.key==="m"||e.key==="M"){musicOn=!musicOn;$("mu").textContent=musicOn?"🎵":"🔇"}});
addEventListener("keyup",e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.tagName==="INPUT")return;if(KM[e.key])inp[KM[e.key]]=false;else if(JK.includes(e.key))inp.j=false});
rebuildKeys();const bind=(id,k)=>{const b=$(id);const on=e=>{e.preventDefault();b.classList.add("on");if(k==="j"&&!inp.j)inp.jp=true;inp[k]=true},off=e=>{e.preventDefault();b.classList.remove("on");inp[k]=false};b.addEventListener("pointerdown",on);["pointerup","pointerleave","pointercancel"].forEach(n=>b.addEventListener(n,off))};
bind("bl","l");bind("br","r");bind("ba","at");bind("bj","j");
const setMs=e=>{const r=cv.getBoundingClientRect();ms.x=(e.clientX-r.left)*W/r.width;ms.y=(e.clientY-r.top)*H/r.height;ms.t=performance.now();ms.pt=e.pointerType||"mouse"};
cv.addEventListener("pointermove",setMs);
cv.addEventListener("pointerdown",e=>{setMs(e);if(S==="play"){if(e.button===0||e.pointerType!=="mouse"){inp.ck=true;inp.mb=true}}else{for(const b of btns)if(!b.dis&&ms.x>=b.x&&ms.x<=b.x+b.w&&ms.y>=b.y&&ms.y<=b.y+b.h){sfx(700,.08,"square",.05,300);b.fn();break}}});
addEventListener("pointerup",()=>{inp.mb=false});cv.addEventListener("contextmenu",e=>e.preventDefault());
$("mu").onclick=()=>{musicOn=!musicOn;$("mu").textContent=musicOn?"🎵":"🔇"};$("mn").onclick=()=>{if(S!=="menu")toMenu()};
// ---- utilidades
const pop=(x,y,t)=>pops.push({x,y,t,l:.8});
const burst=(x,y,col,n)=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++)parts.push({x,y,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*300,vy:-Math.random()*320,l:.7+Math.random()*.4,c:col})};
const add=(n,x,y)=>{score+=n;if(x!=null)pop(x,y,"+"+n)};
function hurt(){if(p.inv>0||p.sh>0||S!=="play")return;if(hurtAbs())return;if(p.hp<=1&&dif===3&&minis.some(m=>m.big&&m.act&&m.hp>0)){p.inv=1.4;p.vy=-300;p.vx=-p.f*170;shake=.2;pop(p.x+p.w/2,p.y-30,"¡NO PUEDE MATARTE!");sfx(300,.15,"triangle",.06,100);return}p.hp--;p.inv=1.6+(pAb("golem")?1.2:0);p.vy=-320;p.vx=-p.f*180;shake=.25;sfx(180,.3,"sawtooth",.08,-120);if(p.hp<=0)die()}
function die(){if(lvl!==99&&!(ups.rv&&!rvUsed))lives--;else if(lvl!==99){rvUsed=true;pop(p.x,p.y-30,"¡SEGUNDA OPORTUNIDAD!")}sfx(300,.6,"square",.07,-250);if(lives<=0){S="over";save();endRun();runEnd();return}
 p.x=cp;p.y=12*T-56;p.vx=p.vy=0;p.hp=HPM();p.inv=1.5;p.sh=p.dj=p.bg=p.spd=p.star=p.frz=p.mag=0;
 if(boss.active&&!boss.dead){resetBoss();balls=[];enemies=enemies.filter(e=>e.x<AR*T);for(let r=3;r<12;r++)G[r][AR]=0;gateOpen=true}}
function spawnItem(c,r){if(lvl!==99&&Math.random()>.6-.1*HD())return;let k=pickItem();if(k===0&&p.hp>=HPM())k=2;items.push({x:c*T+5,y:r*T-24,w:22,h:22,vx:0,vy:-260,k,ground:false});sfx(660,.15,"triangle",.07,500)}
function bump(c,r){const t=G[r][c];if(t===3){G[r][c]=4;gc();add(200,c*T+8,r*T-6);sfx(988,.08,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06);burst(c*T+16,r*T,"#ffd166",6);spawnItem(c,r)}
 else if(t===2){G[r][c]=0;add(50,c*T+8,r*T);sfx(150,.15,"square",.06,-60);burst(c*T+16,r*T+16,th.br,10)}}
const plantBox=pl=>{const q=((time+pl.ph)%3.4)/3.4,near=0;let f=q<.15?q/.15:q<.55?1:q<.7?(.7-q)/.15:0;pl.f=f;return{x:pl.x-18,y:pl.top-f*44,w:36,h:f*44}};
const swingA=(a,q)=>{const s=Math.cos(a)>=0?1:-1,e=Math.min(1,q/.65);return a+s*(-1.3+2.6*(1-(1-e)*(1-e)))};
// ---- jefes nuevos (niveles 7-14)
const BCFG={
 6:{m:"walk",sp:62,cs:360,jp:560,jn:3.4,jf:2.4,r:2.7,fr:1.8,a:["aim","rain","spike","charge","aim"],sk:0,c:"#f59e0b"},
 7:{m:"walk",sp:88,cs:470,jp:640,jn:2.6,jf:1.8,r:2.2,fr:1.5,a:["lob","wave","laser","aim","charge"],sk:1,c:"#22d3ee"},
 8:{m:"fly",r:2.2,fr:1.5,a:["aim","nova","rain","summon","aim"],sk:2,c:"#c084fc"},
 9:{m:"fly",r:2.1,fr:1.4,a:["lob","rain","nova","aim","wave"],sk:0,c:"#ff6b35"},
 10:{m:"fly",r:2.0,fr:1.3,a:["spike","aim","laser","rain","spike"],sk:2,c:"#facc15"},
 11:{m:"still",r:1.9,fr:1.3,a:["spike","wave","lob","summon","rain","aim"],sk:1,c:"#2dd4bf"},
 12:{m:"tele",r:2.0,fr:1.4,a:["aim","wave","summon","spike","laser","aim"],sk:2,c:"#a78bfa",tj:3.6},
 13:{m:"walk",sp:70,cs:430,jp:620,jn:2.8,jf:1.9,r:1.9,fr:1.2,a:["aim","lob","wave","laser","spike","rain","nova","summon","charge"],sk:1,c:"#f43f5e",sh:11,dbl:1}};
function bAtk(b,id,fury,DM,CF){
 const cx=b.x+b.w/2,cy=b.y+b.h/2,c=CF.c,S1=Math.min(DM,2),nE=enemies.filter(e=>!e.gone&&e.dead<=0&&e.x>AR*T).length;
 if(id==="charge"&&CF.m!=="walk")id="aim";
 if(id==="summon"&&nE>=3)id="aim";
 if(id==="aim"){const a=Math.atan2(p.y+p.h/2-cy,p.x-cx),n=fury?5:3;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const an=a+(i-(n-1)/2)*.28;balls.push({x:cx,y:cy,vx:Math.cos(an)*230*S1,vy:Math.sin(an)*230*S1,k:"o",c,w:16,h:16,l:0})}sfx(300,.3,"sawtooth",.06,-150)}
 else if(id==="lob"){const n=fury?4:3;for(let i=0;i<n;i++)balls.push({x:cx,y:b.y+20,vx:b.f*(120+i*80),vy:-430-i*45,g:1,k:"p",c,w:16,h:16,l:0});sfx(260,.3,"square",.06,-100)}
 else if(id==="rain"){rainF(fury?9:6,c,"f");pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡LLUVIA!")}
 else if(id==="spike"){(fury?[-150,-75,0,75,150]:[-80,0,80]).forEach(d=>balls.push({k:"s",x:clamp(p.x+p.w/2+d,(AR+2)*T,(COLS-4)*T),y:0,vx:0,vy:0,warn:.8,life:.7,l:0}));pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡PINCHOS!");sfx(200,.3,"square",.06,200)}
 else if(id==="wave"){[-1,1].forEach(s=>balls.push({x:cx,y:12*T-12,vx:s*230*S1,vy:0,w:44,h:24,k:"w",c,l:0}));shake=.3;sfx(80,.4,"sawtooth",.1,-30);pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡ONDA!")}
 else if(id==="laser"){balls.push({k:"z",x:0,y:Math.random()<.5?12*T-30:12*T-108,vx:0,vy:0,warn:.9,life:.5,l:0});pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡LÁSER!");sfx(900,.9,"sawtooth",.05,-500)}
 else if(id==="nova"){const n=fury?14:9;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=i/n*Math.PI*2+.2;balls.push({x:cx,y:cy,vx:Math.cos(a)*170,vy:Math.sin(a)*170,k:"f",c,w:16,h:16,l:0})}pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡NOVA!");sfx(180,.4,"sawtooth",.08,-100)}
 else if(id==="summon"){for(let i=0;i<2;i++)mkE(clamp(Math.floor(b.x/T)+(i?-5:5),AR+2,COLS-6),CF.sk,90+i);pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡REFUERZOS!");sfx(180,.4,"sawtooth",.07,200)}
 else if(id==="charge"){b.rl=1.5;b.df=b.f;pop(cx,b.y-14,"¡EMBESTIDA!");sfx(150,.5,"sawtooth",.08,200)}}
function bossNew(b,dt,fury,DM){
 const CF=BCFG[b.k],DB=Math.min(DM,2.1),minX=(AR+2)*T,maxX=(COLS-3)*T-b.w,wv=s=>balls.push({x:b.x+b.w/2,y:12*T-12,vx:s*215*DB,vy:0,w:44,h:24,k:"w",c:CF.c,l:0});let vul=true;
 b.ft-=dt;b.jt-=dt;b.sT-=dt;
 const rate=()=>(fury?CF.fr:CF.r)/DB*(b.k>=11&&b.k<=19?1.18:1),atk=()=>{b.ft=rate();bAtk(b,CF.a[Math.floor(Math.random()*CF.a.length)],fury,DM,CF);if(CF.dbl&&fury&&Math.random()<.5)bAtk(b,CF.a[Math.floor(Math.random()*CF.a.length)],fury,DM,CF)};
 if(CF.sh){if(b.shT>0){b.shT-=dt;vul=false}else{b.ca-=dt;if(b.ca<=0){b.ca=CF.sh;b.shT=2.6;pop(b.x,b.y-26,"¡ESCUDO!");sfx(600,.4,"sine",.06,300);if(!enemies.some(e=>!e.gone&&e.dead<=0&&e.x>AR*T))for(let i=0;i<2;i++)mkE(clamp(Math.floor(b.x/T)+(i?-5:5),AR+2,COLS-6),CF.sk,95+i)}}}
 if(CF.m==="walk"){
  if(b.rl>0){b.rl-=dt;b.vx=b.rl>.9?0:b.df*(fury?CF.cs*1.2:CF.cs)*DB;move(b,dt)}
  else{
   if(b.st===0){b.vx=b.hurt>0?0:b.f*(fury?CF.sp*1.5:CF.sp)*DB;if(b.jt<=0&&b.ground){b.vy=-CF.jp;b.st=1;b.ground=false;b.jt=(fury?CF.jf:CF.jn)/DB}}else b.vx=b.f*CF.sp*1.4*DB;
   if(b.ft<=0)atk();
   move(b,dt);
   if(b.st===1&&b.ground){b.st=0;shake=.35;sfx(80,.4,"sawtooth",.1,-30);wv(-1);wv(1)}}}
 else if(CF.m==="fly"){
  if(b.st===0){const tx=clamp(p.x-b.w/2+Math.sin(time*1.3)*140,minX,maxX);b.x+=clamp((tx-b.x)*1.5,-150*DB,150*DB)*dt;b.y+=(140+Math.sin(time*2)*20-b.y)*3*dt;
   if(b.ft<=0)atk();
   if(b.sT<=0){if(Math.random()<.5){b.st=1;b.sT=.6;b.tx=p.x-b.w/2;pop(b.x,b.y-20,"¡PICADA!")}else b.sT=(fury?1.6:2.4)/DB}}
  else if(b.st===1){if(b.sT<=0){b.st=2;b.tx=clamp(b.tx,minX,maxX);b.sT=2;sfx(200,.4,"sawtooth",.08,300)}}
  else if(b.st===2){const gy=12*T-b.h-6,dx=b.tx-b.x,dy=gy-b.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1,sp=(fury?540:440)*DB;b.x+=dx/d*Math.min(sp*dt,d);b.y+=dy/d*Math.min(sp*dt,d);if(d<8||b.sT<=0){b.st=3;b.sT=.7;shake=.3;wv(-1);wv(1)}}
  else if(b.st===3){if(b.sT<=0)b.st=4}
  else{b.y-=200*dt;if(b.y<=140){b.y=140;b.st=0;b.sT=(fury?1.8:2.8)/DB}}
  b.x=clamp(b.x,minX,maxX)}
 else if(CF.m==="still"){
  b.vx=0;
  if(b.st===0){if(b.ft<=0)atk();if(b.sT<=0){b.st=1;b.sT=2.6;pop(b.x,b.y-14,"¡SE SUMERGE!");sfx(120,.5,"sine",.07,-60)}}
  else{vul=false;if(b.ft<=0){b.ft=rate()*.7;bAtk(b,Math.random()<.5?"spike":"wave",fury,DM,CF)}if(b.sT<=0){b.st=0;b.sT=5.5;pop(b.x,b.y-14,"¡EMERGE!")}}
  move(b,dt)}
 else{
  if(b.tps===0){b.vx=b.hurt>0?0:b.f*(fury?90:60)*DB;b.tj-=dt;
   if(b.tj<=0){b.tps=1;b.tpt=.35;b.tj=(fury?CF.tj*.7:CF.tj)/DB;sfx(500,.3,"sine",.06,-300)}
   if(b.ft<=0)atk()}
  else{b.vx=0;b.tpt-=dt;if(b.tpt<=0){if(b.tps===1){b.x=clamp(p.x+(Math.random()<.5?-1:1)*(170+Math.random()*90),minX,maxX);b.tps=2;b.tpt=.35;sfx(300,.3,"sine",.06,400);if(fury)bAtk(b,"nova",fury,DM,CF)}else b.tps=0}}
  move(b,dt);vul=b.tps===0}
 return vul}
// ---- bailes de victoria (cada nivel baila distinto)
const DANCES=[{n:"SALTITOS"},{n:"GIRO LOCO"},{n:"FLOSS"},{n:"ROBOT"},{n:"MACARENA"},{n:"SALTO MORTAL"},{n:"DISCO"},{n:"TWIST"},{n:"MOONWALK"},{n:"HEADBANG"},{n:"PATADAS"},{n:"COSACO"},{n:"POSE ÉPICA"},{n:"COMBO FINAL"}];
function danceFX(P,t,id){
 if(id===13){const seq=[1,2,5,6];id=seq[Math.floor(t/2.4)%4];t=t%2.4}
 const S=Math.sin,C=Math.cos,PI=Math.PI,G=P.G,B=P.bodyG,H=P.headG,aL=P.aL,aR=P.aR,lL=P.lL,lR=P.lR;
 G.rotation.set(0,0,0);G.position.x=0;G.position.z=0;B.rotation.set(0,0,0);
 if(id===0)return;
 if(id===1){G.rotation.y=t*7;G.position.y=Math.abs(S(t*5))*.5}
 else if(id===2){const s=S(t*8);G.position.x=-s*.35;B.rotation.z=s*.18;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.9-s*1.3);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,.9-s*1.3);aL.el.rotation.z=-s*.9;aR.el.rotation.z=s*.9;lL.h.rotation.z=.15+s*.12;lR.h.rotation.z=-.15+s*.12}
 else if(id===3){const q=Math.floor(t*3.2)%4,sn=q%2?1:-1;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-1.57);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,1.57);aL.el.rotation.z=q<2?1.57:-1.57;aR.el.rotation.z=q<2?-1.57:1.57;H.rotation.y=sn*.7;G.position.y=0;B.rotation.y=sn*.25;lL.h.rotation.z=.12;lR.h.rotation.z=-.12}
 else if(id===4){const q=Math.floor(t*2)%8,u=(t*2)%1;aL.sh.rotation.set(q<2?-1.4:0,0,q<2?-.2:-.6-(q>3?.8:0));aR.sh.rotation.set(q>=1&&q<3?-1.4:0,0,q>=1&&q<3?.2:.6+(q>4?.8:0));aL.el.rotation.z=q>=4?-1.9:-.3;aR.el.rotation.z=q>=5?1.9:.3;G.position.x=S(t*4)*.2;B.rotation.z=S(t*4)*.12;G.position.y=Math.abs(S(t*4))*.1}
 else if(id===5){const u=(t%1.6)/1.6,a=u*PI*2,h=Math.sin(u*PI)*1.2;G.rotation.x=-a;G.position.y=1.5+h-1.5*Math.cos(a);G.position.z=-1.5*Math.sin(a);aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.5);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,.5);lL.kn.rotation.x=-.8;lR.kn.rotation.x=-.8}
 else if(id===6){const s=Math.floor(t*2)%2?1:-1;aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,s>0?2.5:.4);aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,s>0?-.4:-2.5);aR.el.rotation.z=.2;aL.el.rotation.z=-.2;G.position.x=s*.15;B.rotation.z=-s*.12;G.position.y=Math.abs(S(t*6))*.12;H.rotation.z=s*.15}
 else if(id===7){const s=S(t*6);G.rotation.y=s*.9;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.5-s*.3);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,.5-s*.3);lL.kn.rotation.x=-.35*(1+s);lR.kn.rotation.x=-.35*(1-s);G.position.y=-.1+Math.abs(s)*.08}
 else if(id===8){const s=S(t*.9);G.position.x=s*1.4;G.rotation.y=s>0?.5:-.5;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.35);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,.35);const w=S(t*9);lL.h.rotation.x=w*.5;lR.h.rotation.x=-w*.5;lL.kn.rotation.x=Math.max(0,-w)*.7;lR.kn.rotation.x=Math.max(0,w)*.7;G.position.y=0;B.rotation.x=-.08}
 else if(id===9){const s=S(t*8);B.rotation.x=.5*s;H.rotation.x=.4*s;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-2.3);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,2.3);aL.el.rotation.z=.9;aR.el.rotation.z=-.9;lL.h.rotation.z=.3;lR.h.rotation.z=-.3;G.position.y=Math.abs(s)*.1}
 else if(id===10){const s=S(t*6);lL.h.rotation.x=-1.3*Math.max(0,s);lR.h.rotation.x=-1.3*Math.max(0,-s);aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-1.1);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,1.1);aL.sh.rotation.x=.6*s;aR.sh.rotation.x=-.6*s;G.position.y=Math.abs(S(t*6))*.12;B.rotation.x=-.1}
 else if(id===11){const s=S(t*6),d=Math.abs(s);G.position.y=-.55;lL.kn.rotation.x=-1.4;lR.kn.rotation.x=-1.4;lL.h.rotation.x=s>0?-1.2:-.5;lR.h.rotation.x=s>0?-.5:-1.2;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.9);aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,.9);aL.el.rotation.z=-1.6;aR.el.rotation.z=1.6;B.rotation.z=s*.1}
 else if(id===12){G.rotation.y=S(t*.8)*.7;aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,2.95);aR.el.rotation.z=.05;aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-.55);aL.el.rotation.z=-1.2;B.rotation.x=-.08;H.rotation.set(-.1,0,0);G.position.y=Math.abs(S(t*2))*.12;lL.h.rotation.z=.28;lR.h.rotation.z=-.28}}

// ---- ENDLESS infinito, jefes bebés, poderes nuevos, bloque de la suerte
let dBase=0,maxD=0,miniN=0,minis=[],nextArenaD=40,bgOff=0,flashUntil=0;
const distM=()=>dBase+Math.floor(p.x/T)-2;
function genEndless(a,e,first){gen(Math.min(Math.max(lvl,1)+lap,9),a,e);let gd=0;
 while(gd++<8){const col=nextArenaD-dBase+2;if(col>e-30)break;if(col>=a+4)makeArena(col);nextArenaD+=Math.max(55,90-lap*3)}}
function makeArena(q){const x0=(q-2)*T,x1=(q+28)*T;
 for(let r=0;r<12;r++)for(let c=q-2;c<=q+27;c++)G[r][c]=0;
 gaps=gaps.filter(a=>a[1]<q-2||a[0]>q+27);coins=coins.filter(c=>c.x<x0||c.x>x1);plants=plants.filter(pl=>pl.x<x0||pl.x>x1);
 cr(q+6,9,3);cr(q+18,9,3);mkMini(q,q+25,nextArenaD)}
function mkMini(q0,q1,D){const k=miniN%14,sl=lvl,sb=boss;lvl=k;resetBoss();const t=boss;lvl=sl;boss=sb;
 const big=miniN%4===3,s=big?.8:.42,W0=t.w,H0=t.h,w=Math.round(W0*s),h=Math.round(H0*s),fly=[2,8,9,10].includes(k),hp=big?Math.min(12+miniN*2,45):Math.min(3+Math.floor(miniN*1.1),16);
 minis.push({k,t,s,W0,H0,w,h,x:(q0+17)*T,y:fly?12*T-h-90:12*T-h,fly,hp,max:hp,hurt:0,inv:0,f:-1,act:false,ft:1.5,aL:q0*T+10,aR:(q1+1)*T-10,D,dead:0,done:0,n:miniN,big});miniN++}
function endEnemies(a,e){const w=WT[Math.min(lvl+Math.floor(lap/2),13)]||WT[0];let i=50+Math.floor(R()*50);const P=Math.min(.14+.014*lap,.4);
 const inA=c=>minis.some(m=>c*T>=m.aL-90&&c*T<=m.aR+90);
 for(let c=a;c<e;c++){if(inA(c))continue;if(G[12][c]===1&&G[12][c-1]===1&&G[12][c+1]===1&&!G[11][c]&&!G[11][c-1]&&!G[11][c+1]&&R()<P){let r=R(),k=0;for(;k<6&&r>w[k];k++)r-=w[k];mkE(c,k,i++)}}
 const ex=Math.min(2+lap,10);for(let j=0;j<ex;j++){const c=a+Math.floor(R()*(e-a));if(!inA(c))mkE(c,R()<.5?2:4,i++)}}
function safeX(c){for(let d=0;d<24;d++){const q=c-d;if(q>2&&G[12][q]&&G[12][q+1]&&G[12][q-1]&&!G[11][q]&&!G[11][q+1]&&!G[10][q]&&!minis.some(m=>q*T>m.aL-60&&q*T<m.aR+60))return q*T}return null}
function endUpd(){const d=distM();if(d>maxD){score+=(d-maxD)*3;maxD=d}lap=Math.floor(Math.max(d,0)/150);
 if(p.x>150*T)scrollWorld();
 if(p.ground&&p.x>cp+640){const s=safeX(Math.floor(p.x/T));if(s!=null)cp=s}}
function scrollWorld(){const n=100,dx=n*T;let cut=-1;
 for(let q=238;q>=190;q--){if(q*T<p.x+T*40)break;if(minis.some(m=>q*T>m.aL-90&&q*T<m.aR+90))continue;let ok=G[12][q-1]&&G[12][q]&&G[12][q+1];for(let r=0;r<12&&ok;r++)if(G[r][q-1]||G[r][q]||G[r][q+1])ok=false;if(ok){cut=q;break}}
 if(cut<0){if(p.x>195*T)cut=238;else return}
 const cx=cut*T;
 for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++){G[r][COLS-1]=0;G[r][COLS-2]=0;G[r].fill(0,cut)}
 coins=coins.filter(o=>o.x<cx);plants=plants.filter(o=>o.x<cx);enemies=enemies.filter(o=>o.x<cx);items=items.filter(o=>o.x<cx);
 const lost=minis.filter(m=>m.aR>=cx&&m.hp>0);if(lost.length)nextArenaD=Math.min(nextArenaD,...lost.map(m=>m.D));minis=minis.filter(m=>m.aR<cx);
 for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++){G[r].copyWithin(0,n);G[r].fill(0,COLS-n)}
 [coins,enemies,items,balls,parts,pops,plants].forEach(a=>a.forEach(o=>{o.x-=dx}));minis.forEach(m=>{m.x-=dx;m.aL-=dx;m.aR-=dx});
 coins=coins.filter(o=>o.x>-300);enemies=enemies.filter(o=>o.x>-400);plants=plants.filter(o=>o.x>-300);items=items.filter(o=>o.x>-300);minis=minis.filter(m=>m.aR>-400);
 p.x-=dx;cam-=dx;bgOff+=dx;dBase+=n;boss.x-=dx;
 const s=safeX(Math.floor(p.x/T));cp=s!=null?s:p.x;
 gaps=[];const a=cut-n;genEndless(a,238,false);
 for(let c=a;c<COLS-2;c++)if(!gaps.some(g2=>c>=g2[0]&&c<=g2[1])){G[12][c]=1;G[13][c]=1}
 for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++){G[r][COLS-1]=1;G[r][COLS-2]=1}
 endEnemies(a+2,238)}
const endTrack=()=>{const m=minis.find(m=>m.act&&m.hp>0);return m?"boss"+m.k:"L"+((lvl+Math.floor(Math.max(distM(),0)/200))%14)};
function minisUpd(dt,hit,DM){const fz=p.frz>0?.3:1;
 minis.forEach(m=>{if(m.hp<=0){if(!m.done){m.done=1;m.dead=.8;const cx=m.x+m.w/2;add((m.big?900:300)+m.n*150,cx,m.y-10);pop(cx,m.y-34,m.big?"¡JEFE DERROTADO!":"¡JEFE BEBÉ DERROTADO!");burst(cx,m.y+m.h/2,BC[m.k],22);shake=.3;sfx(300,.3,"sawtooth",.08,-200);sfx(880,.2,"square",.06,600);
   for(let i=0;i<9;i++)coins.push({x:cx+(i-4)*20,y:12*T-30-Math.abs(i-4)*(-7)-28,got:false});items.push({x:cx-11,y:m.y,w:22,h:22,vx:0,vy:-300,k:pickItem(),ground:false})}else m.dead-=dt;return}
  m.hurt=Math.max(0,m.hurt-dt);m.inv=Math.max(0,m.inv-dt);if(p.x+p.w>m.aR){p.x=m.aR-p.w;if(p.vx>0)p.vx=0;if(!m.warn||time-m.warn>2){m.warn=time;pop(p.x+p.w/2,p.y-30,m.big?"¡Derrota al jefe!":"¡Derrota al jefe bebé!");sfx(180,.15,"square",.06,-60)}}const cx=m.x+m.w/2,pc=p.x+p.w/2;
  if(!m.act){if(Math.abs(cx-pc)<470&&pc>m.aL-300){m.act=true;pop(cx,m.y-24,m.big?"¡JEFE GRANDE!":"¡JEFE BEBÉ!");sfx(200,.3,"sawtooth",.07,-80)}else return}
  const sp=Math.min(48+m.n*7,120)*DM*fz;m.f=pc<cx?-1:1;
  if(pc>m.aL&&pc<m.aR)m.x+=m.f*sp*dt;else m.x+=Math.sign((m.aL+m.aR)/2-cx)*sp*.6*dt;
  m.x=clamp(m.x,m.aL,m.aR-m.w);const hop=m.fly?Math.sin(time*2.5+m.n)*30:-Math.abs(Math.sin(time*6+m.n))*14;m.y=m.fly?12*T-m.h-90+hop:12*T-m.h+hop;
  m.ft-=dt*fz;if(m.ft<=0&&Math.abs(cx-pc)<540){m.ft=Math.max(1.05,2.7-m.n*.13)/DM;const fx=m.x+m.w/2,fy=m.y+m.h/2,an=Math.atan2(p.y+p.h/2-fy,pc-fx),v=165+Math.min(m.n*8,95),nb=1+(m.n>=4?1:0)+(m.n>=9?1:0);
   for(let i=0;i<nb;i++){const a=an+(i-(nb-1)/2)*.3;balls.push({x:fx,y:fy,vx:Math.cos(a)*v,vy:Math.sin(a)*v,g:0,k:"a",c:BC[m.k],w:12,h:12,l:0})}sfx(260,.12,"square",.05,-120)}
  const hb={x:m.x,y:m.y,w:m.w,h:m.h};
  if(m.inv<=0&&hit(hb)){m.hp-=1+(p.bg>0?1:0);m.hurt=.25;m.inv=.35;burst(cx,m.y+m.h/2,BC[m.k],8);sfx(260,.12,"square",.06,-100);add(100,cx,m.y)}
  else if(ov(p,hb)){if(m.inv<=0&&p.vy>80&&p.y+p.h-m.y<26){m.hp--;m.hurt=.25;m.inv=.5;p.vy=inp.j?-600:-400;sfx(220,.12,"square",.07,-120);add(100,cx,m.y)}else if(p.star>0&&m.inv<=0){m.hp-=2;m.hurt=.25;m.inv=.4}else hurt()}});
 minis=minis.filter(m=>!(m.hp<=0&&m.dead<=0&&m.done))}
function drawMinis(){minis.forEach(m=>{if(m.hp>0){const x=m.aR-cam;if(x>-20&&x<W+20){g.save();const a=.3+.2*Math.sin(time*6);g.fillStyle="rgba(255,70,120,"+a+")";g.fillRect(x-4,0,8,12*T);g.fillStyle="rgba(255,220,235,.8)";g.fillRect(x-1,0,2,12*T);for(let i=0;i<8;i++){g.fillRect(x-6,((time*90+i*56)%(12*T)),12,3)}g.restore()}}});minis.forEach(m=>{if(m.x-cam>W+200||m.x-cam<-300)return;const t=m.t,sv=boss,fa=m.hp<=0?Math.max(0,m.dead/.8):1;
 t.active=true;t.dead=false;t.f=m.f;t.hurt=m.hurt;t.hp=Math.max(1,m.hp);t.max=m.max;t.vx=0;t.w=m.W0;t.h=m.H0;t.x=m.x+m.w/2-m.W0/2;t.y=m.y+m.h-m.H0;
 const fx=m.x+m.w/2-cam,fy=m.y+m.h;g.save();g.globalAlpha=fa;g.translate(fx,fy);g.scale(m.s,m.s);g.translate(-fx,-fy);boss=t;try{drawBoss()}catch(e){}boss=sv;g.restore();
 if(m.hp>0&&m.act){g.fillStyle="#000a";g.fillRect(m.x-cam,m.y-12,m.w,6);g.fillStyle=BC[m.k];g.fillRect(m.x-cam+1,m.y-11,(m.w-2)*m.hp/m.max,4)}})}
// ---- poderes nuevos
function pickItem(){const t=lvl===99?0:(dif===3?Math.min(13,lvl+lap):lvl);let L=[0,0,1,1,2,2,2,3,3,4];if(t>=2)L=L.concat([7,7]);if(t>=4)L=L.concat([6,6]);if(t>=6)L=L.concat([5,8]);if(t>=9)L=L.concat([5,6,8]);if(dif===4)L=L.filter(x=>x!==0&&x!==1);return L[Math.floor(Math.random()*L.length)]}
function powerUp(k,PW){if(k===5){p.star=9*PW;p.sh=Math.max(p.sh,p.star);p.spd=Math.max(p.spd,p.star)}else if(k===6)p.frz=10*PW;else if(k===7)p.mag=12*PW;else if(k===8)bombBlast()}
function bombBlast(){shake=.6;flashUntil=performance.now()+320;sfx(90,.6,"sawtooth",.1,-60);sfx(60,.9,"square",.08,-40);
 enemies.forEach(e=>{if(!e.gone&&!e.dead&&e.x>cam-60&&e.x<cam+W+60){e.dead=.5;add(100,e.x,e.y);burst(e.x+e.w/2,e.y+e.h/2,"#fb923c",8)}});
 balls=balls.filter(b=>b.x<cam-60||b.x>cam+W+60);plants.forEach(pl=>{if(pl.x>cam-60&&pl.x<cam+W+60)pl.dead=true});
 minis.forEach(m=>{if(m.hp>0&&m.act&&m.x>cam-100&&m.x<cam+W+100){m.hp-=3;m.hurt=.4;m.inv=.4}});
 if(boss.active&&!boss.dead&&boss.inv<=0){boss.hp-=2;boss.hurt=.5;boss.inv=.6}}
function magUpd(dt){coins.forEach(c=>{if(c.got)return;const dx=p.x+p.w/2-c.x,dy=p.y+p.h/2-c.y;if(Math.hypot(dx,dy)<320){const s=Math.min(1,dt*7);c.x+=dx*s;c.y+=dy*s}})}
function itemIcon(k){if(k===5){g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const a=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,r=i%2?3.6:8.5;g.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r)}g.closePath();g.fill()}
 else if(k===6){g.lineWidth=2;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(Math.cos(a)*8,Math.sin(a)*8);g.lineTo(-Math.cos(a)*8,-Math.sin(a)*8);g.stroke()}}
 else if(k===7){g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,6,Math.PI,0);g.lineTo(6,7);g.moveTo(-6,0);g.lineTo(-6,7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(-8,5,4,4);g.fillRect(4,5,4,4)}
 else{const c=g.fillStyle;g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,2,7,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle=c;g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(3,-4);g.quadraticCurveTo(6,-9,9,-7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(9,-7,2,0,7);g.fill()}}
function starAura(){const x=p.x+p.w/2-cam,y=p.y+p.h/2;g.save();g.strokeStyle="hsl("+((time*300)%360|0)+",100%,60%)";g.lineWidth=4;g.globalAlpha=.8;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,36+Math.sin(time*10)*2,0,7);g.stroke();g.restore();if(Math.random()<.6)parts.push({x:p.x+p.w/2+(Math.random()-.5)*30,y:p.y+p.h/2+(Math.random()-.5)*50,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*60,vy:-40,l:.4,c:"hsl("+((Math.random()*360)|0)+",100%,65%)"})}
function frzFX(){g.fillStyle="rgba(125,211,252,.13)";g.fillRect(-10,-10,W+20,H+20);g.fillStyle="#fff";for(let i=0;i<30;i++){g.fillRect((i*97+time*20*(1+i%3))%W,(time*60*(1+i%2)+i*53)%H,2,2)}}
function flashFX(){const r=flashUntil-performance.now();if(r>0){g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+Math.min(1,r/200)+")";g.fillRect(-10,-10,W+20,H+20)}}
// ---- bloque de la suerte (nuevo diseño)
function luckyTile(q,c,x,y){const bob=q?-Math.abs(Math.sin(time*4+c))*1.5:0;
 if(q){const gr=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+T);gr.addColorStop(0,"#ffe680");gr.addColorStop(1,"#f59e0b");g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(x,y,T,T);
  g.fillStyle="#fff8c9";g.fillRect(x,y,T,3);g.fillRect(x,y,3,T);g.fillStyle="#b45309";g.fillRect(x,y+T-3,T,3);g.fillRect(x+T-3,y,3,T);
  g.strokeStyle="#7c2d12";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(x+1,y+1,T-2,T-2);g.fillStyle="#92400e";[[4,4],[T-7,4],[4,T-7],[T-7,T-7]].forEach(a=>g.fillRect(x+a[0],y+a[1],3,3));
  g.save();g.beginPath();g.rect(x,y,T,T);g.clip();const sw=((time*1.1+c*.37)%2.2)*T*1.6-T*.6;g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.5)";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+sw,y);g.lineTo(x+sw+7,y);g.lineTo(x+sw-8,y+T);g.lineTo(x+sw-15,y+T);g.fill();g.restore();
  g.textAlign="center";g.font="bold 20px monospace";g.lineWidth=3;g.strokeStyle="#7c2d12";g.strokeText("?",x+16,y+23+bob);g.fillStyle=Math.sin(time*6)>.7?"#fff":"#fff7ed";g.fillText("?",x+16,y+23+bob);
  g.fillStyle="#fff";if(Math.sin(time*3+c)>.8){g.fillRect(x+T-9,y+6,2,6);g.fillRect(x+T-11,y+8,6,2)}}
 else{g.fillStyle="#6b4f2a";g.fillRect(x,y,T,T);g.fillStyle="#8a6a3d";g.fillRect(x,y,T,3);g.fillRect(x,y,3,T);g.fillStyle="#3f2d17";g.fillRect(x,y+T-3,T,3);g.fillRect(x+T-3,y,3,T);g.strokeStyle="#2b1d0e";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(x+1,y+1,T-2,T-2);g.fillStyle="#3f2d17";[[4,4],[T-7,4],[4,T-7],[T-7,T-7]].forEach(a=>g.fillRect(x+a[0],y+a[1],3,3));g.strokeStyle="#3f2d17";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+9,y+10);g.lineTo(x+15,y+16);g.lineTo(x+13,y+23);g.moveTo(x+24,y+8);g.lineTo(x+20,y+14);g.stroke()}}

// ---- configuración, teclas y combos
function saveCfg(){try{localStorage.setItem("stk_cfg",JSON.stringify({kb:KB,c:CFG}))}catch(e){}}
function rebuildKeys(){Object.keys(KM).forEach(k=>delete KM[k]);KM.ArrowLeft="l";KM.ArrowRight="r";JK.length=0;JK.push("ArrowUp");
 const add=(k,a)=>{if(a==="j"){JK.push(k);if(k.length===1)JK.push(k.toUpperCase())}else{KM[k]=a;if(k.length===1)KM[k.toUpperCase()]=a}};
 add(KB.l,"l");add(KB.r,"r");add(KB.j,"j");add(KB.at,"at");
 if(KB.at==="j")["x","k","z"].forEach(k=>add(k,"at"));if(KB.j===" ")add("w","j")}
addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(S==="settings"&&bindWait){e.preventDefault();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(e.key!=="Escape"&&e.key!=="Enter"){KB[bindWait]=e.key.length===1?e.key.toLowerCase():e.key;rebuildKeys();saveCfg()}bindWait=null}},true);
const keyName=k=>k===" "?"ESPACIO":k.startsWith("Arrow")?({ArrowLeft:"←",ArrowRight:"→",ArrowUp:"↑",ArrowDown:"↓"})[k]:k.toUpperCase();
function drawSettings(mt){
 g.fillStyle="#070714ea";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);g.strokeStyle="#7dd3fc88";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(8,8,W-16,H-16,18);g.stroke();
 txt("CONFIGURACIÓN",26,56,32,"#7dd3fc");
 txt("TECLAS (clic y presiona la tecla nueva)",34,92,13,"#ffd166");
 [["l","IZQUIERDA"],["r","DERECHA"],["j","SALTAR"],["at","ESPADA / ATACAR"]].forEach((o,i)=>{const y=104+i*54;txt(o[1],40,y+24,15,"#fff");button(250,y,150,36,bindWait===o[0]?"¿…?":keyName(KB[o[0]]),()=>{bindWait=bindWait===o[0]?null:o[0]},bindWait===o[0])});
 txt("También: flechas ← → ↑ y clic izquierdo = espada",34,332,11,"#94a3b8");
 button(40,346,360,34,"RESTABLECER TECLAS",()=>{KB={l:"a",r:"d",j:" ",at:"j"};rebuildKeys();saveCfg();bindWait=null});
 txt("OPCIONES",470,92,13,"#ffd166");
 button(450,104,300,40,"MÚSICA: "+(musicOn?"SÍ":"NO"),()=>{musicOn=!musicOn;$("mu").textContent=musicOn?"🎵":"🔇"},musicOn);
 button(450,158,300,40,"EFECTOS DE SONIDO: "+(CFG.sfx?"SÍ":"NO"),()=>{CFG.sfx=CFG.sfx?0:1;saveCfg()},!!CFG.sfx);
 button(450,212,300,40,"SACUDIDA DE PANTALLA: "+(CFG.shk?"SÍ":"NO"),()=>{CFG.shk=CFG.shk?0:1;saveCfg()},!!CFG.shk);
 txt("Esc = volver al menú · M = música",450,282,11,"#94a3b8");txt("↓ o S en el aire = CULATAZO (rompe ladrillos)",450,312,11,"#ffd166");txt("T = hablar con personajes",450,332,11,"#ffd166");
 button(300,396,200,36,"VOLVER",()=>{bindWait=null;S="menu"})}
const COMBOS=[
 {n:"PACK HÉROE",it:{skin:"fu",body:"rj",back:"cr",face:"an",sword:"hi"}},
 {n:"PACK NINJA",it:{skin:"vi",body:"ni",face:"ga",back:"mo",sword:"ka"}},
 {n:"PACK MAGO",it:{hat:"mg",body:"tm",back:"cm",face:"mn",sword:"mg"}},
 {n:"PACK CABALLERO",it:{hat:"cc",body:"kn",back:"es",face:"bi",sword:"fi"}},
 {n:"PACK ESPACIAL",it:{skin:"hi",body:"es",back:"jp",face:"m3",sword:"lv"}},
 {n:"PACK ÁNGEL",it:{skin:"fa",hat:"ha",body:"tm",back:"aa",face:"fe",sword:"or"}},
 {n:"PACK DRAGÓN",it:{skin:"fu",hat:"vk",body:"dr",back:"ad",face:"en",sword:"dr"}},

 {n:"PACK MARIPOSA",it:{skin:"vi",hat:"gl",back:"bt",face:"bl",sword:"mg"}},
 {n:"PACK REY DORADO",it:{skin:"or",hat:"co",body:"kd",back:"cd",face:"mn",sword:"or"}},
 {n:"PACK ARCOÍRIS",it:{skin:"ar",hat:"gl",back:"cx",face:"ec",sword:"ar"}}];
const cbList=c=>Object.keys(c.it).map(s=>({s,it:COS[s].find(o=>o.id===c.it[s])})),
 cbFull=c=>cbList(c).reduce((a,o)=>a+o.it.c,0),cbPrice=c=>Math.max(10,Math.round(cbList(c).reduce((a,o)=>a+(isOwned(o.s,o.it.id)?0:o.it.c),0)*.6/10)*10),
 cbOwned=c=>cbList(c).every(o=>isOwned(o.s,o.it.id)),cbEq=c=>cbList(c).every(o=>cos.eq[o.s]===o.it.id);
function cbBuy(c){const l=cbList(c);if(!cbOwned(c)){const pr=cbPrice(c);if(wallet<pr){sfx(150,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-50);noticeTxt="Te faltan "+(pr-wallet)+" monedas";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2200;return}wallet-=pr;l.forEach(o=>cos.own[o.s+":"+o.it.id]=1);sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06);noticeTxt="¡Compraste el "+c.n+"!";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2500}
 else sfx(660,.1,"square",.06);l.forEach(o=>cos.eq[o.s]=o.it.id);save();saveCos()}
function drawCombos(mt){
 const per=6,pages=Math.ceil(COMBOS.length/per);shopPg=clamp(shopPg,0,pages-1);const pg=COMBOS.slice(shopPg*per,shopPg*per+per);let hov=null;
 pg.forEach((c,j)=>{const x=228+(j%2)*280,y=104+Math.floor(j/2)*92;if(ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+270&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+82)hov=c});
 g.fillStyle="#0e0e2a";rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();const pgd=g.createRadialGradient(114,250,10,114,250,130);pgd.addColorStop(0,"#ff6b3d55");pgd.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=pgd;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffffff22";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.stroke();
 g.fillStyle="#0007";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(114,338,52,8,0,0,7);g.fill();
 const sv=Object.assign({},cos.eq);if(hov)Object.assign(cos.eq,{skin:"cl",hat:"no",body:"no",back:"no",face:"no",sword:"cl"},hov.it);
 g.save();rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.clip();drawChar(104,338,2.55,mt,-.6+Math.sin(mt*2)*.1);g.restore();Object.assign(cos.eq,sv);
 txt(hov?"PROBANDO…":"TU ESTILO",114,128,13,hov?"#ff9ff3":"#ffd166","center");
 pg.forEach((c,j)=>{const x=228+(j%2)*280,y=104+Math.floor(j/2)*92,w=270,h=82,own=cbOwned(c),eq=cbEq(c),pr=cbPrice(c),fu=cbFull(c);
  button(x,y,w,h,"",()=>cbBuy(c),eq);
  txt(c.n,x+10,y+22,13,"#fff");
  cbList(c).forEach((o,i)=>miniIcon(o.s,o.it.id,x+10+i*31,y+34,28));
  if(eq)txt("EQUIPADO ✓",x+w-8,y+20,12,"#7ae582","right");else if(own)txt("EQUIPAR",x+w-8,y+20,12,"#7dd3fc","right");
  else{g.fillStyle="#ef4444";rrect(x+w-52,y+7,44,17,8);g.fill();txt("-"+Math.round(100-pr/fu*100)+"%",x+w-30,y+20,12,"#fff","center");txt("● "+pr,x+w-8,y+72,15,wallet>=pr?"#ffd166":"#ef4444","right");txt("● "+fu,x+w-8,y+60,10,"#94a3b8","right");g.fillStyle="#94a3b8";g.fillRect(x+w-8-(""+fu).length*6-14,y+57,(""+fu).length*6+14,1)}});
 button(228,388,90,36,"◀",()=>shopPg=Math.max(0,shopPg-1),false,shopPg===0);txt((shopPg+1)+" / "+pages,W/2+90,412,15,"#fff","center");button(440,388,90,36,"▶",()=>shopPg=Math.min(pages-1,shopPg+1),false,shopPg===pages-1);
 button(600,388,178,36,"VOLVER",()=>S="menu");
 txt("Combos completos con 40% de descuento",24,392,11,"#fb923c");txt("Lo que ya tienes se descuenta",24,408,10,"#94a3b8");
 if(performance.now()<noticeUntil){g.fillStyle="#1e3a8aee";rrect(170,20,330,32,10);g.fill();txt(noticeTxt,335,41,13,"#fff","center")}}

// ---- v27.7: personajes, mascotas, culatazo
let wld=0;const gi=()=>lvl+14*wld,bnw=k=>BN[k],bnm=i=>BN[i];
let chs={own:{},met:{},eq:"",lv:{},pown:{},peq:""},pendTalk=null,talk=null,npcs=[],npcPlaced=false,dnKey=false,pound=false,petUsed=0,drgT=7,talkDecl={},pet={x:0,y:0,f:1};
try{Object.assign(chs,JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stk_chs")||"{}"))}catch(e){}
const OKP={chancito:1,dragon:1,slime:1,fantasma:1,robot:1,fenix:1,nube:1,golem:1,grifo:1,cerbero:1,behemot:1,unicornio:1};let sit=false;
try{const old={firu:200,michi:250,conejo:180,rana:220,pollito:260,tortuga:240};let rf=0;for(const k in chs.pown)if(!(k in OKP)){rf+=old[k]||0;delete chs.pown[k]}if(chs.peq&&!(chs.peq in OKP))chs.peq="";if(rf){wallet+=rf;save();localStorage.setItem("stk_chs",JSON.stringify(chs))}}catch(e){}
["janja","luna","roco"].forEach(id=>{if(chs.lv[id]==null)chs.lv[id]=1+Math.floor(Math.random()*12)});
function saveChs(){try{localStorage.setItem("stk_chs",JSON.stringify(chs))}catch(e){}}saveChs();
const CHARS=[
{id:"janja",n:"JANJA",t:"Ninja de las sombras",ab:"Doble salto siempre y +10% de velocidad",c:300,hint:"Escondida en algún nivel…"},
{id:"luna",n:"LUNA",t:"Maga de las estrellas",ab:"Imán de monedas permanente",c:350,hint:"Escondida en algún nivel…"},
{id:"roco",n:"ROCO",t:"Caballero de roca",ab:"+1 corazón y escudo al empezar cada nivel",c:400,hint:"Escondido en algún nivel…"},
{id:"ignacio",n:"IGNACIO",t:"Guardián legendario",ab:"+2 corazones y espada con 40% más de alcance",c:1200,ex:1,hint:"Llega a 1200 m en Endless"},
{id:"luca",n:"LUCA",t:"El corredor veloz",ab:"+25% velocidad, salto alto y monedas x2",c:1500,ex:1,hint:"Llega a 1500 m en Endless"},
{id:"drago",n:"DRAGO",t:"Domador de fuego",ab:"Llamarada que quema todo cada 7 segundos",c:2000,ex:1,hint:"Vence a Drago al inicio del nivel 14"}];
const PETS=[
{id:"chancito",n:"Chancito",ab:"Cerdito de Minecraft: +50% de monedas",c:150,sc:1.05},
{id:"dragon",n:"Dragoncito",ab:"Escupe llamarada cada 14 s",c:420,sc:1.2},
{id:"slime",n:"Gelatina",ab:"Rebota: salto 15% más alto",c:200,sc:1.05},
{id:"fantasma",n:"Fantasmín",ab:"Ignora el 1er golpe de cada nivel",c:260,sc:1.05},
{id:"robot",n:"Robotín",ab:"Imán: atrae las monedas",c:240,sc:1.05},
{id:"fenix",n:"Fénix",ab:"Renace: empiezas con 1 vida extra",c:360,sc:1.1},
{id:"nube",n:"Nubecita",ab:"Planeo: mantén saltar para flotar",c:280,sc:1.05},
{id:"golem",n:"Golem",ab:"Más tiempo invencible al ser golpeado",c:240,sc:1.05},
{id:"grifo",n:"Grifo",ab:"Planea y salta 25% más alto",c:520,sc:1.2},
{id:"cerbero",n:"Cerbero",ab:"Espada +50% y llamarada cada 10 s",c:680,sc:1.2},
{id:"behemot",n:"Behemot",ab:"+2 corazones y golpea más fuerte",c:820,sc:1.35},
{id:"unicornio",n:"Unicornio",ab:"+20% velocidad y salto +15%",c:900,sc:1.2}];
const psc=id=>(PETS.find(x=>x.id===id)||{sc:1.05}).sc;
const DLG={
janja:{i:"…Shhh. ¿Me viste llegar? Nadie me ve nunca. Soy Janja, la ninja de las sombras.",q:[["¿Quién eres?","Una ninja sin clan. Entreno en silencio para ser la más ágil del reino."],["¿Qué sabes hacer?","Salto dos veces en el aire y corro como el viento. Los enemigos ni me ven."],["¿Te perdiste?","¿Perdida? Los ninjas no nos perdemos… tomamos rutas secretas."]],j:"Me caes bien, stickman. Búscame en la tienda y viajaré contigo."},
luna:{i:"¡Oh! Un viajero. Las estrellas dijeron que vendrías. Soy Luna, maga del cielo.",q:[["¿Qué haces aquí?","Sigo la lluvia de monedas. Las estrellas dejan monedas por todo el camino."],["¿Qué puedes hacer?","Mi magia atrae el oro hacia ti. Nunca más dejarás una moneda atrás."],["¿Eres peligrosa?","Solo con los enemigos que molestan a mis amigos."]],j:"Será un honor ayudarte. Encuéntrame en la tienda y mi magia será tuya."},
roco:{i:"¡Alto! ...Perdón, costumbre. Soy Roco, caballero de piedra. ¿Vienes en son de paz?",q:[["Vengo en paz","Entonces eres bienvenido. Pocos caminantes se detienen a hablar con un viejo guerrero."],["¿Por qué tanta armadura?","Porque un golpe de más duele. Mi escudo de roca protege a mis amigos."],["¿Puedes ayudarme?","Siempre. Un corazón más y un escudo al comenzar el camino, eso te ofrezco."]],j:"Pasaré por la tienda. Si me llevas, ningún enemigo te tocará fácil."},
ignacio:{i:"Llegaste a los 1200 metros del Endless... Pocos resisten tanto. Soy Ignacio, el guardián de este reino.",q:[["¿Eras el guardián?","Vigilé estas tierras mucho tiempo. Solo quien resiste el Endless merece conocerme."],["¿Qué poder tienes?","Mi espada legendaria llega más lejos y mi corazón aguanta más golpes."],["¿Por qué me eliges?","Porque nunca te rendiste. Eso vale más que cualquier espada."]],j:"Estaré en la tienda esperando. Seremos leyenda juntos."},
luca:{i:"¡Wow, llegaste a los 1500 metros! ¡Soy Luca, el más rápido del Endless!",q:[["¿Cómo eres tan veloz?","Corro desde que nací. Cada metro del Endless es mío."],["¿Qué ganas conmigo?","Más velocidad, saltos altísimos y el doble de monedas."],["¿Hasta dónde llega el Endless?","Dicen que no tiene fin… ¡y yo quiero comprobarlo contigo!"]],j:"¡Genial! Nos vemos en la tienda. ¡Prepárate para correr!"},
drago:{i:"Me venciste... Eres digno. Soy Drago. El fuego me obedece y ahora te reconoce.",q:[["¿Quién eres?","Un domador de llamas. Viajo por el reino buscando a alguien digno."],["¿Qué haces con el fuego?","Cada tanto desato una llamarada que quema todo a mi alrededor."],["¿Es peligroso?","Solo para tus enemigos. Contigo seré leal."]],j:"Hecho. Búscame en la tienda y juntos incendiaremos el camino."}};
// habilidades
const chAb=id=>chs.eq===id,pAb=id=>chs.peq===id,magOn=()=>p.mag>0||chAb("luna")||pAb("robot"),
 spdBonus=()=>(ups.xs||0)*.08+(chAb("joaquin")?.15:0)+(chAb("janja")?.1:0)+(chAb("luca")?.25:0)+(pAb("unicornio")?.2:0),hpBonus=()=>(ups.xh||0)+(chAb("roco")?1:0)+(chAb("ignacio")?2:0)+(pAb("behemot")?2:0),swBonus=()=>(ups.xw||0)*.1+(chAb("ignacio")?.4:0)+(pAb("cerbero")?.5:0)+(pAb("behemot")?.2:0),
 jumpBonus=()=>(ups.xj||0)*.1+(chAb("joaquin")?.12:0)+(chAb("luca")?.1:0)+(pAb("slime")?.15:0)+(pAb("grifo")?.25:0)+(pAb("unicornio")?.15:0),petLives=()=>(ups.xl||0)+(pAb("fenix")?1:0),
 cm=v=>{const m=v*((pAb("chancito")?1.5:1)*(chAb("luca")?2:1));return Math.floor(m)+(Math.random()<m%1?1:0)};
function hurtAbs(){if(pAb("fantasma")&&!petUsed){petUsed=1;p.inv=1.4;pop(p.x+p.w/2,p.y-30,"¡9 VIDAS!");sfx(700,.2,"triangle",.06,200);burst(p.x+p.w/2,p.y+p.h/2,"#fde047",10);return true}return false}
function chBuild(){npcs=[];npcPlaced=false;pound=false;petUsed=0;drgT=7;talkDecl={};pet.x=p.x;pet.y=p.y+p.h;if(chAb("roco"))p.sh=Math.max(p.sh,8);if(ups.xp)p.sh=Math.max(p.sh,3);dgInit()}
function placeNpcs(){npcPlaced=true;if(lvl===99||dif===3||wld)return;let k=0;CHARS.forEach(c=>{if(c.ex||chs.met[c.id]||chs.lv[c.id]!==lvl)return;
 for(let q=55+k*45;q<AR-40;q++){let ok=true;for(let d=0;d<3&&ok;d++){if(G[12][q+d]!==1)ok=false;for(let r=8;r<12;r++)if(G[r][q+d])ok=false}if(ok){npcs.push({id:c.id,x:(q+1)*T+16});k++;break}}})}

// ---- duelo con Drago (inicio del nivel 14)
let dgo=null,dgF=[];
const DGL=["¡Alto, viajero! Soy Drago, domador de fuego.","Para seguir tendrás que vencerme.","¡El fuego me obedece!","¡Nada mal... pero no basta!","¡Siente mi llamarada!","Pocos me han hecho retroceder.","¡Sigue luchando, stickman!","¿Eso es todo lo que tienes?"];
const dgOn=()=>!!(dgo&&dgo.hp>0);
function dgInit(){dgo=null;dgF=[];if(lvl===99||dif===3||wld||lvl!==13||chs.met.drago)return;
 for(let r=0;r<12;r++)for(let c=0;c<=25;c++)G[r][c]=0;for(let c=0;c<=25;c++){G[12][c]=1;G[13][c]=1}
 enemies=enemies.filter(e=>e.x>27*T);plants=plants.filter(q=>q.x>27*T);balls=[];items=items.filter(i=>i.x>27*T);coins=coins.filter(c=>c.x>27*T);
 const mx=dif===4?20:dif===0?10:14;dgo={x:16*T,y:12*T-54,w:24,h:54,f:-1,hp:mx,mx,inv:0,hurt:0,t:0,st:"walk",tt:0,cd:2.4,at:"",q:0,li:0,sp:.3,lt:0,line:"",dead:0,fury:false,vx:0}}
function dgSay(l,t){dgo.line=l;dgo.lt=t||3}
function dgUpd(dt,hit){if(!dgo)return;const d=dgo;
 if(d.hp<=0){d.dead+=dt;d.vx=0;if(d.dead>2.4){dgo=null;dgF=[];chs.met.drago=1;saveChs();startTalk("drago","play")}return}
 p.x=clamp(p.x,0,25*T-p.w);d.t+=dt;d.inv-=dt;d.hurt-=dt;d.lt-=dt;d.sp-=dt;d.f=p.x+p.w/2<d.x+d.w/2?-1:1;
 if(d.sp<=0&&d.t>.2){d.sp=3.6;if(d.lt<=0)dgSay(DGL[d.li++%DGL.length],3.2)}
 if(!d.fury&&d.hp<=d.mx*.45){d.fury=true;dgSay("¡Ahora voy en serio!",2.6);shake=.3;sfx(110,.4,"sawtooth",.08,-40)}
 const dist=Math.abs(p.x-d.x);
 if(d.st==="walk"){d.vx=dist>190?d.f*85:dist<110?-d.f*55:0;d.cd-=dt;if(d.cd<=0&&d.t>2){d.at=Math.random()<.5?"fire":"dash";d.st="tel";d.tt=d.at==="fire"?.7:.6;d.vx=0}}
 else if(d.st==="tel"){d.vx=0;d.tt-=dt;if(d.tt<=0){if(d.at==="fire"){dgF.push({x:d.x+d.w/2+d.f*20,y:12*T-22,vx:d.f*250,w:26,h:18});d.q=d.fury?.35:.5;d.st="fire";d.tt=.55;sfx(200,.25,"sawtooth",.07,-80)}else{d.st="dash";d.tt=.55;d.vx=d.f*(d.fury?430:360);sfx(260,.3,"sawtooth",.07,-100)}}}
 else if(d.st==="fire"){d.vx=0;d.tt-=dt;if(d.q>0){d.q-=dt;if(d.q<=0){dgF.push({x:d.x+d.w/2+d.f*20,y:12*T-52,vx:d.f*270,w:26,h:18});sfx(220,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-80)}}if(d.tt<=0){d.st="walk";d.cd=d.fury?1.4:2.2}}
 else if(d.st==="dash"){d.tt-=dt;if(d.tt<=0){d.st="rec";d.tt=.95;d.vx=0}}
 else if(d.st==="rec"){d.vx=0;d.tt-=dt;if(d.tt<=0){d.st="walk";d.cd=d.fury?1.2:1.9}}
 d.x=clamp(d.x+d.vx*dt,20,25*T-30-d.w);
 const bx={x:d.x,y:d.y,w:d.w,h:d.h};
 if(ov(p,bx))hurt();
 dgF.forEach(f=>{f.x+=f.vx*dt;if(ov(p,f)){f.gone=true;hurt();burst(f.x,f.y,"#fb923c",8)}if(f.x<-40||f.x>25*T+40)f.gone=true});dgF=dgF.filter(f=>!f.gone);
 if(d.inv<=0&&hit(bx)){const dm=d.st==="rec"?2:1;d.hp-=dm;d.inv=.5;d.hurt=.35;shake=.2;burst(d.x+d.w/2,d.y+20,"#fb923c",10);pop(d.x+d.w/2,d.y-12,"-"+dm);sfx(420,.12,"square",.07,-120);d.x=clamp(d.x+p.f*18,20,25*T-30-d.w);
  if(d.hp<=0){d.dead=.01;dgF=[];dgSay("Imposible... me has vencido. Eres digno.",3);shake=.5;sfx(80,.6,"sawtooth",.1,-40)}else if(Math.random()<.25)dgSay(["¡Ugh!","¡Buen golpe!","¡Aún no caigo!"][Math.floor(Math.random()*3)],1.4)}}
function dgDraw(){if(!dgo)return;const d=dgo,x=d.x+d.w/2-cam,y=d.y+d.h;
 dgF.forEach(f=>{const fx=f.x-cam,fy=f.y+f.h/2,fl=Math.sin(time*30)*2;g.fillStyle="#f9731666";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(fx,fy,22,13,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fb923c";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(fx,fy,14+fl,9,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(fx+(f.vx>0?3:-3),fy,7,5,0,0,7);g.fill()});
 g.save();g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,y,18,4,0,0,7);g.fill();
 if(d.hp<=0){g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(Math.min(1.5,d.dead*1.3)*d.f);g.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,1-d.dead/2.6);g.translate(-x,-y)}
 else if(d.hurt>0&&Math.floor(d.hurt*30)%2)g.globalAlpha=.45;
 if(d.st==="tel"){const rg=g.createRadialGradient(x,y-30,4,x,y-30,50);rg.addColorStop(0,"#fb923caa");rg.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=rg;g.fillRect(x-60,y-90,120,100)}
 if(d.st==="rec"){txt("¡ABIERTO!",x,y-76,11,"#fde047","center")}
 g.translate(x,y);g.scale(d.f<0?-1:1,1);g.translate(-x,-y);charArt("drago",x,y,.95,time);g.restore();
 if(d.hp>0){g.fillStyle="#000a";rrect(250,44,300,22,8);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#7f1d1d";rrect(254,48,292,14,6);g.fill();g.fillStyle=d.fury?"#ef4444":"#f97316";const w=Math.max(0,292*d.hp/d.mx);if(w>4){rrect(254,48,w,14,6);g.fill()}txt("DRAGO",400,59,11,"#fff","center")}
 if(d.lt>0&&d.line){const L=wrapT(d.line,26),bw=Math.min(230,Math.max(...L.map(l=>l.length))*7.4+22),bh=L.length*15+14,bx=clamp(x-bw/2,8,W-bw-8),by=d.y-bh-26;
  g.fillStyle="#1f1b2ef2";rrect(bx,by,bw,bh,10);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#f97316";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(bx,by,bw,bh,10);g.stroke();
  g.fillStyle="#1f1b2ef2";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-6,by+bh-1);g.lineTo(x+4,by+bh-1);g.lineTo(x,by+bh+10);g.fill();L.forEach((l,i)=>txt(l,bx+bw/2,by+17+i*15,12,"#fff","center"))}}
function nearNpc(){return npcs.find(n=>Math.abs(p.x+p.w/2-n.x)<64)}
function startTalk(id,back){const d=DLG[id];talk={id,back,st:0,txt:d.i};S="talk";talkDecl[id]=true;sfx(520,.1,"triangle",.05,200)}
function closeTalk(){if(!talk)return;S=talk.back;talk=null}
function talkOpts(){const d=DLG[talk.id];if(talk.st===0)return d.q.map(q=>[q[0],()=>{talk.txt=q[1];talk.st=1}]);
 if(talk.st===1)return[["¡Únete a mi aventura!",()=>{chs.met[talk.id]=1;saveChs();talk.txt=d.j;talk.st=2;sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.25,"square",.06);npcs=npcs.filter(n=>n.id!==talk.id)}],["Quizás luego",closeTalk]];
 return[["¡Genial! (ya aparece en la tienda)",closeTalk]]}
function wrapT(s,n){const o=[];let l="";s.split(" ").forEach(w=>{if((l+" "+w).trim().length>n){o.push(l);l=w}else l=(l+" "+w).trim()});if(l)o.push(l);return o}
function drawTalk(mt){btns=[];g.fillStyle="#000b";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);const c=CHARS.find(o=>o.id===talk.id);
 g.fillStyle="#0e0e2af2";rrect(24,196,752,240,18);g.fill();g.strokeStyle=c.ex?"#ffd166":"#7dd3fc";g.lineWidth=3;rrect(24,196,752,240,18);g.stroke();
 const rg=g.createRadialGradient(104,330,10,104,300,110);rg.addColorStop(0,c.ex?"#ffd16666":"#7dd3fc55");rg.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=rg;g.fillRect(30,200,150,230);charArt(talk.id,104,330,2.1,mt);
 txt(c.n,196,228,20,c.ex?"#ffd166":"#7dd3fc");txt(c.t,196+c.n.length*13,228,11,"#94a3b8");
 wrapT(talk.txt,60).forEach((l,i)=>txt(l,196,256+i*20,14,"#fff"));
 talkOpts().forEach((o,i)=>button(196,340+i*32,556,28,(i+1)+". "+o[0],o[1]));
 txt("Esc = cerrar",W-40,222,10,"#64748b","right")}
function chTick(){if(!chs.met.joaquin&&CHARS.some(c=>c.id==="joaquin")){chs.met.joaquin=1;saveChs()}}
function chUpd(dt){
 if(!npcPlaced)placeNpcs();
 if(dif===3&&lvl!==99){const m=distM();if(m>=1200&&!chs.met.ignacio&&!talkDecl.ignacio)startTalk("ignacio","play");else if(m>=1500&&!chs.met.luca&&!talkDecl.luca)startTalk("luca","play")}
 if((pAb("nube")||pAb("grifo"))&&!p.ground&&p.vy>60&&inp.j)p.vy=Math.min(p.vy,110);
 if(chAb("drago")||pAb("dragon")||pAb("cerbero")){drgT-=dt;if(drgT<=0){drgT=chAb("drago")?7:pAb("cerbero")?10:14;bombBlast()}}
 if(pound&&p.ground){pound=false;const r=Math.floor((p.y+p.h+2)/T);let hit=0;const ex=chs.peq?16:0;for(let c=Math.floor((p.x-ex)/T);c<=Math.floor((p.x+p.w-1+ex)/T);c++){const t=G[r]&&G[r][c];if(t===2||t===3){bump(c,r);G[r][c]=0;hit++}}
  shake=.25;sfx(70,.25,"square",.09,-30);for(let i=-3;i<=3;i++)parts.push({x:p.x+p.w/2,y:p.y+p.h-3,vx:i*55,vy:-60,l:.4,c:hit?"#ffd166":"#e8e8e8"});if(hit)pop(p.x+p.w/2,p.y-26,"¡CULATAZO!")}
 else if(pound&&p.vy<500)pound=false;
 if(!p.ground&&dnKey&&!pound&&p.vy>-250){pound=true;p.vx*=.2;p.vy=820;sfx(260,.18,"sawtooth",.06,-200)}
 else if(pound)p.vy=Math.max(p.vy,820);
 pet.f=p.f;const tx=p.x+p.w/2-p.f*36;pet.x+=(tx-pet.x)*Math.min(1,dt*6);pet.y+=(p.y+p.h-pet.y)*Math.min(1,dt*12);if(Math.abs(pet.x-p.x)>380)pet.x=tx}
function drawNpcs(){dgDraw();npcs.forEach(n=>{const x=n.x-cam;if(x<-60||x>W+60)return;g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,12*T,16,4,0,0,7);g.fill();charArt(n.id,x,12*T,.9,time);
 txt("!",x,12*T-92+Math.sin(time*4)*3,22,"#fde047","center");if(nearNpc()===n){txt("[T] HABLAR",x,12*T-110,12,"#fff","center")}})}
function drawRide(){if(!chs.peq){drawPlayer();return}const u=3*psc(chs.peq),mv=Math.abs(p.vx)>40||!p.ground,hop=mv?-Math.abs(Math.sin(time*9))*2.4*u:0;
 petArt(chs.peq,p.x+p.w/2-cam,p.y+p.h,u/3,time,p.f,mv);g.save();g.translate(0,24-10*u+hop);sit=true;drawPlayer();sit=false;g.restore()}
function drawPet(){if(!chs.peq)return;const mv=Math.abs(p.vx)>40||!p.ground;petArt(chs.peq,pet.x-cam,pet.y,.85,time,pet.f,mv)}
addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(e.target&&e.target.tagName==="INPUT")return;
 if(S==="talk"&&talk){if(e.key==="Escape"){e.stopImmediatePropagation();e.preventDefault();closeTalk();return}const o=talkOpts(),n=parseInt(e.key);if(n>=1&&n<=o.length){e.stopImmediatePropagation();o[n-1][1]()}return}
 if(e.key==="ArrowDown"||e.key==="s"||e.key==="S"){dnKey=true;if(e.key==="ArrowDown")e.preventDefault()}
 if((e.key==="t"||e.key==="T")&&S==="play"){const n=nearNpc();if(n)startTalk(n.id,"play")}},true);
addEventListener("keyup",e=>{if(e.key==="ArrowDown"||e.key==="s"||e.key==="S")dnKey=false});
addEventListener("blur",()=>{dnKey=false});
// ---- arte
function charArt(id,x,y,s,t,sil){const C={janja:["#111827","#f1f5f9","#ef4444"],luna:["#6d28d9","#fde6c8","#fde047"],roco:["#6b7280","#fde6c8","#9ca3af"],ignacio:["#d97706","#fde6c8","#facc15"],luca:["#2563eb","#fde6c8","#facc15"],drago:["#b91c1c","#fde6c8","#f97316"],joaquin:["#0ea5e9","#fde6c8","#38bdf8"]}[id],bob=Math.sin(t*3)*1.4,b=sil?"#0b0b1a":C[0],h=sil?"#0b0b1a":C[1],a=sil?"#0b0b1a":C[2],hy=-60+bob;
 if(id==="joaquin")s*=.78;g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.scale(s,s);g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.strokeStyle=b;g.lineWidth=5;
 if(!sil&&id==="luna"){g.fillStyle="#4c1d95";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-2,-48);g.lineTo(-20,-4+Math.sin(t*3)*2);g.lineTo(2,-8);g.closePath();g.fill()}
 if(!sil&&id==="ignacio"){g.fillStyle="#b91c1c";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-2,-48);g.lineTo(-22,-2+Math.sin(t*3)*2);g.lineTo(4,-8);g.closePath();g.fill()}
 if(!sil&&id==="luca"){g.strokeStyle="#93c5fd";g.lineWidth=2;for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const o=((t*60+i*14)%30);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-18-o,-20-i*14);g.lineTo(-34-o,-20-i*14);g.stroke()}g.strokeStyle=b;g.lineWidth=5}
 g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-26);g.lineTo(-8,0);g.moveTo(0,-26);g.lineTo(8,0);g.moveTo(0,-26);g.lineTo(0,-50+bob);g.moveTo(0,-47+bob);g.lineTo(-12,-34);g.moveTo(0,-47+bob);g.lineTo(12,-35+Math.sin(t*4)*3);g.stroke();
 g.fillStyle=h;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy,10,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();
 if(sil){g.fillStyle="#7dd3fc";g.font="bold 20px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillText("?",0,hy+7);g.restore();return}
 const eye=()=>{g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(-4,hy-2,2.4,3);g.fillRect(2,hy-2,2.4,3)};
 if(id==="janja"){g.fillStyle="#111827";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy,10.4,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#f1f5f9";g.fillRect(-8,hy-4,16,5);g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(-5,hy-3,3,3);g.fillRect(2,hy-3,3,3);g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.fillRect(-10.4,hy-8,20.8,4);g.strokeStyle="#ef4444";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(9,hy-6);g.quadraticCurveTo(20,hy-4+Math.sin(t*6)*4,28,hy-12+Math.sin(t*5)*5);g.moveTo(9,hy-6);g.quadraticCurveTo(18,hy+2+Math.sin(t*6)*3,24,hy+4);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.fillRect(-6,-52+bob,12,4);g.strokeStyle="#9ca3af";g.lineWidth=2.5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,-52);g.lineTo(8,-80);g.stroke()}
 else if(id==="luna"){eye();g.fillStyle="#6d28d9";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-14,hy-7);g.lineTo(14,hy-7);g.lineTo(3,hy-36);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#5b21b6";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,hy-7,16,4,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const q=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,r=i%2?2:5;g.lineTo(2+Math.cos(q)*r,hy-22+Math.sin(q)*r)}g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#92400e";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(14,-36);g.lineTo(18,-70);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(18,-72,4+Math.sin(t*5),0,7);g.fill()}
 else if(id==="roco"){eye();g.fillStyle="#9ca3af";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy-1,11,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillRect(-11,hy-1,22,3);g.fillStyle="#374151";g.fillRect(-1,hy-10,2,10);g.fillStyle="#dc2626";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,hy-11);g.quadraticCurveTo(-8,hy-24,-14,hy-14);g.lineTo(-2,hy-11);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#9ca3af";g.fillRect(-5,-50+bob,10,24);g.strokeStyle="#4b5563";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(-5,-50+bob,10,24);g.fillStyle="#6b7280";g.beginPath();g.arc(-16,-34,9,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#dc2626";g.fillRect(-18,-42,4,16)}
 else if(id==="joaquin"){eye();if(!sil){g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(-5.5,hy,5.4,0,7);g.moveTo(10.9,hy);g.arc(5.5,hy,5.4,0,7);g.moveTo(-.1,hy);g.lineTo(.1,hy);g.moveTo(-11,hy-1);g.lineTo(-14,hy-3);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#38bdf855";g.beginPath();g.arc(-5.5,hy,5,0,7);g.arc(5.5,hy,5,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#3b2412";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy-2,10.5,Math.PI*1.05,Math.PI*1.95);g.fill()}}
 else if(id==="ignacio"){eye();g.fillStyle="#facc15";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,hy-8);g.lineTo(-9,hy-18);g.lineTo(-4,hy-12);g.lineTo(0,hy-20);g.lineTo(4,hy-12);g.lineTo(9,hy-18);g.lineTo(9,hy-8);g.closePath();g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#92400e";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#d97706";g.fillRect(-5,-50+bob,10,24);g.strokeStyle="#f8fafc";g.lineWidth=5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(14,-36);g.lineTo(26,-86);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#facc15";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(10,-40);g.lineTo(20,-44);g.stroke()}
 else if(id==="luca"){eye();g.fillStyle="#facc15";g.fillRect(-10,hy-8,20,4);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-8,hy-9);g.lineTo(-4,hy-19);g.lineTo(0,hy-9);g.lineTo(4,hy-19);g.lineTo(8,hy-9);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.6;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy+2,4,.2,2.94);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#f8fafc";g.fillRect(-12,-3,10,3);g.fillRect(3,-3,10,3)}
 else if(id==="drago"){eye();g.fillStyle="#991b1b";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,hy-1,11,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,hy-6);g.lineTo(-14,hy-20);g.lineTo(-4,hy-9);g.fill();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(9,hy-6);g.lineTo(14,hy-20);g.lineTo(4,hy-9);g.fill();const fl=3+Math.sin(t*14)*2;g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.beginPath();g.arc(15,-38,4+fl*.4,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(15,-38,2,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#7f1d1d";g.fillRect(-5,-50+bob,10,22)}
 g.restore()}
function petArt(id,x,y,s,t,fl,mv){const u=3*s,hop=mv?-Math.abs(Math.sin(t*9))*2.4:0,lg=mv?Math.sin(t*12)*1:0;g.save();g.translate(x,y+hop*u);g.scale(u*(fl<0?-1:1),u);
 const R=(c,a,b,w,h)=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(a,b,w,h)};
 g.fillStyle="#0003";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,-hop,8,1.4,0,0,7);g.fill();
 if(id==="chancito"){R("#e58a96",-6,-3+lg*.4,2.2,3);R("#e58a96",-2,-3-lg*.4,2.2,3);R("#e58a96",2,-3+lg*.4,2.2,3);R("#e58a96",5,-3-lg*.4,2.2,3);R("#a64d5c",-6,-.8,2.2,.8);R("#a64d5c",2,-.8,2.2,.8);R("#a64d5c",-2,-.8,2.2,.8);R("#a64d5c",5,-.8,2.2,.8);R("#f4a5b0",-7,-10,14,7);R("#e58a96",-7,-10,14,1.5);R("#f4a5b0",5,-12,7,7);R("#e58a96",5.5,-13,2.2,1.6);R("#f9c4cb",10,-9.5,2.6,3.2);R("#c0586a",10.6,-8.6,.7,1);R("#c0586a",11.8,-8.6,.7,1);R("#fff",8,-11,1.6,1.6);R("#3b2a5a",8.6,-11,.9,1.6);R("#e58a96",-8,-9,1.4,1.4)}
 else if(id==="firu"){R("#8a5a30",-6,-3+lg*.4,2,3);R("#8a5a30",-2,-3-lg*.4,2,3);R("#8a5a30",2,-3+lg*.4,2,3);R("#8a5a30",5,-3-lg*.4,2,3);R("#b07a4a",-7,-10,13,7);R("#b07a4a",5,-14,7,7);R("#e0b88a",10,-10.5,3.4,3.4);R("#222",12.4,-10.5,1.2,1.2);R("#6b4423",4.6,-14.5,2.4,5);R("#fff",8,-12.6,1.6,1.6);R("#222",8.6,-12.6,.9,1.4);R("#b07a4a",-9,-13+Math.sin(t*14)*1.5,1.8,5)}
 else if(id==="michi"){R("#6b7280",-6,-3+lg*.4,2,3);R("#6b7280",-2,-3-lg*.4,2,3);R("#6b7280",2,-3+lg*.4,2,3);R("#6b7280",4,-3-lg*.4,2,3);R("#9ca3af",-6,-10,12,7);R("#9ca3af",3.5,-15,7.5,7);R("#9ca3af",3.5,-17.5,2,2.5);R("#9ca3af",9,-17.5,2,2.5);R("#fda4af",4.1,-16.8,.9,1.5);R("#4ade80",5.4,-13,1.5,1.5);R("#4ade80",8.4,-13,1.5,1.5);R("#111",5.9,-13,.6,1.5);R("#111",8.9,-13,.6,1.5);R("#fda4af",7,-11.2,1.2,1);R("#9ca3af",-8,-10,1.6,5);R("#9ca3af",-8,-16+Math.sin(t*5)*.8,1.6,6);R("#6b7280",-8,-12,1.6,1)}
 else if(id==="conejo"){R("#d1d5db",-5,-3+lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#d1d5db",-1,-3-lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#d1d5db",3,-3,2.4,3);R("#f3f4f6",-6,-10,11,7);R("#f3f4f6",3,-14,6.5,6.5);R("#f3f4f6",3.8,-22,2,8.5);R("#f3f4f6",6.8,-22,2,8.5);R("#fda4af",4.2,-20,1,5);R("#fda4af",7.2,-20,1,5);R("#111",7,-12,1.2,1.2);R("#fda4af",9,-10.4,1,1);R("#fff",-8,-8,2.8,2.8)}
 else if(id==="rana"){R("#22c55e",-7,-4,4,4);R("#22c55e",3,-3,4,3);R("#4ade80",-5,-9,11,6);R("#4ade80",2,-12,3.5,3.5);R("#4ade80",6,-12,3.5,3.5);R("#fff",2.6,-11.6,2.4,2.4);R("#fff",6.6,-11.6,2.4,2.4);R("#111",3.6,-11,1.2,1.4);R("#111",7.6,-11,1.2,1.4);R("#166534",4,-6,6,.9);R("#bbf7d0",-3,-6.5,7,3)}
 else if(id==="pollito"){R("#fb923c",-2.4,-2.4,.9,2.4+lg*.3);R("#fb923c",1.6,-2.4,.9,2.4-lg*.3);R("#fde047",-5,-10,10,8);R("#fde047",-3,-11,6,1.6);R("#fde047",0,-16,7,7);R("#fb923c",7,-13.4,3,2);R("#111",4.6,-14,1.2,1.2);R("#facc15",-3,-8+Math.sin(t*10)*.8,5,3);R("#facc15",2,-18,1,2.4);R("#facc15",3.6,-18.6,1,2.4)}
 else if(id==="slime"){const sq=mv?Math.sin(t*10)*.8:Math.sin(t*3)*.3;R("#10b981",-8,-1,16,1);R("#34d399",-8,-7-sq,16,6+sq);R("#34d399",-6.5,-9-sq,13,2);R("#a7f3d0",-5,-8.6-sq,4,1.4);R("#fff",1.5,-6.4-sq,3,3);R("#111",2.9,-5.6-sq,1.4,1.8);R("#fff",-4,-6.4-sq,3,3);R("#111",-2.6,-5.6-sq,1.4,1.8);R("#065f46",-1,-3,3,.8)}
 else if(id==="fantasma"){const fl=-1.5+Math.sin(t*4)*1;g.globalAlpha=.92;R("#f1f5f9",-7,-10+fl,14,9);R("#f1f5f9",-5,-12+fl,10,2);R("#f1f5f9",-7,-1+fl,4,2.4);R("#f1f5f9",-1.5,-1+fl,4,1.6);R("#f1f5f9",3.5,-1+fl,3.5,2.4);R("#cbd5e1",-7,-4+fl,14,.8);R("#111",1,-8+fl,2,2.6);R("#111",-3.5,-8+fl,2,2.6);R("#fda4af",3.5,-5.5+fl,2,1);R("#f1f5f9",7,-6+fl,3,1.6);g.globalAlpha=1}
 else if(id==="robot"){R("#64748b",-5,-3+lg*.4,3,3);R("#64748b",2,-3-lg*.4,3,3);R("#94a3b8",-7,-11,14,8);R("#cbd5e1",-7,-11,14,1.4);R("#facc15",-4,-8.4,3,3);R("#ef4444",1,-8.4,3,3);R("#cbd5e1",4,-16,7,6);R("#22d3ee",8,-14,3,2);R("#111",9.2,-13.6,.9,1.4);R("#475569",7,-19,.8,3);R("#ef4444",6.3,-20.2,2.2,2.2)}
 else if(id==="fenix"){const w=Math.sin(t*10)*1.6;R("#fbbf24",-2,-3+lg*.4,1.2,3);R("#fbbf24",2,-3-lg*.4,1.2,3);R("#f97316",-6,-10,12,7);R("#fde047",-12,-9+w*.4,6,2.4);R("#ef4444",-14,-11+w*.4,4,2);R("#fde047",-4.4,-7,8,3);R("#ef4444",-4,-13-w,6,4.4);R("#f97316",4,-14,6,6);R("#fde047",10,-12,3.4,2);R("#fde047",5.4,-17.6,1.4,3.6);R("#ef4444",7.4,-17.2,1.4,2.8);R("#111",7.8,-12.6,1.3,1.3)}
 else if(id==="nube"){const fl=-1.2+Math.sin(t*3)*.8;g.fillStyle="#e0f2fe";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,-5+fl,9,4.4,0,0,7);g.ellipse(-5,-6.4+fl,4.6,4,0,0,7);g.ellipse(5,-7+fl,4.8,4.2,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#bae6fd";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,-2+fl,8,1.6,0,0,7);g.fill();R("#111",1.4,-6.6+fl,1.2,1.8);R("#111",5,-6.6+fl,1.2,1.8);R("#fda4af",.4,-4.6+fl,1.6,1);R("#fda4af",5.8,-4.6+fl,1.6,1);R("#fde047",-2,-1+fl,1.6,2.4)}
 else if(id==="golem"){R("#78716c",-5,-3+lg*.4,3.6,3);R("#78716c",2,-3-lg*.4,3.6,3);R("#a8a29e",-7,-10,14,7);R("#78716c",-8.5,-11,4.6,3.4);R("#4ade80",-6,-10.8,7,1.4);R("#57534e",-2,-9,.8,4.4);R("#a8a29e",4,-14.4,6,6);R("#78716c",4,-14.4,6,1.2);R("#f97316",7,-12.4,1.6,1.6);R("#f97316",4.6,-12.4,1.4,1.4)}
 else if(id==="dragon"){R("#7c3aed",-5,-3+lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#7c3aed",3,-3-lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#8b5cf6",-6,-10,12,7);R("#fde68a",-4,-6.5,8,3);R("#8b5cf6",5,-14,7,6);R("#a78bfa",10,-12,3,3);R("#fde047",6,-16,1,2.4);R("#fde047",9,-16,1,2.4);R("#111",8,-12.6,1.2,1.2);R("#c4b5fd",-4,-15-Math.sin(t*9)*1.4,5,5.6);R("#8b5cf6",-10,-7,4,2);R("#f97316",-11.2,-7.4,1.8,2.6);if(Math.sin(t*8)>0)R("#fb923c",13,-11.4,2.2,2)}
 else if(id==="grifo"){const w=Math.sin(t*10)*2;R("#92400e",-4,-3+lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#92400e",2,-3-lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#d97706",-7,-10,14,7);R("#92400e",-11,-8,4,2.2);R("#fef3c7",-9,-17-w,7,6.5);R("#fde68a",-11,-15-w*.6,3,3);R("#f8fafc",4,-16,6,6);R("#facc15",9.6,-13.6,3.6,2.4);R("#facc15",9.6,-12,2,1.4);R("#111",7.6,-14.4,1.2,1.2);R("#fde68a",4,-17.6,3,1.8)}
 else if(id==="cerbero"){const fl=Math.sin(t*12)>0;R("#111827",-5,-3+lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#111827",3,-3-lg*.4,2.4,3);R("#1f2937",-7,-10,14,7);R("#374151",-7,-10,14,1.4);R("#f97316",-11,-9+(fl?-1:0),4,2.6);R("#fde047",-12,-9.6,2,1.6);R("#1f2937",4,-14,6,5.4);R("#1f2937",1,-17,5,4.6);R("#1f2937",7.6,-18.4,5,4.6);R("#4b5563",9,-12,3.4,2.4);R("#ef4444",7.4,-12.8,1.3,1.3);R("#ef4444",3,-15.6,1.2,1.2);R("#ef4444",10,-17,1.2,1.2);R("#fde047",11.2,-10,.8,1);R("#1f2937",1,-18.4,1.4,1.8);R("#1f2937",8,-19.8,1.4,1.8);R("#1f2937",5,-15.2,1.4,1.4)}
 else if(id==="behemot"){R("#312e81",-6.5,-4+lg*.4,4.2,4);R("#312e81",2.6,-4-lg*.4,4.2,4);R("#4338ca",-8.5,-11.6,17,8.6);R("#3730a3",-8.5,-11.6,17,1.6);R("#6366f1",-4,-13.4,3,2.4);R("#6366f1",1,-13.4,3,2.4);R("#4338ca",5,-16.4,8,8);R("#fef3c7",5,-20.6,1.6,4.6);R("#fef3c7",11.4,-20.6,1.6,4.6);R("#fef3c7",11.4,-10.4,1.4,3.2);R("#fef3c7",8,-10.4,1.4,3);R("#ef4444",9.4,-13.8,1.6,1.6);R("#3730a3",-11,-9,3.6,2)}
 else if(id==="unicornio"){const sw=Math.sin(t*6)*1.2;R("#bae6fd",-4.6,-3+lg*.4,2,3);R("#bae6fd",2.4,-3-lg*.4,2,3);R("#e0f2fe",-7,-10,13,7);R("#f0f9ff",-7,-10,13,1.5);R("#67e8f9",-10+sw*.3,-9,4,1.6);R("#f0abfc",-11+sw*.3,-7.6,4.4,1.6);R("#a78bfa",-10+sw*.3,-6.2,3.6,1.6);R("#e0f2fe",4,-15,6,6.4);R("#f0f9ff",8.6,-12.4,3.4,2.8);R("#f0abfc",1,-16,3.6,6);R("#67e8f9",-.6,-14,2.6,5);R("#fde047",6.6,-22,1.4,6.6);R("#fff",6.8,-22,.6,6.6);R("#7c3aed",7,-12.8,1.3,1.3)}
 else if(id==="tortuga"){R("#84cc16",-4,-3.4+lg*.3,3,3.4);R("#84cc16",2,-3.4-lg*.3,3,3.4);R("#84cc16",6,-9,4.4,4);R("#111",8.6,-8,1,1);R("#16a34a",-6.5,-12,12.5,8);R("#14532d",-6.5,-5,12.5,1.2);R("#15803d",-3,-11,2.4,2.4);R("#15803d",1,-11,2.4,2.4);R("#15803d",-1,-8,2.4,2.4);R("#65a30d",-9,-6,3,1.6)}
 g.restore()}
// ---- tienda: personajes y mascotas
function skinNav(){if(shopTab===1||shopTab>=8){button(250,20,150,36,"PERSONAJES",()=>{shopTab=8;shopPg=0},shopTab===8);button(410,20,140,36,"MASCOTAS",()=>{shopTab=9;shopPg=0},shopTab===9)}}
function noticeBox(){if(performance.now()<noticeUntil){g.fillStyle="#1e3a8aee";rrect(250,390,330,30,10);g.fill();txt(noticeTxt,415,410,13,"#fff","center")}}
function buyCh(c){if(chs.own[c.id]){chs.eq=chs.eq===c.id?"":c.id;sfx(660,.1,"square",.06)}else if(wallet>=c.c){wallet-=c.c;chs.own[c.id]=1;chs.eq=c.id;save();sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06);noticeTxt="¡"+c.n+" se unió a tu equipo!";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2500}else{sfx(150,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-50);noticeTxt="Te faltan "+(c.c-wallet)+" monedas";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2200}saveChs()}
function buyPet(c){if(chs.pown[c.id]){chs.peq=chs.peq===c.id?"":c.id;sfx(660,.1,"square",.06)}else if(wallet>=c.c){wallet-=c.c;chs.pown[c.id]=1;chs.peq=c.id;save();sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06);noticeTxt="¡"+c.n+" es tu nueva mascota!";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2500}else{sfx(150,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-50);noticeTxt="Te faltan "+(c.c-wallet)+" monedas";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2200}saveChs()}
function panel(){g.fillStyle="#0e0e2a";rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();const gd=g.createRadialGradient(114,250,10,114,250,130);gd.addColorStop(0,"#6d4aff55");gd.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=gd;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffffff22";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#0007";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(114,338,52,8,0,0,7);g.fill()}
let chPg=0;
function drawChars(mt){skinNav();let hov=null;const pos=i=>[228+(i%3)*180,104+Math.floor(i/3)*132],ncp=Math.ceil(CHARS.length/6);chPg=Math.min(chPg,ncp-1);const CV=CHARS.slice(chPg*6,chPg*6+6);
 CV.forEach((c,i)=>{const[x,y]=pos(i);if(ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+174&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+124)hov=c});
 panel();g.save();rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.clip();
 if(hov&&(chs.met[hov.id]))charArt(hov.id,114,338,2.5,mt);else{drawChar(104,338,2.55,mt,-.6+Math.sin(mt*2)*.1);if(chs.eq)charArt(chs.eq,170,330,1.3,mt)}g.restore();
 txt(hov&&chs.met[hov.id]?hov.n:"TU EQUIPO",114,128,13,"#ffd166","center");
 CV.forEach((c,i)=>{const[x,y]=pos(i),met=!!chs.met[c.id]||false,own=!!chs.own[c.id],eq=chs.eq===c.id;
  button(x,y,174,124,"",()=>{if(met)buyCh(c)},eq,!met);
  if(c.ex){g.strokeStyle="#ffd166";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(x+2,y+2,170,120,10);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#ffd166";rrect(x+100,y+5,68,15,7);g.fill();txt("EXCLUSIVO",x+134,y+16,9,"#3b2a00","center")}
  if(met){charArt(c.id,x+36,y+72,.95,mt);txt(c.n,x+68,y+34,14,"#fff");txt(c.t,x+68,y+48,8,"#94a3b8")}
  else{charArt(c.id,x+36,y+72,.95,mt,true);txt("???",x+68,y+34,14,"#94a3b8")}
  if(met){wrapT(c.ab,30).forEach((l,j)=>txt(l,x+8,y+92+j*11,9,"#cbd5e1"));txt(eq?"EQUIPADO ✓":own?"EQUIPAR":"● "+c.c,x+166,y+118,12,eq?"#7ae582":own?"#7dd3fc":wallet>=c.c?"#ffd166":"#ef4444","right")}
  else wrapT(c.hint,28).forEach((l,j)=>txt(l,x+8,y+94+j*12,9,"#94a3b8"))});
 if(ncp>1){button(240,360,44,24,"◀",()=>{chPg=(chPg+ncp-1)%ncp},false);txt((chPg+1)+"/"+ncp,306,377,12,"#fff","center");button(328,360,44,24,"▶",()=>{chPg=(chPg+1)%ncp},false)}
 button(600,388,178,36,"VOLVER",()=>S="menu");txt("Habla con ellos en los niveles para desbloquearlos",24,392,10,"#94a3b8");noticeBox()}
let petPg=0;
function drawPets(mt){skinNav();let hov=null;const pos=i=>[228+(i%4)*140,104+Math.floor(i/4)*132],np=Math.ceil(PETS.length/8);petPg=Math.min(petPg,np-1);const PV=PETS.slice(petPg*8,petPg*8+8);
 PV.forEach((c,i)=>{const[x,y]=pos(i);if(ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+134&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+124)hov=c});
 panel();g.save();rrect(14,104,206,266,16);g.clip();const pid=hov?hov.id:chs.peq,u=pid?psc(pid):1;
 if(pid){petArt(pid,120,338,u*1.9,mt,1,false);const U=3*u*1.9;sit=true;drawChar(120,338-10*U+47,2.1,mt);sit=false}else drawChar(120,338,2.55,mt,-.6+Math.sin(mt*2)*.1);g.restore();
 txt(hov?hov.n:chs.peq?"TU MONTURA":"SIN MONTURA",117,128,13,"#ffd166","center");
 PV.forEach((c,i)=>{const[x,y]=pos(i),own=!!chs.pown[c.id],eq=chs.peq===c.id;button(x,y,134,124,"",()=>buyPet(c),eq);
  petArt(c.id,x+67,y+52,1.2,mt,1,false);txt(c.n,x+67,y+68,12,"#fff","center");wrapT(c.ab,23).forEach((l,j)=>txt(l,x+67,y+81+j*10,8,"#cbd5e1","center"));
  txt(eq?"EQUIPADA ✓":own?"EQUIPAR":"● "+c.c,x+67,y+118,12,eq?"#7ae582":own?"#7dd3fc":wallet>=c.c?"#ffd166":"#ef4444","center")});
 if(np>1){button(240,360,44,24,"◀",()=>{petPg=(petPg+np-1)%np},false);txt((petPg+1)+"/"+np,306,377,12,"#fff","center");button(328,360,44,24,"▶",()=>{petPg=(petPg+1)%np},false)}
 button(600,388,178,36,"VOLVER",()=>S="menu");txt("Móntate en tu mascota · no se pierden",24,392,10,"#94a3b8");noticeBox()}

// ---- v27.7: 10 mundos nuevos + 10 jefes nuevos
const h_par=(cx,k,gap,fn)=>{const o=((cx*k)%gap+gap)%gap,b=Math.floor(cx*k/gap);for(let i=-1;i<=Math.ceil(W/gap)+1;i++)fn(i*gap-o,i+b)},
 h_sky2=(a,b)=>{const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);gr.addColorStop(0,a);gr.addColorStop(1,b);g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)},
 h_gear=(x,y,r,n,t,col,col2)=>{g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(t);g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<n*2;i++){const a=i*Math.PI/n,rr=i%2?r*.82:r;g.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*rr,Math.sin(a)*rr)}g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle=col2;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,r*.35,0,7);g.fill();g.restore()},
 h_puff=(x,y,r,col)=>{g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,r,0,7);g.arc(x+r*.9,y-r*.3,r*.8,0,7);g.arc(x-r*.9,y-r*.2,r*.7,0,7);g.fill()},
 h_stars2=(cx,t,n)=>{g.fillStyle="#fff";for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const x=((i*173-cx*.05)%W+W)%W,y=(i*97)%330;g.globalAlpha=.3+.6*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*2+i));g.fillRect(x,y,2,2)}g.globalAlpha=1};
const bgCandy=(cx,t)=>{h_sky2("#ffb3d9","#d9c2ff");
 h_par(cx,.15,260,(x,i)=>{const y=50+((i*37%80)+80)%80;h_puff(x,y,22,i%2?"#ffffffcc":"#bfe9ffcc")});
 h_par(cx,.55,200,(x,i)=>{g.fillStyle=["#ff8fc7","#9be7ff","#ffe28a"][((i%3)+3)%3];g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,12*T,90,60+(((i*13)%30)+30)%30,0,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ffffff66";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x-24,12*T-34,9,16,.3,0,7);g.fill()});
 h_par(cx,.35,170,(x,i)=>{const y=12*T-150-(((i*29)%50)+50)%50;g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(x-2,y,4,12*T-y);g.fillStyle=["#ff4d6d","#ffd166","#4cc9f0","#b5179e"][((i%4)+4)%4];g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,24,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();for(let a=0;a<11;a+=.25)g.lineTo(x+Math.cos(a)*a*2.1,y+Math.sin(a)*a*2.1);g.stroke()});
 for(let i=0;i<24;i++){const x=((i*97+t*18)%W+W)%W,y=(i*53+t*40)%H;g.fillStyle=["#ff4d6d","#ffd166","#4cc9f0","#fff"][i%4];g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(i+t);g.fillRect(-4,-1.5,8,3);g.restore()}};

 const px=W*.7-cx*.02;const pg=g.createRadialGradient(px-20,110,6,px,130,64);pg.addColorStop(0,"#fdba74");pg.addColorStop(1,"#9a3412");g.fillStyle=pg;g.beginPath();g.arc(px,130,62,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fde68a99";g.lineWidth=7;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(px,130,104,22,-.35,0,7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#c2410c88";g.fillRect(px-58,118,116,6);g.fillRect(px-52,142,104,5);
 h_par(cx,.18,420,(x,i)=>{g.fillStyle="#1e293b";g.fillRect(x,230,190,26);g.fillRect(x+70,190,36,40);g.fillRect(x-30,240,250,6);for(let q=0;q<9;q++){g.fillStyle=Math.sin(t*3+q+i)>0?"#fde047":"#475569";g.fillRect(x+10+q*19,236,8,6)}g.fillStyle="#94a3b8";g.fillRect(x+86,160,3,32);g.fillStyle="#f43f5e";g.fillRect(x+84,156,7,6)});
 const s=(t*260)%1600;g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(W-s+120,s*.25);g.lineTo(W-s,s*.25-30);g.stroke()};

 const ax=W-((t*70)%(W+240)),ay=90+Math.sin(t*2)*14;g.fillStyle="#1f2937";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(ax,ay);g.lineTo(ax-30,ay-14+Math.sin(t*9)*10);g.lineTo(ax-8,ay+4);g.lineTo(ax+30,ay-14+Math.sin(t*9)*10);g.closePath();g.fill()};
const bgToys=(cx,t)=>{h_sky2("#a5d8ff","#e0f2fe");
 h_par(cx,.15,300,(x,i)=>h_gear(x,120+((i*31)%60+60)%60,56,10,t*.4*(i%2?1:-1),"#fde68a99","#fbbf2499"));
 h_par(cx,.4,200,(x,i)=>{const n=2+(((i*7)%3)+3)%3,cs=["#ef4444","#3b82f6","#facc15","#22c55e","#a855f7"];for(let q=0;q<n;q++){const c=cs[(((i+q*2)%5)+5)%5];g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(x+q*2,12*T-(q+1)*40,40,40);g.strokeStyle="#0003";g.lineWidth=3;g.strokeRect(x+q*2,12*T-(q+1)*40,40,40);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.font="bold 24px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillText("ABC"[(((i+q)%3)+3)%3],x+q*2+20,12*T-(q+1)*40+28)}});
 for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const x=((i*131-cx*.2)%W+W)%W,y=H-((t*30+i*80)%H);g.fillStyle=["#f87171","#60a5fa","#fbbf24","#4ade80"][i%4];g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,y,13,16,0,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#0003";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y+16);g.lineTo(x,y+36);g.stroke()}};

 g.fillStyle="#0369a1";g.fillRect(0,250,W,12*T-250);for(let i=0;i<16;i++){const x=((i*60-cx*.3+t*20)%W+W)%W;g.strokeStyle="#bae6fd";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,262+(i%3)*26,12,Math.PI,0);g.stroke()}
 const sx=W+120-((t*24+cx*.08)%(W+420));g.fillStyle="#1c1917";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sx-60,248);g.lineTo(sx+70,248);g.lineTo(sx+48,274);g.lineTo(sx-44,274);g.fill();g.fillRect(sx-2,170,4,80);g.fillStyle="#f5f5f4";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sx+4,174);g.lineTo(sx+50,236);g.lineTo(sx+4,236);g.fill();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sx-6,184);g.lineTo(sx-44,236);g.lineTo(sx-6,236);g.fill();
 h_par(cx,.55,230,(x,i)=>{g.fillStyle="#78350f";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-5,12*T);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+12,12*T-60,x+4,12*T-100);g.lineTo(x+10,12*T-100);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+20,12*T-60,x+9,12*T);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#16a34a";for(let q=0;q<5;q++){const a=-2.9+q*.62;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+7,12*T-100);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+7+Math.cos(a)*30,12*T-122,x+7+Math.cos(a)*54,12*T-92+Math.sin(a)*12);g.lineTo(x+7,12*T-98);g.fill()}});
 for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const gx=((i*300-cx*.1+t*40)%(W+100)+W+100)%(W+100)-50,gy=60+i*30+Math.sin(t*3+i)*6;g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(gx-8,gy);g.quadraticCurveTo(gx-4,gy-6,gx,gy);g.quadraticCurveTo(gx+4,gy-6,gx+8,gy);g.stroke()}};

 h_stars2(cx,t,40)};

 else if(k===23){R(-34,-52,26,52,"#475569");R(8,-52,26,52,"#475569");R(-40,-10,34,10,"#1e293b");R(4,-10,34,10,"#1e293b");R(-46,-116,92,68,"#64748b");R(-46,-116,92,8,"#94a3b8");R(-46,-56,92,8,"#334155");E(0,-84,18,18,"#7f1d1d");E(0,-84,Math.abs(Math.sin(time*4))*6+9,Math.abs(Math.sin(time*4))*6+9,"#ef4444");R(-40,-146,80,30,"#475569");R(-6,-142,46,10,"#ef4444");R(-6,-142,46,3,"#fca5a5");Ln(0,-146,0,-168,"#94a3b8",3);E(0,-170,4,4,"#ef4444");R(40,-112,32,18,"#334155");R(60,-108,32+sw*.3,10,"#1e293b");E(94,-103,5,5,"#f97316");R(-70,-112,28,22,"#334155");Ln(-56,-90,-70,-60+sw,"#64748b",12);h_gear(-70,-56+sw,10,8,time*4,"#cbd5e1","#334155");for(let i=0;i<3;i++)R(-36+i*28,-100,3,40,"#33415599")}
