// ---- tubo nuevo (metal con anillos, remaches y boca oscura)
const pShade=(c,f)=>{c=String(c).replace("#","");if(c.length===3)c=c.split("").map(x=>x+x).join("");const n=[0,2,4].map(i=>Math.max(0,Math.min(255,Math.round(parseInt(c.slice(i,i+2),16)*f))));return"rgb("+n.join(",")+")"};
function drawPipeT(t,c,r,x,y){const left=G[r][c-1]!==t,u0=left?0:T,B=th.pipe,X0=x-u0,gr=g.createLinearGradient(X0,0,X0+2*T,0);
 gr.addColorStop(0,pShade(B,.5));gr.addColorStop(.18,pShade(B,.95));gr.addColorStop(.34,pShade(B,1.45));gr.addColorStop(.5,pShade(B,1.1));gr.addColorStop(.82,pShade(B,.7));gr.addColorStop(1,pShade(B,.42));
 if(t===5){const x0=left?x-5:x,w=T+5;
  g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(left?x:x,y+20,T,T-20);g.fillStyle="#0005";g.fillRect(x,y+20,T,3);
  g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(x0,y,w,22);g.fillStyle="#fff4";g.fillRect(x0,y,w,2);g.fillStyle="#0006";g.fillRect(x0,y+19,w,3);
  g.fillStyle=pShade(B,.12);g.fillRect(x0+(left?3:0),y+3,w-3,6);g.fillStyle=pShade(B,.3);g.fillRect(x0+(left?3:0),y+8,w-3,2);
  g.fillStyle=pShade(B,.6);g.beginPath();g.arc(left?x+9:x+T-9,y+15,2,0,7);g.fill()}
 else{g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(x,y,T,T);
  g.fillStyle=pShade(B,.75);g.fillRect(x,y+12,T,7);g.fillStyle="#fff3";g.fillRect(x,y+12,T,1.5);g.fillStyle="#0006";g.fillRect(x,y+18,T,2);
  g.fillStyle=pShade(B,1.5);g.beginPath();g.arc(left?x+7:x+T-7,y+4,2,0,7);g.arc(left?x+7:x+T-7,y+27,2,0,7);g.fill();
  g.fillStyle="#0004";g.fillRect(x,y,3,T)}}

// ---- STICKMAN LABERINTO: menú
const MZ=(()=>{let s=7;const R=()=>(s=(s*1103515245+12345)&0x7fffffff)/0x7fffffff,C=12,Rw=8,seen=[],rt=[],bt=[];for(let r=0;r<Rw;r++){seen.push(Array(C).fill(0));rt.push(Array(C).fill(1));bt.push(Array(C).fill(1))}
 const par={};const st=[[0,0]];seen[0][0]=1;while(st.length){const[x,y]=st[st.length-1],n=[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]].filter(d=>{const a=x+d[0],b=y+d[1];return a>=0&&b>=0&&a<C&&b<Rw&&!seen[b][a]});if(!n.length){st.pop();continue}const d=n[Math.floor(R()*n.length)],a=x+d[0],b=y+d[1];if(d[0]===1)rt[y][x]=0;else if(d[0]===-1)rt[y][a]=0;else if(d[1]===1)bt[y][x]=0;else bt[b][x]=0;seen[b][a]=1;par[a+","+b]=x+","+y;st.push([a,b])}
 const path=[];let k=(C-1)+","+(Rw-1);while(k){path.push(k.split(",").map(Number));k=par[k]}path.reverse();return{C,Rw,rt,bt,path}})();
let mzHelp=false;
function drawMaze(mt){
 g.fillStyle="#06121aee";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 for(let i=0;i<26;i++){g.fillStyle="rgba(110,231,183,"+(.1+.25*Math.abs(Math.sin(mt*1.1+i)))+")";g.fillRect((i*131)%W,(i*79+mt*6)%H,2,2)}
 txt("STICKMAN",24,72,44,"#6ee7b7");txt("LABERINTO",24,116,44,"#fde047");txt("Escapa de laberintos y trampas",26,142,12,"#94a3b8");
 const bx=24,by=164;
 button(bx,by,260,40,"▶ JUGAR",()=>{startMz3(true);sfx(660,.12,"square",.05)},true);
 button(bx,by+50,260,40,"NIVELES",()=>S="mzlv");
 button(bx,by+100,260,40,mzHelp?"OCULTAR AYUDA":"CÓMO JUGAR",()=>mzHelp=!mzHelp);
 button(bx,by+150,260,40,"VOLVER A JUEGOS",()=>S="games");
 const mx=330,my=70,cs=34,C=MZ.C,Rw=MZ.Rw;
 g.fillStyle="#0b1f2a";rrect(mx-14,my-14,C*cs+28,Rw*cs+28,14);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#6ee7b7";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();
 g.strokeStyle="#5eead4";g.lineWidth=3;g.lineCap="round";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(mx,my);g.lineTo(mx+C*cs,my);g.moveTo(mx,my);g.lineTo(mx,my+Rw*cs);
 for(let r=0;r<Rw;r++)for(let c=0;c<C;c++){if(MZ.rt[r][c]){g.moveTo(mx+(c+1)*cs,my+r*cs);g.lineTo(mx+(c+1)*cs,my+(r+1)*cs)}if(MZ.bt[r][c]){g.moveTo(mx+c*cs,my+(r+1)*cs);g.lineTo(mx+(c+1)*cs,my+(r+1)*cs)}}g.stroke();
 const P=MZ.path,t=(mt*5)%(P.length+6),i=Math.min(P.length-1,Math.floor(t)),f=Math.min(1,t-i),a=P[i],b=P[Math.min(P.length-1,i+1)];
 g.strokeStyle="#fde04766";g.lineWidth=5;g.beginPath();for(let q=0;q<=i;q++)g.lineTo(mx+(P[q][0]+.5)*cs,my+(P[q][1]+.5)*cs);g.stroke();
 const hx=mx+(a[0]+(b[0]-a[0])*f+.5)*cs,hy=my+(a[1]+(b[1]-a[1])*f+.5)*cs;
 g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.fillRect(mx+(C-1)*cs+8,my+(Rw-1)*cs+8,cs-16,cs-16);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy-6,4,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx,hy-2);g.lineTo(hx,hy+6);g.moveTo(hx-4,hy+12);g.lineTo(hx,hy+6);g.lineTo(hx+4,hy+12);g.stroke();
 if(mzHelp){g.fillStyle="#020a10f2";rrect(bx,by+196,262,80,10);g.fill();["WASD mover · Espacio saltar","mouse = cámara · E casillero","V cambia la vista (1ª/2ª/3ª)","las trampas. Cristales: -3 s."].forEach((l,j)=>txt(l,bx+10,by+216+j*18,11,"#cbd5e1"))}
 txt("v0.3 · 7 niveles · 7 monstruos",W-20,H-14,10,"#64748b","right");
 if(performance.now()<noticeUntil){g.fillStyle="#1e3a8aee";rrect(318,366,460,30,10);g.fill();txt(noticeTxt,548,386,13,"#fff","center")}}

// ---- STICKMAN LABERINTO 3D (raycaster propio, sin librerías)
const MZP=[{w:[104,118,160],w2:[70,84,126],fl:"#2b3050",ce:"#080a18",fog:[8,10,26],tc:"#ffb347"},
{w:[206,158,98],w2:[150,108,62],fl:"#5d4527",ce:"#170f06",fog:[28,16,8],tc:"#ff9f1c"},
{w:[92,150,104],w2:[56,100,70],fl:"#21402e",ce:"#06130c",fog:[6,20,12],tc:"#b6f36a"},
{w:[136,196,236],w2:[92,152,204],fl:"#294863",ce:"#07111d",fog:[8,18,32],tc:"#7dd3fc"},
{w:[150,50,52],w2:[104,30,34],fl:"#2a0e10",ce:"#0d0305",fog:[22,4,6],tc:"#ff3b30"},
{w:[150,160,120],w2:[110,124,96],fl:"#2f3a2a",ce:"#0a0f08",fog:[14,18,10],tc:"#d9f99d"},
{w:[120,60,110],w2:[80,36,84],fl:"#1c0f24",ce:"#050208",fog:[16,6,20],tc:"#e879f9"}];
const mz={ph:"cine",t:0,lv:0,m:null,N:0,W:0,Hh:0,px:0,py:0,pa:0,time:0,gems:[],traps:[],torch:[],ex:null,seen:null,flash:0,inv:0,got:0,ls:[.5,1.5],zb:new Float32Array(400),bob:0,last:performance.now()/1000,msg:"",msgT:0,best:0};
const mzHash=(a,b,c,d)=>(((a*37+b*101+c*13+d*29)%17)+17)%17/17;
function mzGen(lv){const N=Math.min(14,6+Math.round(lv*1.4)),Wd=2*N+3,Hd=2*N+1,m=[];for(let y=0;y<Hd;y++)m.push(Array(Wd).fill(1));
 const cell=(cx,cy)=>[2*cx+3,2*cy+1],vis=[];for(let y=0;y<N;y++)vis.push(Array(N).fill(0));
 const st=[[0,0]];vis[0][0]=1;{const[a,b]=cell(0,0);m[b][a]=0}
 while(st.length){const[x,y]=st[st.length-1],nb=[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]].filter(d=>{const a=x+d[0],b=y+d[1];return a>=0&&b>=0&&a<N&&b<N&&!vis[b][a]});
  if(!nb.length){st.pop();continue}const d=nb[Math.floor(Math.random()*nb.length)],a=x+d[0],b=y+d[1];vis[b][a]=1;const[c1,r1]=cell(x,y),[c2,r2]=cell(a,b);m[r2][c2]=0;m[(r1+r2)/2][(c1+c2)/2]=0;st.push([a,b])}
 m[1][0]=0;m[1][1]=0;m[1][2]=0;
 const open=(cx,cy)=>{const[a,b]=cell(cx,cy);return[m[b][a+1],m[b][a-1],m[b+1][a],m[b-1][a]].filter(v=>v===0).length};
 const dead=[],mid=[];for(let y=0;y<N;y++)for(let x=0;x<N;x++){if((x<2&&y<2)||(x===N-1&&y===N-1))continue;const o=open(x,y);if(o===1)dead.push([x,y]);else if(o===2)mid.push([x,y])}
 const pick=(arr,n)=>{const r=[];arr=arr.slice();while(r.length<n&&arr.length)r.push(arr.splice(Math.floor(Math.random()*arr.length),1)[0]);return r};
 mz.gems=pick(dead,Math.min(5,dead.length)).map((c,i)=>{const[a,b]=cell(c[0],c[1]);return{x:a+.5,y:b+.5,got:false,c:["#facc15","#f472b6","#38bdf8","#4ade80","#c084fc"][i%5]}});
 mz.traps=pick(mid.filter(c=>c[0]+c[1]>2),Math.min(3+lv*2,10)).map(c=>{const[a,b]=cell(c[0],c[1]);return{x:a+.5,y:b+.5,ph:Math.random()}});
 mz.torch=[{x:1.4,y:1.18},{x:1.4,y:1.82}].concat(pick(mid.concat(dead),Math.round(N*1.1)).map(c=>{const[a,b]=cell(c[0],c[1]);return{x:a+.5,y:b+.5}}));
 mz.lockers=pick(mid.concat(dead).filter(c=>c[0]+c[1]>1),Math.max(3,Math.round(N*.6))).map(c=>{const[a,b]=cell(c[0],c[1]);let ox=0,oy=0;for(const d of[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]])if(m[b+d[1]][a+d[0]]===1){ox=d[0]*.3;oy=d[1]*.3;break}return{x:a+.5+ox,y:b+.5+oy,cx:a+.5,cy:b+.5}});
 const[ea,eb]=cell(N-1,N-1);mz.ex={x:ea+.5,y:eb+.5};
 mz.m=m;mz.N=N;mz.W=Wd;mz.Hh=Hd;mz.seen=m.map(r=>r.map(()=>0));mz.got=0}
function startMz3(first){mz.lv=first?0:mz.lv;mzGen(mz.lv);mz.px=.5;mz.py=1.5;mz.pa=0;mz.time=0;mz.flash=0;mz.inv=0;mz.ls=[.5,1.5];mz.last=performance.now()/1000;mz.t=0;mz.ph=first?(mz.ok?"cine":"warn"):"walk";mz.pt=0;mz.mon=null;mz.hid=null;mz.z=0;mz.vz=0;mz.whT=0;mz.bk=0;mz.hb=0;mz.int=0;mz.flk=1;mz.dt=0;S="mz3"}
const mzW=(x,y)=>{const c=Math.floor(x),r=Math.floor(y);return r<0||r>=mz.Hh||c<0||c>=mz.W||mz.m[r][c]===1};
const mzHit=(x,y)=>{const r=.2;return mzW(x-r,y-r)||mzW(x+r,y-r)||mzW(x-r,y+r)||mzW(x+r,y+r)};
function mzMove(dx,dy){if(!mzHit(mz.px+dx,mz.py))mz.px+=dx;if(!mzHit(mz.px,mz.py+dy))mz.py+=dy}
const mzCol=(c,k,fg,f)=>"rgb("+((c[0]*k*f+fg[0]*(1-f))|0)+","+((c[1]*k*f+fg[1]*(1-f))|0)+","+((c[2]*k*f+fg[2]*(1-f))|0)+")";
function mzFlame(x,y,s,t,col){const f=1+.12*Math.sin(t*17+x),gl=g.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,s*2.4*f);gl.addColorStop(0,col+"aa");gl.addColorStop(1,col+"00");g.fillStyle=gl;g.fillRect(x-s*2.5,y-s*2.5,s*5,s*5);
 g.fillStyle="#ff7b00";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-s*.5,y);g.quadraticCurveTo(x-s*.6,y-s*.9*f,x+Math.sin(t*9+x)*s*.12,y-s*1.7*f);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+s*.6,y-s*.9*f,x+s*.5,y);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ffe08a";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-s*.25,y);g.quadraticCurveTo(x-s*.3,y-s*.5,x,y-s*.95*f);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+s*.3,y-s*.5,x+s*.25,y);g.fill()}
function mzSprite(sp,cx,yb,s,t){const k=sp.k;
 if(k==="torch"){g.fillStyle="#3f3f46";g.fillRect(cx-s*.05,yb-s*.5,s*.1,s*.5);g.fillStyle="#52525b";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx-s*.16,yb-s*.5);g.lineTo(cx+s*.16,yb-s*.5);g.lineTo(cx+s*.09,yb-s*.4);g.lineTo(cx-s*.09,yb-s*.4);g.fill();g.globalCompositeOperation="lighter";mzFlame(cx,yb-s*.52,s*.16,t,MZP[mz.lv%7].tc);g.globalCompositeOperation="source-over"}
 else if(k==="gem"){const fl=Math.sin(t*3+sp.x)*s*.04,y=yb-s*.38+fl;g.globalCompositeOperation="lighter";const gl=g.createRadialGradient(cx,y,0,cx,y,s*.5);gl.addColorStop(0,sp.c+"99");gl.addColorStop(1,sp.c+"00");g.fillStyle=gl;g.fillRect(cx-s*.5,y-s*.5,s,s);g.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";
  const w=s*.15*(.55+.45*Math.abs(Math.cos(t*2+sp.y)));g.fillStyle=sp.c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx,y-s*.17);g.lineTo(cx+w,y);g.lineTo(cx,y+s*.17);g.lineTo(cx-w,y);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ffffff88";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx,y-s*.17);g.lineTo(cx+w*.4,y-s*.02);g.lineTo(cx,y+s*.05);g.lineTo(cx-w*.5,y-s*.04);g.fill()}
 else if(k==="trap"){const up=sp.up;g.fillStyle="#1f2937";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cx,yb-s*.02,s*.38,s*.07,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle=up>.5?"#7f1d1d":"#374151";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cx,yb-s*.03,s*.33,s*.055,0,0,7);g.fill();
  for(let i=-2;i<=2;i++){const x=cx+i*s*.12,hh=s*.34*up*(1-Math.abs(i)*.12);if(hh<1)continue;g.fillStyle=up>.6?"#e5e7eb":"#9ca3af";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-s*.035,yb-s*.03);g.lineTo(x,yb-s*.03-hh);g.lineTo(x+s*.035,yb-s*.03);g.fill()}}
 else if(k==="locker"){const w=s*.36,h=s*.86,x=cx-w/2,y=yb-h,lg=g.createLinearGradient(x,0,x+w,0);lg.addColorStop(0,"#374151");lg.addColorStop(.5,"#6b7280");lg.addColorStop(1,"#1f2937");g.fillStyle=lg;g.fillRect(x,y,w,h);g.strokeStyle="#111827";g.lineWidth=Math.max(1,s*.015);g.strokeRect(x,y,w,h);g.fillStyle="#111827";for(let i=0;i<5;i++)g.fillRect(x+w*.18,y+h*.08+i*h*.028,w*.64,Math.max(1,h*.012));g.fillRect(cx-w*.02,y+h*.5,w*.07,h*.1);g.fillStyle="#7f1d1d88";g.fillRect(x+w*.62,y+h*.62,w*.1,h*.24);g.fillStyle="#d1d5db";g.fillRect(x+w*.3,y+h*.26,w*.4,h*.05)}
 else if(k==="mon"){mzMon(cx,yb,s,t)}
 else if(k==="me"){mzAvatar(cx,yb,s,t,sp.front)}
 else if(k==="exit"){const y=yb-s*.5,r=s*.42;g.globalCompositeOperation="lighter";const bm=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,yb);bm.addColorStop(0,"#22d3ee00");bm.addColorStop(1,"#22d3ee55");g.fillStyle=bm;g.fillRect(cx-s*.2,0,s*.4,yb);
  const gl=g.createRadialGradient(cx,y,0,cx,y,r*1.5);gl.addColorStop(0,"#ffffffcc");gl.addColorStop(.35,"#22d3ee88");gl.addColorStop(1,"#a855f700");g.fillStyle=gl;g.fillRect(cx-r*1.6,y-r*1.6,r*3.2,r*3.2);
  g.strokeStyle="#67e8f9";g.lineWidth=Math.min(3,Math.max(1.2,s*.012));for(let i=0;i<3;i++){g.beginPath();g.ellipse(cx,y,r*(.55+i*.22),r*(.55+i*.22)*(.35+.65*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*1.6+i))),t*(i%2?1:-1)*1.2,0,7);g.stroke()}g.globalCompositeOperation="source-over"}}
function mzRender(t){const cm=mzCam(),CX=cm[0],CY=cm[1],CA=cm[2],CZ=cm[3],P=MZP[mz.lv%7],fg=P.fog,NC=400,cw=W/NC,hor=H/2+Math.sin(mz.bob)*3,fgs="rgb("+fg+")";
 let gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,hor);gr.addColorStop(0,P.ce);gr.addColorStop(1,fgs);g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,hor+1);
 gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,hor,0,H);gr.addColorStop(0,fgs);gr.addColorStop(1,P.fl);g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,hor,W,H-hor);
 const dx=Math.cos(CA),dy=Math.sin(CA),plx=-dy*.66,ply=dx*.66,pr=CX*dx+CY*dy,fr=pr-Math.floor(pr);
 for(let k=1;k<11;k++){const d=k-fr;if(d<.3)continue;const y=hor+H*(.5+CZ)/d;g.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,"+(.34/(1+d*.25))+")";g.fillRect(0,y,W,Math.max(1,3/d))}
 const m=mz.m,fl=1+.05*Math.sin(t*13);
 for(let i=0;i<NC;i++){const cx=2*i/NC-1,rx=dx+plx*cx,ry=dy+ply*cx;let mx=Math.floor(CX),my=Math.floor(CY);const ddx=Math.abs(1/(rx||1e-9)),ddy=Math.abs(1/(ry||1e-9));let sx,sy,sdx,sdy;

function mzTeeth(A,C,B,dir,n,len){for(let i=1;i<n;i++){const t=i/n,u=1-t,x=u*u*A[0]+2*u*t*C[0]+t*t*B[0],y=u*u*A[1]+2*u*t*C[1]+t*t*B[1],w=(B[0]-A[0])/n*.42;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-w,y);g.lineTo(x,y+dir*len*(.7+.5*((i*7)%3)/2));g.lineTo(x+w,y);g.fill()}}
function mzFx(t){if(mz.int>0){const a=mz.int*(.3+.2*Math.sin(t*7)),rv=g.createRadialGradient(W/2,H/2,H*.25,W/2,H/2,H*.95);rv.addColorStop(0,"rgba(120,0,0,0)");rv.addColorStop(1,"rgba(130,0,0,"+a+")");g.fillStyle=rv;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.1)";for(let i=0;i<mz.int*90;i++)g.fillRect(Math.random()*W,Math.random()*H,2,1)}}
function mzBfs(sx,sy,tx,ty){const m=mz.m,Wd=mz.W,Hd=mz.Hh,par=new Int32Array(Wd*Hd).fill(-2),q=[sy*Wd+sx];par[q[0]]=-1;let h=0,last=q[0];
 while(h<q.length){const c=q[h++],x=c%Wd,y=(c/Wd)|0;last=c;if(x===tx&&y===ty)break;for(const d of[[1,0],[-1,0],[0,1],[0,-1]]){const nx=x+d[0],ny=y+d[1];if(nx<0||ny<0||nx>=Wd||ny>=Hd||m[ny][nx]===1)continue;const k=ny*Wd+nx;if(par[k]!==-2)continue;par[k]=c;q.push(k)}}
 if(tx<0)return[[last%Wd,(last/Wd)|0]];const out=[];let c=ty*Wd+tx;if(par[c]===-2)return out;while(par[c]>=0){out.push([c%Wd,(c/Wd)|0]);c=par[c]}return out.reverse()}
function mzMonUpd(dt){const mo=mz.mon,lvs=Math.min(2.05,(1.5+mz.lv*.08)*[1,.92,1.04,1,.95,1.12,1.1][mz.lv%7]);
 if(!mo.path.length){const cx=Math.floor(mo.x),cy=Math.floor(mo.y);let tx,ty;
  if(mo.st==="chase"){tx=Math.floor(mz.px);ty=Math.floor(mz.py)}else if(mo.st==="check"){tx=mo.tx;ty=mo.ty}
  else{if(!mo.wt)for(let k=0;k<30;k++){const rx=Math.floor(Math.random()*mz.W),ry=Math.floor(Math.random()*mz.Hh);if(mz.m[ry][rx]===0&&Math.abs(rx-cx)+Math.abs(ry-cy)>6){mo.wt=[rx,ry];break}}tx=mo.wt?mo.wt[0]:cx;ty=mo.wt?mo.wt[1]:cy}
  mo.path=mzBfs(cx,cy,tx,ty);
  if(!mo.path.length){if(mo.st==="check"){mo.ck+=dt;if(mo.ck>2.6){mo.ck=0;if(mz.hid&&Math.random()<.55)mzCatch();else{mo.st="wander";mo.wt=null}}}else if(mo.st==="wander")mo.wt=null}}
 if(mo.path.length){const[wx,wy]=mo.path[0],tx=wx+.5,ty=wy+.5,dx=tx-mo.x,dy=ty-mo.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy),sp=lvs*dt;if(d<=sp){mo.x=tx;mo.y=ty;mo.path.shift();if(mo.st==="chase")mo.path=[]}else{mo.x+=dx/d*sp;mo.y+=dy/d*sp}}}
function mzHorror(dt){if(!mz.mon&&mz.pt>=10){const f=mzBfs(Math.floor(mz.px),Math.floor(mz.py),-1,-1)[0];mz.mon={x:f[0]+.5,y:f[1]+.5,path:[],st:mz.hid?"wander":"chase",ck:0,wt:null,tx:0,ty:0,on:true};mz.msg="Algo despertó... ¡CORRE!";mz.msgT=3.2;mz.flash=.5;sfx(70,1.2,"sawtooth",.14,-30);sfx(900,.8,"sawtooth",.05,-700)}

 if(mz.int>.1&&Math.random()<mz.int*.12)mz.flk=.2+Math.random()*.3;

 if(!mz.hid&&d<.62)mzCatch()}
function mzNoise(d,v,f0){try{if(CFG&&CFG.sfx===0)return;AC=AC||new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();const n=Math.floor(AC.sampleRate*d),bf=AC.createBuffer(1,n,AC.sampleRate),a=bf.getChannelData(0);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)a[i]=(Math.random()*2-1)*Math.pow(1-i/n,.6);const sr=AC.createBufferSource();sr.buffer=bf;const gn=AC.createGain();gn.gain.value=v;const fl=AC.createBiquadFilter();fl.type="bandpass";fl.frequency.value=f0||1800;sr.connect(fl);fl.connect(gn);gn.connect(AC.destination);sr.start()}catch(e){}}
function mzCatch(){if(mz.ph!=="play")return;const mo=mz.mon;if(mo)mz.pa=Math.atan2(mo.y-mz.py,mo.x-mz.px);mz.ph="dead";mz.dt=0;mz.sf=0;mz.hid=null}

 if(s%64===0){sfxM(base,sec*60,"sawtooth",.03);sfxM(base*1.4142,sec*60,"sawtooth",.018);sfxM(base*2.03,sec*60,"sine",.02);sfxM(base*.5,sec*60,"sine",.03)}

 else{g.moveTo(0,hy);g.lineTo(lx,-12*s);g.lineTo(lx*1.3,0);g.moveTo(0,hy);g.lineTo(-lx,-12*s);g.lineTo(-lx*1.3,0);g.moveTo(2*s,sy);g.lineTo(2*s-lx*.9,-27*s);g.lineTo(2*s-lx*1.2,-18*s);g.moveTo(2*s,sy);g.lineTo(2*s+lx*.9,-27*s);g.lineTo(2*s+lx*1.2,-18*s)}

function mzFoDraw(dt,mt){
 if(mz.ph==="portal"){mzPortal(dt);return}
 if(mz.ph==="home"){mzHome(dt);return}
 const f=mz.fo,GY=FO_GY;f.t+=dt;const t=f.t;
 if(!f.dead){
  let want=210;if(mzK.d||mzK.arrowright)want=295;if(mzK.a||mzK.arrowleft)want=125;if(f.stun>0){want=55;f.stun-=dt}if(t<1.3)want=0;
  f.sp+=(want-f.sp)*Math.min(1,dt*6);f.x+=f.sp*dt;
  if((mzK[" "]||mzK.arrowup||mzK.w)&&f.y<=0&&f.vy<=0&&t>1.3){f.vy=560;sfx(330,.08,"sine",.05,200)}
  if(f.y>0||f.vy>0){f.vy-=1500*dt;f.y+=f.vy*dt;if(f.y<=0){f.y=0;f.vy=0}}
  f.ob.forEach(o=>{if(!o.hit&&f.x+9>o.x&&f.x-9<o.x+o.w&&f.y<o.h-4){o.hit=1;f.stun=.8;f.sp=40;f.sh=.3;sfx(120,.2,"sawtooth",.08,-60);mzNoise(.15,.1,300)}});
  const ease=Math.min(.18,f.fails*.06);f.mons.forEach(m=>{if(t>2.2)m.x+=(176+Math.min(t,30)*1.3+m.i*1.5)*(1-ease)*dt});
  const gap=f.x-f.mons[0].x;f.hb-=dt;if(f.hb<=0&&t>1){f.hb=Math.max(.3,gap/420);sfx(52,.12,"sine",.14,-15)}
  if(gap<36&&t>2.2){f.dead=.001;f.by=Math.floor(Math.random()*7);sfx(70,1,"sawtooth",.2,-30);sfx(1500,.7,"square",.1,-1300);mzNoise(.5,.2,400)}
  if(f.x>=FO_LEN){mz.ph="portal";f.pt=0;f.pk=0;sfx(200,1.8,"sine",.1,900);return}}
 else f.dead+=dt;
 const cx=f.x-250,gap=f.x-f.mons[0].x,prox=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,1-(gap-40)/300));f.sh=Math.max(0,f.sh-dt);
 g.save();if(f.sh>0||prox>.5)g.translate((Math.random()-.5)*(f.sh*14+prox*4),(Math.random()-.5)*(f.sh*14+prox*4));
 foScene(f,t,cx);
 const old=mz.lv;f.mons.forEach(m=>{const x=m.x-cx;if(x<-120||x>W+80)return;mz.lv=m.i;g.save();g.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,t/1.4);mzMon(x,GY+4,82+(m.i%3)*6,t+m.i);g.restore()});mz.lv=old;
 if(f.dead<=0||f.dead>0){const st=f.stun>0;if(!(f.dead>.15&&f.dead<3))foStick(250,GY-f.y,1.15,t*(f.sp/32),f.y>0,1)}
 g.restore();
 const vg=g.createRadialGradient(W/2,H/2,H*.3,W/2,H/2,H*.85);vg.addColorStop(0,"rgba(0,0,0,0)");vg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(40,0,10,"+(.5+prox*.4)+")");g.fillStyle=vg;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 if(t<2.4){g.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,"+Math.max(0,.9-t*.5)+")";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);mzCap("Todos despertaron...",H*.4,28,"#fca5a5",mzFade(t,.2,2.3))}
 if(t>2.3&&t<4.6)mzCap("¡ESCAPA POR EL BOSQUE!",H*.3,30,"#ef4444",mzFade(t,2.3,4.6));
 if(t>4&&t<10)mzCap("ESPACIO = saltar obstáculos  ·  D = correr  ·  A = frenar",H-16,13,"#e2e8f0",mzFade(t,4,10));
 const bx=210,bw=380,pr=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,f.x/FO_LEN));g.fillStyle="#000a";rrect(bx-6,H-44,bw+12,22,8);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#312e81";g.fillRect(bx,H-37,bw,8);g.fillStyle="#a78bfa";g.fillRect(bx,H-37,bw*pr,8);g.fillStyle="#e0f2fe";g.beginPath();g.arc(bx+bw+2,H-33,7,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#f1f5f9";g.beginPath();g.arc(bx+bw*pr,H-33,5,0,7);g.fill();

 if(t<1.6){g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+Math.max(0,1-t/1.6)+")";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)}
 if(t>18.4){const a=Math.min(1,(t-18.4)/1.4);g.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,"+a+")";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)}
 if(t>19.6){g.globalAlpha=Math.min(1,(t-19.6)/1.2);txt("Todo era un sueño.",W/2,H/2-4,36,"#fde68a","center");g.globalAlpha=1}
 if(t>23){g.fillStyle="#000";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);const a=Math.min(1,(t-23)/1.2);g.globalAlpha=a;txt("FIN",W/2,H/2-14,56,"#e2e8f0","center");txt("Gracias por jugar Stickman Laberinto",W/2,H/2+22,14,"#94a3b8","center");g.globalAlpha=1;if(t>24)button(300,H/2+54,200,38,"MENÚ",()=>{mz.es={};mz.ph="warn";S="maze"},true)}
 else{g.fillStyle="#000";g.fillRect(0,0,W,38);g.fillRect(0,H-38,W,38);button(W-150,H-34,130,28,"SALTAR ▶",()=>{mz.ht=23.5})}}

// ---- actualización
function update(dt){chTick();
 if(S!==prevS){prevS=S;endT=0;if(S==="win"||S==="over")set3D(S==="win")}if(S==="win"||S==="over")endT+=dt;if(S==="win")wonT+=dt;if(S!=="play")return;
 time+=dt;chUpd(dt);shake=Math.max(0,shake-dt);if(ups.rg&&p.hp>0&&p.hp<HPM()){p.rgt+=dt;if(p.rgt>=(ups.rg>1?22:38)){p.rgt=0;p.hp++;pop(p.x,p.y-30,"+♥");sfx(880,.15,"triangle",.05)}}const D=DIFS[dif],DM=D.sp*(1+((lvl===99?0:Math.min(lvl,8))+(dif===3?Math.min(lap,10)*.6:0))*.08)*(1+.1*HD()),RX=RUN*(p.spd>0?1.35:1)*(1+.12*ups.spd)*(1+spdBonus());p.sh=Math.max(0,p.sh-dt);p.dj=Math.max(0,p.dj-dt);p.bg=Math.max(0,p.bg-dt);p.spd=Math.max(0,p.spd-dt);p.star=Math.max(0,(p.star||0)-dt);p.frz=Math.max(0,(p.frz||0)-dt);p.mag=Math.max(0,(p.mag||0)-dt);
 const ax=(inp.r?1:0)-(inp.l?1:0),SX=p.x+p.w/2,SY=p.y+p.h-38,aimOn=performance.now()-ms.t<(ms.pt==="mouse"?4000:500);
 if(ax){p.vx+=ax*2300*dt;p.f=ax}else p.vx-=Math.sign(p.vx)*Math.min(Math.abs(p.vx),1900*dt);
 if(!ax&&aimOn&&ms.pt==="mouse"&&Math.abs(ms.x+cam-SX)>10)p.f=ms.x+cam>=SX?1:-1;
 p.vx=clamp(p.vx,-RX,RX);
 if(p.ground)p.coy=.1;else p.coy-=dt;
 if(inp.jp){p.buf=.12;inp.jp=false}else p.buf-=dt;
 if(p.ground)p.air=0;if(p.buf>0&&p.coy>0){p.vy=-700*(1+.08*ups.jh+jumpBonus());p.coy=0;p.buf=0;sfx(320,.18,"square",.06,380)}else if(p.buf>0&&(p.dj>0||ups.dj>0||chAb("janja"))&&!p.ground&&p.air<1&&p.coy<=0){p.vy=-640;p.air=1;p.buf=0;sfx(520,.18,"square",.06,400);for(let i=-1;i<=1;i+=2)parts.push({x:p.x+p.w/2,y:p.y+p.h,vx:i*80,vy:30,l:.3,c:"#7ae582"})}
 if(!inp.j&&p.vy<-240)p.vy=-240;
 const aim=aimOn?Math.atan2(ms.y-SY,ms.x+cam-SX):(p.f>0?-.3:Math.PI+.3);
 p.atk=Math.max(0,p.atk-dt);
 if((inp.at||inp.mb||inp.ck)&&p.atk<=0){p.atk=.34;p.th=aim;sfx(900,.12,"sawtooth",.04,-600)}inp.ck=false;
 if(p.atk<=0){const da=((aim-p.as+Math.PI*3)%(2*Math.PI))-Math.PI;p.as+=da*.35}
 p.inv=Math.max(0,p.inv-dt);const wg=p.ground;move(p,dt);
 if(p.ground)p.ph+=Math.abs(p.vx)*dt*.075;
 if(!wg&&p.ground){p.sq=.22;for(let i=-1;i<=1;i+=2)parts.push({x:p.x+p.w/2,y:p.y+p.h-3,vx:i*70,vy:-50,l:.3,c:"#e8e8e8"})}
 p.sq=Math.max(0,p.sq-dt*1.8);
 if(p.ground&&Math.abs(p.vx)>140){p.dt-=dt;if(p.dt<=0){p.dt=.11;parts.push({x:p.x+p.w/2-p.f*10,y:p.y+p.h-4,vx:-p.f*30,vy:-45,l:.3,c:"#e8e8e8"})}}
 if(p.bonk)bump(p.bonk[0],p.bonk[1]);
 if(p.y>H+60){p.hp=0;die();return}
 cps.forEach(c=>{if(p.x>c-40&&cp<c)cp=c});if(dif===3&&lvl!==99)endUpd();if(lvl===99&&p.x>90*T){S="win";wonT=0;tutDone=true;try{localStorage.setItem("stk_t","1")}catch(e){}return}
 if(lvl!==99&&dif!==3&&!boss.active&&!boss.dead&&p.x>(AR+3)*T){boss.active=true;for(let r=3;r<12;r++)G[r][AR]=2;gateOpen=false;shake=.3;sfx(120,.5,"sawtooth",.08,-60)}
 cam=dgOn()?0:clamp(p.x-300,0,COLS*T-W);
 const RE=(p.bg>0?112:68)*(1+.15*ups.sw+swBonus()),swing=p.atk>.12,psi=swingA(p.th,1-p.atk/.34),hit=r=>{if(!swing)return false;for(let d=8;d<=RE;d+=8){const x=SX+Math.cos(psi)*d,y=SY+Math.sin(psi)*d;if(x>r.x-5&&x<r.x+r.w+5&&y>r.y-5&&y<r.y+r.h+5)return true}return false};
 dgUpd(dt,hit);if(magOn())magUpd(dt);coins.forEach(c=>{if(!c.got&&Math.abs(c.x-(p.x+p.w/2))<22*(ups.mg?3:1)*(magOn()?3:1)&&Math.abs(c.y-(p.y+p.h/2))<34*(ups.mg?2:1)*(magOn()?3:1)){c.got=true;gc();add(100);sfx(988,.07,"square",.05);sfx(1319,.15,"square",.05);if(coinsN%100===0){lives++;pop(p.x,p.y-20,"¡VIDA +1!")}}});
 items.forEach(it=>{if(it.gone)return;move(it,dt);if(it.y>H+60)it.gone=true;if(ov(p,it)){it.gone=true;add(300,it.x,it.y);sfx(880,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1175,.2,"square",.06);burst(it.x+11,it.y+11,ITC[it.k],10);pop(it.x,it.y-18,ITN[it.k]);
  if(it.k===0)p.hp=Math.min(HPM(),p.hp+1);else{const PW=1+.5*ups.pw;if(it.k===1)p.sh=8*PW;else if(it.k===2)p.dj=20*PW;else if(it.k===3)p.bg=12*PW;else if(it.k===4)p.spd=10*PW;else powerUp(it.k,PW)}}});items=items.filter(i=>!i.gone);
 // bichos
 {const dtO=dt;{const dt=p.frz>0?dtO*.2:dtO;enemies.forEach(e=>{if(e.gone)return;if(e.dead>0){e.dead-=dt;if(e.dead<=0)e.gone=true;return}
  if(Math.abs(e.x-p.x)>620)return;
  if(e.t===2||e.t===7){e.x+=e.vx*DM*dt;e.y=e.y0+Math.sin(time*3+e.ph)*36;if(e.x<cam-80)e.gone=true}
  else if(e.t===4){e.y=e.y0+(.5+.5*Math.sin(time*1.6+e.ph))*245}
  else if(e.t===5||e.t===11){e.sh-=dt;if(e.sh<=0&&Math.abs(e.x-p.x)<420){e.sh=(e.t===11?1.6:2.4)/DM;const dr=p.x<e.x?-1:1;balls.push({x:e.x+15+dr*14,y:e.y+12,vx:dr*190,vy:0,w:14,h:8,k:"b",c:"#fb923c",l:0});sfx(240,.12,"square",.05,-100)}}
  else if(e.t===6){const q=((time+e.ph)%3.6)/3.6;e.f=q<.4?0:q<.5?(q-.4)/.1:q<.85?1:(1-q)/.15;e.y=12*T-e.f*24;e.h=24}
  else{if(e.t===1||e.t===9){e.hj-=dt;if(e.ground&&e.hj<=0){e.vy=e.t===9?-540:-440;e.hj=1+Math.random()*.8}e.vx=e.ground?0:e.d*75*DM}else e.vx=e.d*(e.t===3?62:e.t===8?92:e.t===10?54:42)*DM;
   move(e,dt);if(e.hx)e.d*=-1;if(e.y>H+80){e.gone=true;return}}
  if(e.t===6&&e.f<.5)return;if(hit(e)){e.dead=.5;add(e.t>=3?200:100,e.x,e.y);burst(e.x+e.w/2,e.y+e.h/2,ECOL[e.t],8);sfx(260,.12,"square",.06,-100);return}
  if(ov(p,e)){if(e.t!==3&&e.t!==5&&e.t!==10&&e.t!==11&&p.vy>80&&p.y+p.h-e.y<24){e.dead=.5;p.vy=(inp.j?-600:-400)*(1+.12*ups.pb);add(100,e.x,e.y);sfx(220,.12,"square",.07,-120)}else if(p.star>0){e.dead=.5;add(200,e.x,e.y);burst(e.x+e.w/2,e.y+e.h/2,ECOL[e.t],8)}else hurt()}});}}
 if(dif===3&&lvl!==99)minisUpd(dt,hit,DM);
 // ojos vigía
 plants.forEach(pl=>{if(pl.dead)return;const b=plantBox(pl);if(b.h<4)return;if(hit(b)){pl.dead=true;add(200,pl.x,pl.top-30);burst(pl.x,pl.top-20,th.st,12);sfx(200,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-100)}else if(ov(p,b))hurt()});
 // jefe

     if(r<.3){const a=Math.atan2(p.y+p.h/2-(b.y+40),p.x-b.x),n=fury?3:1;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const an=a+(i-(n-1)/2)*.3;balls.push({x:b.x+b.w/2,y:b.y+40,vx:Math.cos(an)*215,vy:Math.sin(an)*215,l:0})}sfx(300,.3,"sawtooth",.06,-150)}

 g.fillStyle="#67e8f9";g.shadowColor="#22d3ee";g.shadowBlur=16;g.beginPath();g.arc(44,-136+fl,9*(1+Math.sin(time*6)*.15),0,7);g.fill();g.shadowBlur=0;
 g.fillStyle="#fff";g.beginPath();g.arc(36,-62+fl,8,0,7);g.fill();g.beginPath();g.arc(-34,-70+fl+Math.sin(time*4)*4,8,0,7);g.fill();g.restore()}

const COS={
 skin:[{id:"cl",n:"Clásico",c:0,col:"#111",hd:"#fff"},{id:"fu",n:"Rojo Fuego",c:60,col:"#b91c1c",hd:"#fee2e2"},{id:"hi",n:"Azul Hielo",c:60,col:"#0369a1",hd:"#e0f2fe"},{id:"ne",n:"Verde Neón",c:90,col:"#16a34a",hd:"#dcfce7",glow:"#4ade80"},{id:"vi",n:"Violeta Sombra",c:90,col:"#6d28d9",hd:"#ede9fe"},{id:"fa",n:"Fantasma",c:160,col:"#e5e7eb",hd:"#fff",alpha:.7},{id:"or",n:"Oro Real",c:220,col:"#b8860b",hd:"#fff7cc",glow:"#ffd700"},{id:"ar",n:"Arcoíris",c:300,col:"rainbow",hd:"#fff"}],
 hat:[{id:"no",n:"Sin sombrero",c:0},{id:"gl",n:"Gorro de lana",c:50},{id:"ge",n:"Orejas de gato",c:90},{id:"cc",n:"Casco de caballero",c:140},{id:"mg",n:"Sombrero de mago",c:130},{id:"vk",n:"Casco vikingo",c:170},{id:"ha",n:"Halo angelical",c:200},{id:"co",n:"Corona dorada",c:320}],
 body:[{id:"no",n:"Sin ropa",c:0},{id:"rj",n:"Camiseta roja",c:40},{id:"ch",n:"Chaleco explorador",c:70},{id:"ni",n:"Traje ninja",c:150},{id:"tm",n:"Túnica mágica",c:180},{id:"kn",n:"Armadura caballero",c:260},{id:"es",n:"Traje espacial",c:400},{id:"dr",n:"Armadura de dragón",c:520},{id:"kd",n:"Armadura dorada",c:650}],
 back:[{id:"no",n:"Sin espalda",c:0},{id:"mo",n:"Mochila aventurera",c:80},{id:"cr",n:"Capa roja",c:120},{id:"cm",n:"Capa real morada",c:200},{id:"aa",n:"Alas de ángel",c:350},{id:"ad",n:"Alas de demonio",c:350},{id:"ts",n:"Caparazón de tortuga",c:160},{id:"es",n:"Escudo a la espalda",c:140},{id:"gu",n:"Guitarra rockera",c:130},{id:"jp",n:"Mochila cohete",c:250},{id:"bt",n:"Alas de mariposa",c:300},{id:"cd",n:"Capa dorada",c:380},{id:"cx",n:"Capa arcoíris",c:450}],
 face:[{id:"no",n:"Sin accesorio",c:0},{id:"ga",n:"Gafas de sol",c:60},{id:"an",n:"Antifaz de héroe",c:80},{id:"bi",n:"Bigote",c:50},{id:"mn",n:"Monóculo",c:110},{id:"pi",n:"Parche pirata",c:70},{id:"ba",n:"Barba",c:60},{id:"pa",n:"Nariz de payaso",c:50},{id:"ch",n:"Ojos de corazón",c:90},{id:"en",n:"Cara enojada",c:60},{id:"fe",n:"Cara feliz",c:40},{id:"ll",n:"Cara llorando",c:60},{id:"ci",n:"Cicatriz",c:80},{id:"m3",n:"Gafas 3D",c:100},{id:"gt",n:"Bigotes de gato",c:70},{id:"sx",n:"Sonrisa grande",c:40},{id:"ko",n:"Ojos felices",c:40},{id:"bl",n:"Sonrojado",c:50},{id:"gu",n:"Guiño",c:40},{id:"ln",n:"Lengua afuera",c:50},{id:"ri",n:"Risa a lágrimas",c:70},{id:"ec",n:"Ojos de estrella",c:80},{id:"tr",n:"Cara troll",c:80},{id:"ce",n:"Cejas arriba",c:60},{id:"po",n:"Cara de póker",c:50},{id:"dw",n:"Lentes Deal With It",c:120},{id:"su",n:"Cara de susto",c:60},{id:"lo",n:"Cara loca",c:70},{id:"mi",n:"Mimir",c:60},{id:"cg",n:"Cringe",c:70},{id:"ca",n:"Calavera",c:100}],
 sword:[{id:"cl",n:"Espada clásica",c:0},{id:"hi",n:"Espada de hielo",c:70},{id:"ka",n:"Katana ninja",c:100},{id:"fi",n:"Espada de fuego",c:110},{id:"mg",n:"Espada mística",c:140},{id:"lv",n:"Sable láser verde",c:150},{id:"lr",n:"Sable láser rojo",c:150},{id:"or",n:"Espada dorada",c:220},{id:"dr",n:"Espada de dragón",c:320},{id:"ar",n:"Espada arcoíris",c:450}]};
let cos={own:{},eq:{skin:"cl",hat:"no",body:"no",back:"no",face:"no",sword:"cl"}},tryOn=null,shopTab=0,lvPg=0;
try{const o=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stk_cos")||"null");if(o){cos.own=o.own||{};Object.assign(cos.eq,o.eq||{})}}catch(e){}
const saveCos=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem("stk_cos",JSON.stringify(cos))}catch(e){}};
const eqId=s=>{if(tryOn){if(tryOn.slot===s)return tryOn.id;if(tryOn.only)return s==="skin"?"cl":"no"}return cos.eq[s]};
const cItem=(s,id)=>COS[s].find(x=>x.id===id)||COS[s][0];
const isOwned=(s,id)=>cItem(s,id).c===0||!!cos.own[s+":"+id];
function skinNow(){const s=cItem("skin",eqId("skin"));return{col:s.col==="rainbow"?"hsl("+((time*140)%360|0)+",90%,50%)":s.col,hd:s.hd,glow:s.glow,alpha:s.alpha||1}}
const COSF={
 back(id,hip,sh,hd,k){if(!id||id==="no")return;g.save();const t=time,w=Math.sin(t*8)*2*(1+k);
  if(id==="cr"||id==="cm"||id==="cd"||id==="cx"){if(id==="cx"){const gr=g.createLinearGradient(sh[0]-24,sh[1],sh[0],hip[1]+20);["#ef4444","#f59e0b","#22c55e","#3b82f6","#a855f7"].forEach((c,i)=>gr.addColorStop(i/4,c));g.fillStyle=gr}else g.fillStyle=({cr:"#dc2626",cm:"#7c3aed",cd:"#f59e0b"})[id];g.strokeStyle=({cr:"#7f1d1d",cm:"#fbbf24",cd:"#78350f",cx:"#fff"})[id];g.lineWidth=1.6;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0],sh[1]-1);g.quadraticCurveTo(sh[0]-9-k*9,sh[1]+8+w*.4,sh[0]-13-k*16,hip[1]+17+w);g.lineTo(hip[0]-3,hip[1]+10);g.lineTo(sh[0]+2,sh[1]+3);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()}
  else if(id==="mo"){g.fillStyle="#92400e";g.strokeStyle="#451a03";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.rect(sh[0]-13,sh[1]+1,10,17);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#b45309";g.fillRect(sh[0]-12,sh[1]+3,8,5)}
  else if(id==="ts"){g.fillStyle="#16a34a";g.strokeStyle="#14532d";g.lineWidth=1.6;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(sh[0]-8,sh[1]+10,8,11,0,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]-14,sh[1]+6);g.lineTo(sh[0]-2,sh[1]+6);g.moveTo(sh[0]-14,sh[1]+14);g.lineTo(sh[0]-2,sh[1]+14);g.moveTo(sh[0]-8,sh[1]);g.lineTo(sh[0]-8,sh[1]+21);g.stroke()}
  else if(id==="es"){g.fillStyle="#94a3b8";g.strokeStyle="#334155";g.lineWidth=1.8;g.beginPath();g.arc(sh[0]-8,sh[1]+10,10,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#dc2626";g.beginPath();g.arc(sh[0]-8,sh[1]+10,4,0,7);g.fill()}
  else if(id==="gu"){g.save();g.translate(sh[0]-7,sh[1]+11);g.rotate(-.7);g.strokeStyle="#451a03";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-4);g.lineTo(0,-22);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#b45309";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,5,7,9,0,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,5,2.5,0,7);g.fill();g.restore()}
  else if(id==="jp"){g.fillStyle="#cbd5e1";g.strokeStyle="#334155";g.lineWidth=1.4;[-14,-8].forEach(o=>{g.beginPath();g.rect(sh[0]+o,sh[1]+1,6,17);g.fill();g.stroke()});const fl=6+Math.sin(t*30)*3+k*8;g.fillStyle="#fb923c";[-11,-5].forEach(o=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]+o-3,sh[1]+18);g.lineTo(sh[0]+o,sh[1]+18+fl+5);g.lineTo(sh[0]+o+3,sh[1]+18);g.fill()})}
  else if(id==="bt"){const f=Math.sin(t*(k>.1?10:5))*.3;[["#f472b6","#be185d",-.6,12,8],["#60a5fa","#1d4ed8",.5,9,6]].forEach(o=>{g.save();g.translate(sh[0]-2,sh[1]+(o[2]>0?9:3));g.rotate(o[2]-f*(o[2]>0?-1:1));g.fillStyle=o[0];g.strokeStyle=o[1];g.lineWidth=1.3;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-o[3]+2,0,o[3],o[4],0,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fff8";g.beginPath();g.arc(-o[3],0,2.5,0,7);g.fill();g.restore()})}
  else{const f=Math.sin(t*(k>.1?9:4))*.28,an=id==="aa";g.fillStyle=an?"#f8fafc":"#7f1d1d";g.strokeStyle=an?"#94a3b8":"#450a0a";g.lineWidth=1.4;
   for(const s of[1,.7]){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]-2,sh[1]+2);g.quadraticCurveTo(sh[0]-14*s,sh[1]-18*s-f*18,sh[0]-30*s,sh[1]-20*s-f*26);g.quadraticCurveTo(sh[0]-24*s,sh[1]-4*s,sh[0]-26*s,sh[1]+6*s-f*10);g.quadraticCurveTo(sh[0]-14*s,sh[1]+6,sh[0]-2,sh[1]+8);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()}}
  g.restore()},
 body(id,hip,sh,hd,k){if(!id||id==="no")return;g.save();const t=time,tor=(a,b,f,s)=>{g.fillStyle=f;g.strokeStyle=s||"#0005";g.lineWidth=1.2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]-a,sh[1]);g.lineTo(sh[0]+a,sh[1]);g.lineTo(hip[0]+b,hip[1]+3);g.lineTo(hip[0]-b,hip[1]+3);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()};
  if(id==="rj")tor(6,5,"#dc2626");
  else if(id==="ch"){tor(6.5,5.5,"#15803d");g.fillStyle="#14532d";g.fillRect(sh[0]-1,sh[1]+2,2,hip[1]-sh[1]-1);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(sh[0]+2,sh[1]+4,3,3)}

  else if(id==="kn"||id==="kd"){const go=id==="kd";if(go){g.shadowColor="#fbbf24";g.shadowBlur=7}tor(7,5.5,go?"#fbbf24":"#9ca3af",go?"#92400e":"#374151");g.fillStyle=go?"#fef08a":"#e5e7eb";g.fillRect(sh[0]-3,sh[1]+2,3,8);g.fillStyle=go?"#f59e0b":"#6b7280";[-1,1].forEach(s=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(sh[0]+s*7,sh[1]+1,4.2,0,7);g.fill()});g.fillStyle="#78350f";g.fillRect(hip[0]-6,hip[1]-2,12,3)}
  else if(id==="es"){tor(7,6,"#f8fafc","#94a3b8");g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.fillRect(sh[0]-7,sh[1]+5,14,3);g.fillStyle="#22d3ee";g.beginPath();g.arc(sh[0],sh[1]+11,2.2+Math.sin(t*5)*.5,0,7);g.fill()}
  else if(id==="dr"){tor(7,5.5,"#b91c1c","#450a0a");g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.fillRect(sh[0]-2,sh[1]+3,4,hip[1]-sh[1]-3);g.fillStyle="#7f1d1d";[-1,1].forEach(s=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]+s*5,sh[1]);g.lineTo(sh[0]+s*10,sh[1]-8);g.lineTo(sh[0]+s*9,sh[1]+3);g.closePath();g.fill()});for(let i=0;i<3;i++){g.strokeStyle="#7f1d1d";g.beginPath();g.arc(sh[0],sh[1]+4+i*4,4,0,Math.PI);g.stroke()}}
  g.restore()},
 head(h,f,hip,sh,hd,k){const x=hd[0],y=hd[1],t=time;g.save();g.lineWidth=1.4;g.strokeStyle="#0006";
  const dome=(col,r)=>{g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,r||10.4,Math.PI,0);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()},st5=(a,b,r)=>{g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const q=-Math.PI/2+i*Math.PI/5,rr=i%2?r*.45:r;g.lineTo(a+Math.cos(q)*rr,b+Math.sin(q)*rr)}g.closePath();g.fill()},eyes=(o)=>{g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(x+2,y-4+(o||0),2.2,3);g.fillRect(x+8,y-4+(o||0),2.2,3)};
  if(f==="ga"){g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(x-1,y-4,11,5);g.fillStyle="#ffffff66";g.fillRect(x+1,y-3,3,2);g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-1,y-2);g.lineTo(x-9,y-3);g.stroke()}

  else if(f==="pi"){g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+5,y-1,3.6,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-9,y-6);g.lineTo(x+9,y+3);g.stroke()}
  else if(f==="ba"){g.fillStyle="#78350f";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-6,y+4);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+2,y+3,x+9,y+3);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+9,y+16,x+1,y+17);g.quadraticCurveTo(x-6,y+14,x-6,y+4);g.fill()}

  else if(f==="ch"){g.fillStyle="#ef4444";[3,9].forEach(o=>{g.save();g.translate(x+o,y-2);g.scale(.42,.42);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,5);g.bezierCurveTo(-8,-1,-5,-9,0,-3);g.bezierCurveTo(5,-9,8,-1,0,5);g.fill();g.restore()});g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.3;g.beginPath();g.arc(x+6,y+3,3,.2,2.9);g.stroke()}
  else if(f==="en"){g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(x+2,y-2,2.4,3);g.fillRect(x+8,y-2,2.4,3);g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.8;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-1,y-6);g.lineTo(x+5,y-3.5);g.moveTo(x+12,y-6);g.lineTo(x+7,y-3.5);g.moveTo(x+2,y+6);g.quadraticCurveTo(x+6,y+3,x+10,y+6);g.stroke()}

  else if(f==="ll"){g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(x+2,y-3,2.4,3);g.fillRect(x+8,y-3,2.4,3);g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.6;g.beginPath();g.arc(x+6,y+7,3.5,3.3,6.1);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#38bdf8";const ty=((t*2)%1)*9;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x+3,y+1+ty,1.3,2,0,0,7);g.fill()}

  else if(f==="gt"){g.fillStyle="#f472b6";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+7,y+1);g.lineTo(x+11,y+1);g.lineTo(x+9,y+4);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.1;[[-1,0],[0,2],[1,4]].forEach(a=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+9,y+3);g.lineTo(x+17,y+3+a[1]+a[0]*2);g.moveTo(x+5,y+3);g.lineTo(x-3,y+3+a[1]+a[0]*2);g.stroke()});g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(x+2,y-4,2.2,2.6);g.fillRect(x+8,y-4,2.2,2.6)}
  else if(f==="sx"){eyes();g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+6,y+1.5,5,.05,Math.PI-.05);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(x+2.5,y+1.5,7,1.6)}
  else if(f==="ko"){g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.7;g.beginPath();g.arc(x+3.5,y-2,2.2,Math.PI,0);g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.arc(x+9,y-2,2.2,Math.PI,0);g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.arc(x+6,y+1,4.2,.2,2.94);g.stroke()}
  else if(f==="bl"){eyes();g.fillStyle="#fb7185aa";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+1.5,y+2.5,2.2,0,7);g.arc(x+11,y+2.5,2.2,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.arc(x+6,y+1,3.2,.3,2.84);g.stroke()}

// ---- efectos de ambiente por nivel
function fxDraw(k,cx,t){
 if(k==="grid"){g.save();g.strokeStyle="#ff2e9744";g.lineWidth=1.5;for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const y=250+i*i*3.2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,y);g.lineTo(W,y);g.stroke()}for(let i=-8;i<=8;i++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(W/2+i*20,250);g.lineTo(W/2+i*130-((cx*.2)%130),H);g.stroke()}g.restore()}
 else if(k==="lightning"){const q=t%6;if(q<.5){g.save();g.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+(1-q/.5)+")";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();let x=((Math.floor(t/6)*211)%700)+50,y=0;g.moveTo(x,y);while(y<260){y+=34;x+=(((y*7+Math.floor(t/6)*13)%40)-20);g.lineTo(x,y)}g.stroke();g.restore()}}}
function fxFront(k,cx,t){
 g.save();
 if(k==="lightning"){const q=t%6;if(q<.12){g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+(.45*(1-q/.12))+")";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)}g.restore();return}
 for(let i=0;i<30;i++){const sd=(i*97.13)%1,r1=((i*53)%100)/100,r2=((i*29)%100)/100;
  if(k==="sand"){const x=(((r1*W*1.5-t*(300+i*8)-cx*.4)%(W+60))+W+60)%(W+60)-30,y=40+r2*340;g.fillStyle="rgba(250,220,150,.35)";g.fillRect(x,y,14+r1*14,2)}
  else if(k==="wind"){const x=(((r1*W*1.5-t*(380+i*10)-cx*.4)%(W+80))+W+80)%(W+80)-40,y=30+r2*330;g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.4)";g.fillRect(x,y,26+r1*22,2)}
  else if(k==="embers"){const x=(r1*W*1.3+Math.sin(t*1.5+i)*20-cx*.3)%(W+20),y=H-(((t*(40+r2*60)+r1*500)%460));g.fillStyle=i%2?"#ff9f1c":"#ffd166";g.globalAlpha=.6+.3*Math.sin(t*6+i);g.fillRect(((x%W)+W)%W,y,3,3);g.globalAlpha=1}
  else if(k==="fireflies"){const x=((r1*W*1.4+Math.sin(t*.8+i*2)*40-cx*.5)%(W+10)+W+10)%(W+10),y=60+r2*290+Math.cos(t*.9+i)*20,a=.4+.5*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*2+i));g.fillStyle="rgba(250,240,120,"+a+")";g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,2.4,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="rgba(250,240,120,"+a*.25+")";g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,7,0,7);g.fill()}
  else if(k==="bubbles"){const x=((r1*W*1.3+Math.sin(t+i)*14-cx*.4)%(W+10)+W+10)%(W+10),y=H-(((t*(24+r2*40)+r1*500)%470)),r=2+r2*5;g.strokeStyle="rgba(190,240,255,.6)";g.lineWidth=1.4;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,r,0,7);g.stroke()}
  else if(k==="wisps"&&i<10){const x=((r1*W*1.4+t*(14+r2*10)-cx*.35)%(W+80)+W+80)%(W+80)-40,y=120+r2*200+Math.sin(t*1.2+i)*18;g.fillStyle="rgba(210,200,255,"+(.10+.07*Math.sin(t+i))+")";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,y,16,22,0,0,7);g.fill();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-16,y);g.lineTo(x-10,y+30);g.lineTo(x,y+22);g.lineTo(x+10,y+30);g.lineTo(x+16,y);g.fill()}}
 g.restore()}

const RC=RCT.split("\n").map(l=>{const a=l.split("|"),o={};(a[8]||"").split(";").forEach(k=>{if(k){const[x,y]=k.split("=");o[x]=+y}});const T0=RTP[a[2]];return Object.assign({id:a[0],n:a[1],tp:a[2],el:+a[3],rar:+a[4],ar:+a[5],col:a[6],acc:a[7],o,T0},{})});
const RCI=Object.fromEntries(RC.map(c=>[c.id,c]));
function rcStat(c,L,n){const T0=c.T0,o=c.o,m=Math.pow(1.1,L-1),cnt=o.n||1,f=cnt>1?(cnt===2?.62:cnt===3?.46:.36):1;
 if(c.tp==="spell")return{dmg:Math.round(T0.d*c.el*(o.dmm||1)*m),rad:o.rad||70,freeze:o.freeze||0,fx:o.fx||"fire"};
 if(c.tp==="bld")return{hp:Math.round(T0.h*c.el*(o.hpm||1)*m),dmg:Math.round(T0.d*c.el*(o.dmm||1)*m),as:T0.as,r:o.r||T0.r,life:o.life||30,spl:o.spl||0,spawn:o.spawn||0};
 return{hp:Math.round(T0.h*c.el*(o.hpm||1)*f*m),dmg:Math.round(T0.d*c.el*(o.dmm||1)*f*m*(c.tp==="tank"?1:1)),as:T0.as,r:o.r||T0.r,s:T0.s*(o.spd||1),fly:T0.fly||o.fly||0,spl:T0.spl||o.spl||0,bld:T0.bld||0,n:cnt,sc:o.sc||1,heal:o.heal||0,slow:o.slow||0}}
const RCOST=[0,2,4,10,20,50,100,200,400,800],RCOIN=[0,5,20,50,150,400,1000,2000,4000,8000];
// ---------- datos guardados
let RD=null;
const rdSave=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem("stk_roy2",JSON.stringify(RD))}catch(e){}};
function rdLoad(){try{RD=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stk_roy2")||"null")}catch(e){RD=null}
 if(!RD){RD={c:300,gm:60,tr:0,cd:{},dk:["pa","es","ar","br","fl","bf","du","eo"],ch:[{t:0,st:0},null,null,null],tut:0,fr:0,sh:{d:"",b:[]},w:0,pl:0};["pa","es","ar","br","fl","bf","du","eo"].forEach(i=>RD.cd[i]={n:0,l:1});rdSave()}}
const rdMaxAr=()=>{let a=0;RARENA.forEach((r,i)=>{if(RD.tr>=r[3])a=i});return a};
const rdLv=id=>RD.cd[id]?RD.cd[id].l:1;
// ---------- cofres
const RCH=[{n:"Cofre de Madera",col:"#a16207",min:1,gm:5,coin:[40,80],cards:3,w:[.8,.18,.02,0]},{n:"Cofre de Plata",col:"#cbd5e1",min:5,gm:15,coin:[110,200],cards:6,w:[.65,.28,.07,0]},{n:"Cofre de Oro",col:"#fbbf24",min:20,gm:45,coin:[300,520],cards:14,w:[.5,.3,.17,.03]},{n:"Cofre Mágico",col:"#a855f7",min:60,gm:120,coin:[900,1400],cards:36,w:[.3,.35,.25,.1]}];
function rdChest(t){const C=RCH[t],ar=rdMaxAr(),pool=RC.filter(c=>c.ar<=ar),res={},coin=C.coin[0]+Math.floor(Math.random()*(C.coin[1]-C.coin[0]));let left=C.cards;
 const kinds=2+t+(Math.random()<.5?1:0);for(let k=0;k<kinds&&left>0;k++){let r=Math.random(),rr=0;for(;rr<3&&r>C.w[rr];rr++)r-=C.w[rr];const ps=pool.filter(c=>c.rar===rr),ct=ps.length?ps:pool,c=ct[Math.floor(Math.random()*ct.length)],q=k===kinds-1?left:Math.max(1,Math.floor(left*(.3+Math.random()*.4)));const cnt=rr===0?q:rr===1?Math.max(1,Math.ceil(q/3)):rr===2?Math.max(1,Math.ceil(q/12)):1;res[c.id]=(res[c.id]||0)+cnt;left-=q}
 const list=Object.keys(res).map(id=>({id,n:res[id],nw:!RD.cd[id]}));list.forEach(o=>{if(!RD.cd[o.id])RD.cd[o.id]={n:0,l:1};RD.cd[o.id].n+=o.n});RD.c+=coin;const gm=Math.random()<.4?1+t:0;RD.gm+=gm;rdSave();return{list,coin,gm,t}}
function rdGive(t){const s=RD.ch.findIndex(x=>!x);if(s<0){RD.c+=60+t*60;rdSave();return false}RD.ch[s]={t,st:0};rdSave();return true}
// ---------- estado de pantalla y batalla
const ry={page:"home",sel:null,cpg:0,rev:null,tab:0,msg:"",mt:0};
let RB=null;const RAX=40,RAY=30,RAW=720,RAH=320,RLY=[105,275];
const rbMid=()=>RAX+RAW/2;
function rbTower(side,k,lane,x,y,hp){return{side,k,lane,x,y,hp,mx:hp,at:0,act:k==="p",fl:0,tw:1}}
function rbStart(tut){const ar=rdMaxAr(),lv=RD.dk.reduce((a,i)=>a+rdLv(i),0)/8,aiL=Math.max(1,Math.round(lv+(ar>2?0:-1))),ph=tut?900:1100,kh=tut?1400:1800,th=tut?500:ph;
 let ed=[];const pool=RC.filter(c=>c.ar<=ar&&c.tp!=="spell"||c.ar<=ar&&c.el<=4);while(ed.length<8){const c=pool[Math.floor(Math.random()*pool.length)];if(!ed.includes(c.id))ed.push(c.id)}
 const dk=tut?["es","ar","br","pa","fl","du","eo","bf"]:RD.dk.slice();
 RB={sp:[],sh:0,em:[],ot:0,tut,ar,t:tut?9999:150,el:5,ee:5,dk:dk.slice(),hq:dk.slice(0,4),nx:dk.slice(4),sel:-1,units:[],pr:[],fx:[],ed,eq:ed.slice(0,4),en:ed.slice(4),ai:1.5,aiL,over:0,pause:0,res:null,uid:1,last:performance.now(),st:tut?0:-1,stT:0,stOk:0,
  tw:[rbTower(0,"p",0,175,RLY[0],tut?ph:ph),rbTower(0,"p",1,175,RLY[1],ph),rbTower(0,"k",-1,95,190,kh),rbTower(1,"p",0,625,RLY[0],tut?th:ph),rbTower(1,"p",1,625,RLY[1],tut?th:ph),rbTower(1,"k",-1,705,190,tut?900:kh)]};
 ry.page="battle";ry.sel=null}
function rbMake(id,side,x,y,L){const c=RCI[id],st=rcStat(c,L,1),us=[];
 if(c.tp==="spell")return null;
 const mk=(dx,dy)=>({uid:RB.uid++,id,side,x:x+dx,y:y+dy,hp:st.hp,mx:st.hp,dmg:st.dmg,as:st.as,at:Math.random()*.4,r:st.r||26,s:st.s||0,fly:st.fly,spl:st.spl,bldT:st.bld,sc:st.sc||1,heal:st.heal,slow:st.slow,col:c.col,acc:c.acc,tgt:null,rt:0,fr:0,sl:0,fx:0,dead:0,dep:.45,face:side?-1:1,anim:Math.random()*6,bld:c.tp==="bld",life:st.life||0,spawn:st.spawn,sp:0,lane:y<190?0:1});
 const n=st.n||1;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=i/n*6.28;us.push(mk(n>1?Math.cos(a)*12:0,n>1?Math.sin(a)*12:0))}return us}
function rbDeploy(id,side,x,y){const c=RCI[id],L=side?RB.aiL:rdLv(id);
 if(c.tp==="spell"){const st=rcStat(c,L,1),k=rbKing(side);RB.sp.push({c,st,side,x,y,x0:k.x,y0:k.y-30,t:0,T:st.fx==="fire"?.8:st.fx==="arrow"?.7:.4});sfx(st.fx==="arrow"?1200:300,.3,"triangle",.05,st.fx==="arrow"?-900:200);return}
 const us=rbMake(id,side,x,y,L);us.forEach(u=>RB.units.push(u));RB.fx.push({k:"pop",x,y,t:.4,col:c.col})}
function rbDmg(t,a,src){if(t.dead||t.hp<=0)return;a=Math.round(a);t.hp-=a;t.fl=.15;RB.fx.push({k:"num",x:t.x+(Math.random()-.5)*10,y:t.y-(t.tw?44:30),t:.7,v:a,col:t.side?"#fde047":"#fca5a5"});if(t.tw&&t.k==="k")t.act=true;
 if(t.hp<=0){t.hp=0;if(t.tw){RB.sh=.5;RB.fx.push({k:"boom",x:t.x,y:t.y,t:.8,col:"#fbbf24"},{k:"crown",x:t.x,y:t.y-30,t:1.3,side:t.side});const kg=rbKing(t.side);if(kg)kg.act=true;sfx(90,.7,"sawtooth",.12,-40);sfx(600,.4,"square",.05,600);if(t.side===0)rbEmo(1,["😂","😜","👑"][Math.floor(Math.random()*3)]);else if(Math.random()<.7)rbEmo(1,["😡","😭"][Math.floor(Math.random()*2)])}else{t.dead=.5;RB.fx.push({k:"puff",x:t.x,y:t.y-10,t:.5,col:t.col})}}}
function rbEmo(side,e){RB.em=RB.em||[];RB.em.push({side,e,t:2.2})}
function rbPk(u){return u.acc==="arc"?"arrow":u.acc==="can"?(u.id==="ls"||u.id==="tl"?"laser":"ball"):u.acc==="bom"?"bomb":u.acc==="mag"?"orb":"orb"}
const rbPr=(x,y,t,dmg,spl,col,side,sl,k)=>({x,y,t,dmg,spl,col,sp:k==="laser"?900:k==="arrow"?430:k==="bomb"?250:k==="ball"?360:330,side,sl,k,d0:Math.max(20,Math.hypot(t.x-x,t.y-y)),tr:0});
function rbSpell(c,st,side,x,y){RB.fx.push({k:st.fx,x,y,t:.7,r:st.rad,col:c.col});
 RB.units.forEach(u=>{if(u.side!==side&&!u.dead&&Math.hypot(u.x-x,u.y-y)<=st.rad+8){rbDmg(u,st.dmg);if(st.freeze)u.fr=st.freeze}});
 RB.tw.forEach(t=>{if(t.side!==side&&t.hp>0&&Math.hypot(t.x-x,t.y-y)<=st.rad+18)rbDmg(t,Math.round(st.dmg*.35))})}
const rbAll=side=>RB.units.filter(u=>u.side===side&&!u.dead),rbTw=side=>RB.tw.filter(t=>t.side===side&&t.hp>0);
function rbKing(side){return RB.tw.find(t=>t.side===side&&t.k==="k")}
function rbSteer(u,tx,ty,dt,sp){let dx=tx,dy=ty;if(!u.fly){const mid=rbMid(),by=RLY[u.y<190?0:1];const wrong=(u.x<mid-6&&tx>mid+6)||(u.x>mid+6&&tx<mid-6);if(wrong){dx=mid;dy=by;if(Math.abs(u.x-mid)<28&&Math.abs(u.y-by)>14){dx=mid;dy=by}else if(Math.abs(u.y-by)>14&&Math.abs(u.x-mid)<60){dx=u.x;dy=by}}}
 const vx=dx-u.x,vy=dy-u.y,d=Math.hypot(vx,vy)||1;u.x+=vx/d*sp*dt;u.y+=vy/d*sp*dt;u.face=vx>=0?1:-1;
 if(!u.fly){const mid=rbMid();if(Math.abs(u.x-mid)<22&&Math.abs(u.y-RLY[0])>20&&Math.abs(u.y-RLY[1])>20){u.x+=(u.x<mid?-1:1)*(22-Math.abs(u.x-mid))}}
 u.x=Math.max(RAX+6,Math.min(RAX+RAW-6,u.x));u.y=Math.max(RAY+8,Math.min(RAY+RAH-8,u.y))}
function rbFind(u){const en=u.side?0:1;let best=null,bd=1e9;
 if(u.heal){RB.units.forEach(a=>{if(a.side===u.side&&!a.dead&&a!==u&&a.hp<a.mx*.9){const d=Math.hypot(a.x-u.x,a.y-u.y);if(d<150&&d<bd){bd=d;best=a}}});if(best)return best;bd=1e9}
 if(!u.bldT)RB.units.forEach(a=>{if(a.side===en&&!a.dead&&!(a.dep>0)&&!(a.bld&&false)){const d=Math.hypot(a.x-u.x,a.y-u.y);if(d<(u.bld?u.r+10:125)&&d<bd&&(!a.fly||true)){bd=d;best=a}}});
 else RB.units.forEach(a=>{if(a.side===en&&!a.dead&&!(a.dep>0)&&a.bld){const d=Math.hypot(a.x-u.x,a.y-u.y);if(d<150&&d<bd){bd=d;best=a}}});
 if(best)return best;bd=1e9;rbTw(en).forEach(t=>{const d=Math.hypot(t.x-u.x,t.y-u.y)+(t.k==="k"?24:0);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=t}});return best}
function rbHit(u,t){if(u.heal&&t.side===u.side){t.hp=Math.min(t.mx,t.hp+u.dmg*1.2);RB.fx.push({k:"heal",x:t.x,y:t.y,t:.5});return}
 const a=u.dmg;if(u.r>40){RB.pr.push(rbPr(u.x,u.y-12*u.sc,t,a,u.spl,u.col,u.side,u.slow,rbPk(u)))}else{rbDmg(t,a);if(u.spl)rbSplash(t,a*.7,u.side,34)}}
function rbSplash(t,a,side,r){RB.units.forEach(o=>{if(o!==t&&o.side!==side&&!o.dead&&Math.hypot(o.x-t.x,o.y-t.y)<r)rbDmg(o,a)})}
function rbUpd(dt){const B=RB;if(B.pause||B.over)return;
 B.t-=dt;const dbl=B.ot?3:B.t<60&&!B.tut?2:1;B.el=Math.min(10,B.el+dt/2.8*dbl*(B.tut?2:1));B.ee=Math.min(10,B.ee+dt/2.8*dbl*(B.tut?.6:1));
 if(B.tut&&B.st<=3)B.ai=99;
 B.ai-=dt;if(B.ai<=0&&!(B.tut&&B.st<4)){const hand=B.eq.map((id,i)=>({id,i})).filter(o=>RCI[o.id].el<=B.ee);if(hand.length){const o=hand[Math.floor(Math.random()*hand.length)],c=RCI[o.id];B.ee-=c.el;const ln=Math.random()<.5?0:1,y=RLY[ln]+(Math.random()-.5)*40,x=c.tp==="spell"?(rbAll(0).length?rbAll(0)[0].x:200):730-Math.random()*130;const yy=c.tp==="spell"&&rbAll(0).length?rbAll(0)[0].y:y;rbDeploy(o.id,1,Math.max(rbMid()+26,x),yy);const nid=B.en.shift();B.en.push(o.id);B.eq[o.i]=nid;B.ai=(B.tut?4.5:Math.max(1.1,2.8-B.ar*.12))+Math.random()*1.4}else B.ai=.6}
 B.units.forEach(u=>{if(u.dead){u.dead-=dt;return}if(u.dep>0){u.dep-=dt;if(u.dep<=0)B.fx.push({k:"land",x:u.x,y:u.y,t:.3,col:u.col});return}u.anim+=dt;if(u.fx>0)u.fx-=dt;if(u.fl>0)u.fl-=dt;if(u.fr>0){u.fr-=dt;return}if(u.sl>0)u.sl-=dt;
  if(u.bld){u.life-=dt;if(u.life<=0){u.dead=.01;return}u.hp-=dt*(u.mx/(u.life+dt+30))*0;if(u.spawn&&(u.sp-=dt)<=0){u.sp=8;const us=rbMake("sk",u.side,u.x,u.y,B.aiL);us&&us.forEach(q=>B.units.push(q))}}
  u.rt-=dt;if(u.rt<=0||!u.tgt||u.tgt.dead||u.tgt.hp<=0){u.tgt=rbFind(u);u.rt=.35}u.at-=dt;const t=u.tgt;if(!t)return;const tr=t.tw?(t.k==="k"?26:20):10*(t.sc||1),d=Math.hypot(t.x-u.x,t.y-u.y);
  if(d<=u.r+tr){if(u.at<=0){u.at=u.as*(u.sl>0?1.5:1);rbHit(u,t);u.fx=.15}}else if(!u.bld)rbSteer(u,t.x,t.y,dt,u.s*(u.sl>0?.55:1))});
 for(let i=0;i<B.units.length;i++){const a=B.units[i];if(a.dead||a.bld||a.fly)continue;for(let j=i+1;j<B.units.length;j++){const b=B.units[j];if(b.dead||b.bld||b.fly||a.side!==b.side)continue;const dx=b.x-a.x,dy=b.y-a.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||.1,m=11*Math.max(a.sc,b.sc);if(d<m){const p=(m-d)/2;a.x-=dx/d*p*.5;a.y-=dy/d*p*.5;b.x+=dx/d*p*.5;b.y+=dy/d*p*.5}}}
 B.tw.forEach(t=>{if(t.hp<=0)return;if(t.fl>0)t.fl-=dt;if(!t.act)return;t.at-=dt;if(t.at>0)return;let best=null,bd=t.k==="k"?135:150;B.units.forEach(u=>{if(u.side!==t.side&&!u.dead&&!(u.dep>0)){const d=Math.hypot(u.x-t.x,u.y-t.y);if(d<bd){bd=d;best=u}}});if(best){t.at=1;B.pr.push(rbPr(t.x,t.y-26,best,t.k==="k"?55:48,0,t.k==="k"?"#334155":"#fde047",t.side,0,t.k==="k"?"ball":"arrow"));if(t.k==="k")sfx(140,.12,"square",.04,-60);else sfx(900,.05,"triangle",.02,-400)}});
 B.pr.forEach(p=>{const t=p.t;if(!t||t.dead||t.hp<=0){p.gone=1;return}const dx=t.x-p.x,dy=t.y-8-p.y,d=Math.hypot(dx,dy);if(d<9){p.gone=1;rbDmg(t,p.dmg);if(p.sl)t.sl=2;if(p.spl){rbSplash(t,p.dmg*.7,p.side,34);B.fx.push({k:"boom",x:t.x,y:t.y-6,t:.4,col:p.col})}else B.fx.push({k:"spark",x:t.x,y:t.y-10,t:.25,col:p.col})}else{const st=Math.min(d,p.sp*dt);p.x+=dx/d*st;p.y+=dy/d*st;p.tr+=st}});B.pr=B.pr.filter(p=>!p.gone);
 B.units.forEach(u=>{if(u.hp<=0&&!u.dead)u.dead=.4});B.units=B.units.filter(u=>!(u.dead<0));
 B.sp.forEach(q=>{q.t+=dt;if(q.t>=q.T&&!q.done){q.done=1;rbSpell(q.c,q.st,q.side,q.x,q.y);B.sh=Math.max(B.sh,q.st.fx==="fire"||q.st.fx==="dark"?.35:.15);sfx(q.st.fx==="zap"?1800:80,.4,"sawtooth",.1,q.st.fx==="zap"?-1500:-40)}});B.sp=B.sp.filter(q=>!q.done);if(B.sh>0)B.sh-=dt;(B.em||[]).forEach(e=>e.t-=dt);B.em=(B.em||[]).filter(e=>e.t>0);B.fx.forEach(f=>f.t-=dt);B.fx=B.fx.filter(f=>f.t>0);
 B.cw=[0,0];B.tw.forEach(t=>{if(t.hp<=0){B.cw[t.side?0:1]+=t.k==="k"?3:1}});
 const kp=rbKing(0),ke=rbKing(1);
 if(!B.tut){if(kp.hp<=0||ke.hp<=0)rbEnd();else if(B.ot&&B.cw[0]!==B.cw[1])rbEnd();else if(B.t<=0){if(!B.ot&&B.cw[0]===B.cw[1]){B.ot=1;B.t=60;B.fx.push({k:"txt",x:400,y:180,t:2.2,s:"¡MUERTE SÚBITA!",col:"#f43f5e"});sfx(220,.8,"sawtooth",.08,300)}else rbEnd()}}else{rbTut(dt)}}
function rbEnd(){const B=RB;B.over=1;const c0=B.cw[0],c1=B.cw[1];let r=c0>c1?1:c0<c1?-1:0;
 if(r===0){const h0=B.tw.filter(t=>t.side===0).reduce((a,t)=>a+t.hp,0),h1=B.tw.filter(t=>t.side===1).reduce((a,t)=>a+t.hp,0);if(Math.abs(h0-h1)>80)r=h0>h1?1:-1}
 const ar=B.ar,gain=r>0?28+ar*2:r<0?-(18+ar*2):0,coin=r>0?30+ar*8+c0*10:8,tr0=RD.tr;RD.tr=Math.max(0,RD.tr+gain);RD.c+=coin;RD.pl++;let ch=-1;if(r>0){RD.w++;const rr=Math.random();ch=rr<.55?0:rr<.85?1:rr<.97?2:3;if(!rdGive(ch))ch=-2}rdSave();B.res={r,c0,c1,gain,coin,ch,up:rdMaxAr()>(function(){let a=0;RARENA.forEach((x,i)=>{if(tr0>=x[3])a=i});return a})()}}
// ---------- tutorial
const RTUT=["¡Bienvenido, comandante! Aquí se pelea con cartas. Destruye las torres enemigas.","Tu ELIXIR (barra morada) sube solo. Cada carta cuesta elixir (la gotita).","Toca una de tus cartas de abajo.","¡Ahora toca TU lado del campo (zona verde) para soltarla!","¡Tus soldados atacan solos! Mira cómo avanzan. Suelta más cartas.","Cada torre princesa = 1 corona. Si cae el rey, ganas al instante. ¡Derriba una torre enemiga!"];
function rbTut(dt){const B=RB;B.stT+=dt;
 if(B.st===2&&B.sel>=0)B.st=3;
 else if(B.st===3&&B.units.some(u=>u.side===0)){B.st=4;B.stT=0}
 else if(B.st===4&&B.stT>9)B.st=5;
 else if(B.st===6){if(B.cw[0]>=1||rbKing(1).hp<=0){B.over=1;RD.tut=1;if(!RD.cd.jo)RD.cd.jo={n:0,l:1};RD.c+=200;RD.gm+=20;rdGive(1);rdSave();B.res={tut:1}}}}
// ---------- dibujo de personajes
function ryUnit(x,y,sc,col,acc,face,t,mv,fly,fl,atk){atk=atk||0;g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.scale(face*sc,sc);const w=Math.sin(t*10)*(mv?5:0);if(fly)g.translate(0,-16+Math.sin(t*5)*2);
 g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";if(fl>0){g.shadowColor="#fff";g.shadowBlur=10}
 if(acc==="ala"){const f=Math.sin(t*14)*6;g.fillStyle=col+"cc";g.strokeStyle="#0006";g.lineWidth=1;[-1,1].forEach(s=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(s*1,-20);g.quadraticCurveTo(s*14,-34+f,s*20,-26+f);g.quadraticCurveTo(s*14,-18,s*2,-14);g.fill();g.stroke()})}
 if(acc==="cap"){g.fillStyle="#7f1d1d";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-1,-21);g.quadraticCurveTo(-12,-12+w*.3,-9,-2);g.lineTo(-1,-8);g.fill()}
 g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-10);g.lineTo(-4+w,0);g.moveTo(0,-10);g.lineTo(4-w,0);g.moveTo(0,-10);g.lineTo(0,-22);g.moveTo(0,-19);g.lineTo(-6,-13-w*.3);g.moveTo(0,-19);g.lineTo(7,-14+w*.3);g.stroke();
 g.fillStyle="#f8fafc";g.strokeStyle=col;g.lineWidth=2.4;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,-28,6,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(2,-30,1.6,1.6);
 g.strokeStyle="#111827";g.lineWidth=2;
 g.save();if(atk>0){g.translate(0,-19);g.rotate(-1.3+Math.min(1,(.15-atk)/.15)*2.1);g.translate(0,19)}
 if(acc==="esp"||acc==="esc"){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(7,-14);g.lineTo(20,-24);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#cbd5e1";g.lineWidth=1.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(8,-15);g.lineTo(19,-23);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#92400e";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(5,-18);g.lineTo(10,-11);g.stroke()}
 if(acc==="esc"){g.fillStyle="#475569";g.strokeStyle="#0f172a";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.arc(-8,-14,6,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke()}
 if(acc==="arc"){g.strokeStyle="#92400e";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(9,-17,9,-1.2,1.2);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#e5e7eb";g.lineWidth=1;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(11,-26);g.lineTo(11,-8);g.stroke()}
 if(acc==="hach"){g.strokeStyle="#78350f";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(7,-12);g.lineTo(16,-30);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#9ca3af";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(14,-30);g.lineTo(23,-27);g.lineTo(15,-22);g.fill()}
 if(acc==="lan"){g.strokeStyle="#78350f";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(5,-6);g.lineTo(26,-30);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#e5e7eb";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(26,-30);g.lineTo(21,-29);g.lineTo(25,-25);g.fill()}
 if(acc==="mar"){g.strokeStyle="#78350f";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(7,-12);g.lineTo(15,-30);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#6b7280";g.fillRect(9,-37,14,9)}
 g.restore();
 if(acc==="mag"){g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,-33);g.lineTo(0,-46);g.lineTo(6,-33);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillRect(-8,-34,16,3);g.strokeStyle="#78350f";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(8,-6);g.lineTo(10,-30);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.shadowColor="#fde047";g.shadowBlur=6;g.beginPath();g.arc(10,-32,3,0,7);g.fill();g.shadowBlur=0}
 if(acc==="gaf"){g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=1.4;g.beginPath();g.arc(-1,-29,2.6,0,7);g.moveTo(8,-29);g.arc(5.4,-29,2.6,0,7);g.moveTo(1.6,-29);g.lineTo(2.8,-29);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#38bdf855";g.beginPath();g.arc(-1,-29,2.4,0,7);g.arc(5.4,-29,2.4,0,7);g.fill()}
 if(acc==="cor"){g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,-33);g.lineTo(-6,-40);g.lineTo(-3,-36);g.lineTo(0,-42);g.lineTo(3,-36);g.lineTo(6,-40);g.lineTo(6,-33);g.fill()}
 if(acc==="cue"){g.fillStyle="#dc2626";[-1,1].forEach(s=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(s*3,-33);g.lineTo(s*8,-42);g.lineTo(s*6,-32);g.fill()})}
 if(acc==="cas"){g.fillStyle="#e5e7eb";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,-28,7.4,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#38bdf8";g.fillRect(1,-31,6,4)}
 if(acc==="bom"){g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.arc(10,-13,5,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#f59e0b";g.lineWidth=1.4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(10,-18);g.lineTo(13,-22);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(12,-24,3,3)}
 if(acc==="can"){g.fillStyle="#374151";g.fillRect(2,-20,16,7);g.fillStyle="#111827";g.beginPath();g.arc(2,-10,5,0,7);g.fill()}
 g.restore()}
function ryBld(x,y,col,acc,t,sc){g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.scale(sc||1,sc||1);g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,0,15,5,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#57534e";g.fillRect(-12,-10,24,10);g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,-14,10,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillRect(-10,-14,20,5);g.fillStyle="#111827";g.fillRect(4,-17,16,5);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(-4,-14,3,3);g.restore()}
function ryUnitU(u,t){const sc=u.sc*1.05;if(u.bld){ryBld(u.x,u.y,u.col,u.acc,t,u.sc);return}
 g.save();g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(u.x,u.y,8*sc,3*sc,0,0,7);g.fill();g.restore();
 const fy=u.fly?u.y-6:u.y;ryUnit(u.x,fy-(u.dep>0?u.dep*140:0),sc,u.col,u.acc,u.face,u.anim,u.tgt&&Math.hypot(u.tgt.x-u.x,u.tgt.y-u.y)>u.r+12,u.fly,u.fl,u.r<=40?u.fx:0);
 if(u.fr>0){g.fillStyle="#7dd3fc66";g.fillRect(u.x-9*sc,fy-36*sc,18*sc,38*sc)}
 if(u.hp<u.mx){const w=22*Math.max(.8,sc),yy=fy-42*sc;g.fillStyle="#000a";g.fillRect(u.x-w/2-1,yy-1,w+2,5);g.fillStyle=u.side?"#ef4444":"#3b82f6";g.fillRect(u.x-w/2,yy,w*u.hp/u.mx,3)}}
function ryCard(x,y,w,h,c,L,o){o=o||{};const rc=RARC[c.rar];g.save();if(o.sel)g.translate(0,-8);
 const gr=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+h);gr.addColorStop(0,o.dis?"#374151":rc);gr.addColorStop(1,o.dis?"#1f2937":"#1e1b4b");g.fillStyle=gr;rrect(x,y,w,h,8);g.fill();g.strokeStyle=o.sel?"#fff":o.lock?"#475569":rc;g.lineWidth=o.sel?3:2;rrect(x,y,w,h,8);g.stroke();
 g.save();rrect(x+3,y+3,w-6,h-16,6);g.clip();g.fillStyle=o.lock?"#111827":"#0b1030";g.fillRect(x+3,y+3,w-6,h-16);
 if(o.lock){txt("?",x+w/2,y+h*.55,22,"#475569","center")}else if(c.tp==="spell"){const fx=c.o.fx||"fire",cx=x+w/2,cy=y+(h-16)/2+4,rg=g.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,2,cx,cy,w*.34);rg.addColorStop(0,"#fff");rg.addColorStop(.4,c.col);rg.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=rg;g.fillRect(x,y,w,h);g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();if(fx==="zap"){g.moveTo(cx-4,cy-12);g.lineTo(cx+3,cy-2);g.lineTo(cx-3,cy+1);g.lineTo(cx+4,cy+12)}else if(fx==="arrow"){g.moveTo(cx-10,cy+8);g.lineTo(cx+10,cy-8);g.moveTo(cx+10,cy-8);g.lineTo(cx+3,cy-8);g.moveTo(cx+10,cy-8);g.lineTo(cx+10,cy-1)}else g.arc(cx,cy,w*.14,0,7);g.stroke()}
 else if(c.tp==="bld"){ryBld(x+w/2,y+h-26,c.col,c.acc,ry.mt,1.5)}
 else{const n=Math.min(3,c.o.n||1);for(let i=0;i<n;i++)ryUnit(x+w/2+(n>1?(i-(n-1)/2)*18:0),y+h-22-(n>1&&i%2?-4:0),(c.o.sc||1)*(n>1?1.15:1.5),c.col,c.acc,1,ry.mt+i,false,c.tp==="fly"||c.o.fly)}
 g.restore();
 if(!o.lock){txt(c.n.length>11?c.n.slice(0,10)+".":c.n,x+w/2,y+h-4,Math.min(10,w/9.5),"#fff","center");g.fillStyle=o.dis?"#6b7280":"#a21caf";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+11,y+11,9,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#f5d0fe";g.lineWidth=1.6;g.stroke();txt(""+c.el,x+11,y+15,11,"#fff","center");if(L>1||o.lv)txt("Nv."+L,x+w-4,y+13,9,"#fde047","right")}
 g.restore()}

function ryArena(){const A=RARENA[RB?RB.ar:rdMaxAr()],mid=rbMid();g.fillStyle=A[1];g.fillRect(RAX,RAY,RAW,RAH);for(let i=0;i<RAW/40;i++)for(let j=0;j<RAH/40;j++)if((i+j)%2){g.fillStyle="#0000001a";g.fillRect(RAX+i*40,RAY+j*40,40,40)}
 g.fillStyle=A[2];g.fillRect(mid-18,RAY,36,RAH);g.fillStyle="#ffffff44";for(let j=0;j<10;j++){const yy=RAY+((j*34+ry.mt*30)%RAH);g.fillRect(mid-12+Math.sin(yy*.1+ry.mt*3)*6,yy,10,2)}
 RLY.forEach(by=>{g.fillStyle="#8b5a2b";g.fillRect(mid-22,by-17,44,34);g.fillStyle="#a16207";for(let i=0;i<5;i++)g.fillRect(mid-22+i*9,by-17,5,34);g.strokeStyle="#451a03";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(mid-22,by-17,44,34)});
 g.strokeStyle="#0006";g.lineWidth=4;g.strokeRect(RAX,RAY,RAW,RAH)}
function ryTower(t){const x=t.x,y=t.y,K=t.k==="k",dead=t.hp<=0,sc=K?1.4:1,cl=t.side?"#dc2626":"#2563eb";g.save();g.translate(x,y);if(t.fl>0){g.shadowColor="#fff";g.shadowBlur=14}
 g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,18,22*sc,7*sc,0,0,7);g.fill();
 if(dead){g.fillStyle="#57534e";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-18*sc,16);g.lineTo(-10*sc,2);g.lineTo(0,10);g.lineTo(10*sc,0);g.lineTo(18*sc,16);g.fill();g.restore();return}
 g.fillStyle="#a8a29e";g.strokeStyle="#44403c";g.lineWidth=2;g.fillRect(-14*sc,-8*sc,28*sc,26*sc);g.strokeRect(-14*sc,-8*sc,28*sc,26*sc);g.fillStyle="#78716c";for(let i=0;i<3;i++)g.fillRect(-14*sc+i*10*sc,-14*sc,8*sc,7*sc);g.fillStyle=cl;g.fillRect(-14*sc,-8*sc,28*sc,6*sc);g.fillStyle="#1c1917";g.fillRect(-4*sc,4*sc,8*sc,14*sc);
 if(K){g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-10,-24);g.lineTo(-10,-34);g.lineTo(-5,-28);g.lineTo(0,-38);g.lineTo(5,-28);g.lineTo(10,-34);g.lineTo(10,-24);g.fill();if(!t.act)txt("zZz",0,-40,10,"#e5e7eb","center")}

 if(r.ch>=0){txt("¡Ganaste un "+RCH[r.ch].n+"!",400,238,15,RCH[r.ch].col,"center");rdChestIcon(400,284,1.3,RCH[r.ch].col)}else if(r.ch===-2)txt("Cofres llenos: +monedas extra",400,238,13,"#9ca3af","center");

function rdChestIcon(x,y,s,col){g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.scale(s,s);g.fillStyle="#0004";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,16,24,6,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle=col;g.strokeStyle="#111827";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.fillRect(-22,-8,44,24);g.strokeRect(-22,-8,44,24);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-22,-8);g.quadraticCurveTo(0,-30,22,-8);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(-4,-6,8,10);g.strokeRect(-4,-6,8,10);g.fillStyle="#0003";g.fillRect(-22,6,44,3);g.restore()}

 const dk=RD.dk;for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const c=RCI[dk[i]];const x=95+i*80,y=160+Math.sin(mt*2+i)*3;if(c.tp==="spell"){g.fillStyle=c.col+"aa";g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y-14,12,0,7);g.fill()}else if(c.tp==="bld")ryBld(x,y,c.col,c.acc,mt,1.1);else ryUnit(x,y,(c.o.sc||1)*1.6,c.col,c.acc,1,mt+i,true,c.tp==="fly"||c.o.fly)}
 g.fillStyle="#00000055";g.fillRect(0,172,W,2);
 for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const x=40+i*190,y=186,c=RD.ch[i];g.fillStyle="#0e1330";rrect(x,y,176,84,12);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffffff2a";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(x,y,176,84,12);g.stroke();
  if(!c){txt("Cofre vacío",x+88,y+50,12,"#6b7280","center");continue}
  const C=RCH[c.t],left=rdLeft(c),ready=c.st&&left<=0;rdChestIcon(x+34,y+46,1.05,C.col);txt(C.n.replace("Cofre de ","").replace("Cofre ",""),x+110,y+22,12,"#fff","center");
  const anyU=RD.ch.some(q=>q&&q.st&&rdLeft(q)>0);

  else if(c.st){txt(rdFmt(left),x+114,y+46,14,"#fde047","center");const cost=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(Math.sqrt(left/60000)*3));button(x+62,y+54,108,26,"💎 "+cost,()=>{if(RD.gm>=cost){RD.gm-=cost;c.st=1;rdSave()}else{ry.msg="Faltan gemas";ry.mm=1}})}

  txt(info,248,352,11,"#e5e7eb");

function rdChest2(x,y,s,col,op,sh){g.save();g.translate(x+(sh?(Math.random()-.5)*sh:0),y);g.scale(s,s);g.fillStyle="#0005";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,16,26,6,0,0,7);g.fill();
 if(op>0){const rg=g.createRadialGradient(0,-8,2,0,-8,60);rg.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,250,220,"+Math.min(1,op)+")");rg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,250,220,0)");g.fillStyle=rg;g.fillRect(-60,-70,120,80)}
 g.fillStyle=col;g.strokeStyle="#111827";g.lineWidth=2.4;g.fillRect(-22,-8,44,24);g.strokeRect(-22,-8,44,24);g.fillStyle="#0003";g.fillRect(-22,6,44,3);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(-22,-2,44,3);
 if(op>0){g.fillStyle="#fff7d6";g.fillRect(-20,-10,40,4)}
 g.save();g.translate(0,-8-op*22);g.rotate(-op*.35);g.scale(1,1-op*.55);g.fillStyle=col;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-22,0);g.quadraticCurveTo(0,-22,22,0);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(-4,-4,8,9);g.strokeRect(-4,-4,8,9);g.restore();g.restore()}
function ryPart(dt){ry.pp=ry.pp||[];ry.pp.forEach(p=>{p.t-=dt;p.x+=p.vx*dt;p.y+=p.vy*dt;p.vy+=(p.gv||300)*dt;const a=Math.max(0,p.t/p.T);g.globalAlpha=a;g.fillStyle=p.c;if(p.st){g.save();g.translate(p.x,p.y);g.rotate(p.t*6);g.fillRect(-p.s,-p.s/4,p.s*2,p.s/2);g.fillRect(-p.s/4,-p.s,p.s/2,p.s*2);g.restore()}else{g.beginPath();g.arc(p.x,p.y,p.s,0,7);g.fill()}g.globalAlpha=1});ry.pp=ry.pp.filter(p=>p.t>0)}
function ryBurst(x,y,c,n,v){ry.pp=ry.pp||[];for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.28,sp=(v||220)*(.3+Math.random());ry.pp.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*sp,vy:Math.sin(a)*sp-80,t:.9+Math.random()*.6,T:1.5,c,s:2+Math.random()*3,st:Math.random()<.4})}}
function ryRays(x,y,c,r,n,mt,al){g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(mt*.6);g.globalAlpha=al;for(let i=0;i<n;i++){g.rotate(Math.PI*2/n);const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,r,0);gr.addColorStop(0,c);gr.addColorStop(1,"rgba(0,0,0,0)");g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,0);g.lineTo(r,-r*.09);g.lineTo(r,r*.09);g.fill()}g.restore()}

 if(R.i<0){if(R.op==null){const b=Math.abs(Math.sin(mt*4))*8;ryRays(400,250,C.col+"55",260,12,mt,.5);rdChest2(400,250-b,3.4,C.col,0,0);txt(C.n,400,96,24,"#fff","center");txt("TOCA PARA ABRIR",400,380,16,"#fde047","center");btns.push({x:0,y:0,w:W,h:H,fn:()=>{R.op=0;sfx(300,.5,"triangle",.06,300)}})}
  else{R.op+=dt;const o=R.op,sh=o<.7?o*30:0,op=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(o-.7)/.25));if(o>.7&&!R.bb){R.bb=1;ryBurst(400,230,C.col,60,380);ryBurst(400,230,"#fde047",40,300);sfx(900,.4,"triangle",.08,600);sfx(80,.5,"sine",.2,-30)}ryRays(400,240,"rgba(255,240,200,.7)",340*op,16,mt*2,op);rdChest2(400,250,3.4+(o<.7?Math.sin(o*40)*.08:0),C.col,op,sh);if(o>1.15){R.i=0;R.ct=0;R.bf=0}}
  ryPart(dt);return}
 if(R.i<N){R.ct+=dt;const o=res.list[R.i],c=RCI[o.id],rc=RARC[c.rar],e0=Math.min(1,R.ct/.55),e=1-Math.pow(1-e0,3),cx=400,cy=330+(190-330)*e,sc=.35+e*1.55,fl=Math.abs(Math.cos(e0*Math.PI)),front=e0>=.5;
  rdChest2(400,392,1.6,C.col,1,0);g.fillStyle="#000a";rrect(430,350,70,24,12);g.fill();txt("quedan "+(N-R.i-1),465,367,11,"#fff","center");
  if(front&&!R.bf){R.bf=1;ryBurst(cx,cy,rc,30+c.rar*25,260+c.rar*60);sfx(700+c.rar*200,.25,"square",.06,300);if(c.rar>=2)sfx(1400,.6,"triangle",.05,700)}
  if(front){ryRays(cx,cy,rc+"aa",160+c.rar*60,10+c.rar*4,mt,.35+c.rar*.15);if(c.rar===3){const gl=g.createRadialGradient(cx,cy,10,cx,cy,200);gl.addColorStop(0,"rgba(251,191,36,.45)");gl.addColorStop(1,"rgba(251,191,36,0)");g.fillStyle=gl;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)}}
  g.save();g.translate(cx,cy);g.scale(sc*Math.max(.04,fl),sc);if(front)ryCard(-52,-41,104,82,c,rdLv(o.id),{});else{const bg=g.createLinearGradient(-52,-41,52,41);bg.addColorStop(0,"#4c1d95");bg.addColorStop(1,"#1e1b4b");g.fillStyle=bg;rrect(-52,-41,104,82,8);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fde047";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(-52,-41,104,82,8);g.stroke();txt("?",0,10,30,"#fde047","center")}
  if(front&&c.rar>=2){g.globalAlpha=.35+.25*Math.sin(mt*8);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.save();rrect(-52,-41,104,82,8);g.clip();g.translate(((mt*90)%200)-100,0);g.rotate(.4);g.fillRect(-6,-80,12,160);g.restore();g.globalAlpha=1}g.restore();
  if(R.ct>.5){const k=Math.min(o.n,1+Math.floor((R.ct-.5)*o.n*3));txt("x"+k,400,80,28,"#fff","center");txt(c.n,400,108,18,rc,"center");txt(RARN[c.rar],400,128,12,rc,"center");if(o.nw){const p=Math.min(1,(R.ct-.5)*5);g.save();g.translate(560,150);g.rotate(.2);g.scale(p,p);g.fillStyle="#16a34a";rrect(-62,-18,124,34,10);g.fill();txt("¡NUEVA!",0,6,18,"#fff","center");g.restore()}}
  btns.push({x:0,y:0,w:W,h:H,fn:()=>{if(R.ct<.6)R.ct=.6;else{R.i++;R.ct=0;R.bf=0;if(R.i>=N){R.ct=0;ryBurst(400,200,"#fbbf24",80,400);sfx(1046,.5,"triangle",.07,300)}}}});

 R.ct=(R.ct||0)+dt;ryRays(400,210,"rgba(251,191,36,.5)",300,14,mt,.5);txt("¡RECOMPENSAS!",400,90,28,"#fde047","center");const k=Math.min(1,R.ct/1.2);txt("🪙 +"+Math.floor(res.coin*k),400,180,30,"#fbbf24","center");if(res.gm)txt("💎 +"+Math.ceil(res.gm*k),400,226,24,"#38bdf8","center");
 res.list.forEach((o,i)=>{const c=RCI[o.id],x=400+(i-(N-1)/2)*72,y=272,p=Math.min(1,Math.max(0,(R.ct-.2-i*.08)*4));g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.scale(p*.62,p*.62);ryCard(-52,-41,104,82,c,rdLv(o.id),{});g.restore();if(p>=1)txt("x"+o.n,x,y+40,11,"#fff","center")});

 if(ry.page==="battle"&&RB){const dt=Math.min(.05,(now-RB.last)/1000);RB.last=now;rbUpd(dt);rbDraw(mt)}
 else if(ry.page==="home")ryHome(mt);else if(ry.page==="cards")ryCards(mt);else if(ry.page==="shop")ryShop(mt);else if(ry.page==="arenas")ryArenas(mt);else if(ry.page==="reveal")ryReveal(mt);else ryIntro(mt);
 if(ry.mm){ry.mm=0;ry.mu=now+1800}if(ry.mu>now){g.fillStyle="#000c";rrect(280,ry.page==="battle"?330:122,240,28,12);g.fill();txt(ry.msg,400,ry.page==="battle"?349:141,13,"#fde047","center")}}

addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(S!=="roy"||e.key!=="Escape")return;e.stopImmediatePropagation();e.preventDefault();if(ry.page==="battle"&&RB&&!RB.over)RB.pause=RB.pause?0:1;else if(ry.page==="home")S="games";else if(ry.page!=="battle")ry.page="home"},true);

function rbDrawPr(p){const t=p.t;if(!t)return;const q=Math.min(1,p.tr/p.d0),Hc=p.k==="arrow"?.22:p.k==="bomb"?.35:p.k==="ball"?.12:0,h=Math.sin(q*Math.PI)*p.d0*Hc;let dx=t.x-p.x,dy=t.y-8-p.y;const d=Math.hypot(dx,dy)||1;dx/=d;dy/=d;const ang=Math.atan2(dy-Math.cos(q*Math.PI)*Math.PI*Hc,dx),px=p.x,py=p.y-h;
 if(h>2){g.fillStyle="#0003";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(p.x,p.y+6,4,1.6,0,0,7);g.fill()}
 p.tl=p.tl||[];p.tl.push([px,py]);if(p.tl.length>6)p.tl.shift();
 g.save();if(p.k==="arrow"){g.translate(px,py);g.rotate(ang);g.strokeStyle="#7c4a1e";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-10,0);g.lineTo(6,0);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#cbd5e1";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(11,0);g.lineTo(5,-3);g.lineTo(5,3);g.fill();g.fillStyle=p.side?"#ef4444":"#3b82f6";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-10,0);g.lineTo(-14,-3);g.lineTo(-8,0);g.lineTo(-14,3);g.fill()}
 else if(p.k==="laser"){g.translate(px,py);g.rotate(ang);g.shadowColor=p.col;g.shadowBlur=12;g.strokeStyle=p.col;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-26,0);g.lineTo(6,0);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=1.2;g.stroke()}
 else if(p.k==="ball"||p.k==="bomb"){p.tl.forEach((o,i)=>{g.fillStyle="rgba(156,163,175,"+(i/12)+")";g.beginPath();g.arc(o[0],o[1],2+i*.5,0,7);g.fill()});g.fillStyle="#111827";g.beginPath();g.arc(px,py,p.k==="bomb"?5.5:4.5,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ffffff55";g.beginPath();g.arc(px-1.5,py-1.5,1.6,0,7);g.fill();if(p.k==="bomb"){g.fillStyle=Math.random()<.5?"#fde047":"#f97316";g.beginPath();g.arc(px+3,py-6,2,0,7);g.fill()}}
 else{p.tl.forEach((o,i)=>{g.fillStyle=p.col;g.globalAlpha=i/10;g.beginPath();g.arc(o[0],o[1],1.5+i*.6,0,7);g.fill()});g.globalAlpha=1;g.shadowColor=p.col;g.shadowBlur=10;const rg=g.createRadialGradient(px,py,0,px,py,6);rg.addColorStop(0,"#fff");rg.addColorStop(1,p.col);g.fillStyle=rg;g.beginPath();g.arc(px,py,5,0,7);g.fill()}
 g.restore()}
function rbDrawSp(q){const fx=q.st.fx,u=Math.min(1,q.t/q.T),R=q.st.rad;g.save();g.strokeStyle=q.side?"#f87171aa":"#93c5fdaa";g.lineWidth=2;g.setLineDash([6,5]);g.lineDashOffset=-q.t*40;g.beginPath();g.arc(q.x,q.y,R*(1.1-u*.1),0,7);g.stroke();g.setLineDash([]);
 if(fx==="fire"||fx==="dark"){const x=q.x0+(q.x-q.x0)*u,y=q.y0+(q.y-q.y0)*u-Math.sin(u*Math.PI)*90,c1=fx==="fire"?"#f97316":"#7c3aed";for(let i=1;i<7;i++){const v=Math.max(0,u-i*.03),xx=q.x0+(q.x-q.x0)*v,yy=q.y0+(q.y-q.y0)*v-Math.sin(v*Math.PI)*90;g.fillStyle=c1;g.globalAlpha=.5-i*.06;g.beginPath();g.arc(xx,yy,10-i,0,7);g.fill()}g.globalAlpha=1;g.shadowColor=c1;g.shadowBlur=18;const rg=g.createRadialGradient(x,y,1,x,y,12);rg.addColorStop(0,"#fff7d6");rg.addColorStop(.5,fx==="fire"?"#fbbf24":"#c084fc");rg.addColorStop(1,c1);g.fillStyle=rg;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,11,0,7);g.fill()}
 else if(fx==="arrow"){for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const a=i*2.4,rr=R*.85*((i*37)%10)/10,tx=q.x+Math.cos(a)*rr,ty=q.y+Math.sin(a)*rr*.8,d0=i*.02,v=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(q.t-d0)/(q.T-.36)));if(v<=0||v>=1)continue;const y=ty-(1-v)*240,x=tx-(1-v)*40;g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(1.4);g.strokeStyle="#7c4a1e";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,0);g.lineTo(6,0);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#e5e7eb";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(11,0);g.lineTo(5,-3);g.lineTo(5,3);g.fill();g.restore()}}
 else if(fx==="zap"){if(u>.6){g.strokeStyle="#fef08a";g.shadowColor="#facc15";g.shadowBlur=16;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();let x=q.x+(Math.random()-.5)*20,y=0;g.moveTo(x,y);while(y<q.y){y+=18;x+=(Math.random()-.5)*22;g.lineTo(x,Math.min(y,q.y))}g.stroke()}}
 else{const c1=fx==="ice"?"#bae6fd":"#d4a373";g.fillStyle=c1;g.globalAlpha=.25+u*.35;g.beginPath();g.arc(q.x,q.y,R*u,0,7);g.fill();g.globalAlpha=1}
 g.restore()}
