const FB={L:1,R:2,U:4,D:8,P:16,K:32,S:64,X:128},FGY=388,FGW=800;
let FG=null;
function fgNew(ci,side,ai){const C=FCH[ci];return{ci,C,side,x:side?560:240,y:FGY,vx:0,vy:0,f:side?-1:1,hp:C.hp,mx:C.hp,dh:C.hp,sp:0,st:"idle",t:0,mv:null,hit:0,hs:0,inv:0,fl:0,combo:0,wins:0,air:0,pz:null,ai,aim:{cd:0,m:0},pm:0,m:0,armor:0,proj:0,chain:0,kd:0,ph:Math.random()*6}}
function fgMatch(c1,c2,stage,opt){FG=Object.assign(FG||{},{pg:"fight",stg:stage,p:[fgNew(c1,0,0),fgNew(c2,1,opt.ai||0)],pr:[],fx:[],ev:[],rd:1,rt:99*60,ph:"intro",pt:0,hst:0,shk:0,cin:null,slow:0,opt,acc:0,last:performance.now(),res:null,frame:0,cmb:[{n:0,t:0},{n:0,t:0}]});FG.p[0].opp=FG.p[1];FG.p[1].opp=FG.p[0]}
function fgRound(){const A=FG.p[0],B=FG.p[1];[A,B].forEach((p,i)=>{Object.assign(p,{x:i?560:240,y:FGY,vx:0,vy:0,f:i?-1:1,hp:p.mx,dh:p.mx,st:"idle",t:0,mv:null,hit:0,hs:0,inv:0,fl:0,combo:0,air:0,armor:0,kd:0,chain:0})});FG.pr=[];FG.rt=99*60;FG.ph="intro";FG.pt=0;FG.cin=null}
const fgEv=(k,x,y,o)=>{FG.ev.push(Object.assign({k,x,y},o||{}))};
function fgBox(p,m){const s=p.C.sc,x1=p.x+p.f*m.x[0]*s,x2=p.x+p.f*m.x[1]*s;return{x:Math.min(x1,x2),y:p.y+m.y[0]*s,w:Math.abs(x2-x1),h:(m.y[1]-m.y[0])*s}}
function fgHurt(p){const s=p.C.sc,h=(p.st==="crouch"||(p.mv&&(p.mv.id==="CP"||p.mv.id==="CK")))?72:118;return{x:p.x-18*s,y:p.y-h*s,w:36*s,h:h*s}}
const fgOv=(a,b)=>a.x<b.x+b.w&&a.x+a.w>b.x&&a.y<b.y+b.h&&a.y+a.h>b.y;
function fgAtk(p,id){const m=FM[id];p.st="atk";p.t=0;p.mv=Object.assign({id},m);p.hit=0;fgEv("swish",p.x,p.y-80,{v:id==="K"||id==="CK"?1:0})}
function fgSpec(p){const o=p.opp,C=p.C;p.st="spec";p.t=0;p.hit=0;p.mv={id:"S",k:C.sp};fgEv("spec",p.x,p.y-80,{c:C.col})}
function fgSuper(p){p.sp=0;p.st="super";p.t=0;p.hit=0;p.mv={id:"X",k:p.C.su,hits:0};FG.cin={own:p.side,t:46,n:p.C.sn};fgEv("super",p.x,p.y-80,{c:p.C.col})}
function fgProj(p,k){const C=p.C,big=k==="rocket",d=Math.round((big?280:k==="book"?80:90)*C.pw);FG.pr.push({own:p.side,x:p.x+p.f*50*C.sc,y:p.y-(k==="book"?96:78)*C.sc,vx:p.f*(big?9:k==="book"?6:7.2),vy:k==="book"?-5.5:0,g:k==="book"?.32:0,k,d,w:big?70:34,h:big?44:26,life:200,kd:big?1:0,big});p.proj=1}
function fgApply(a,b,dmg,o){o=o||{};if(b.inv>0||b.st==="ko"||b.st==="down")return false;const back=(b.f>0&&(b.m&FB.L))||(b.f<0&&(b.m&FB.R)),crouch=b.m&FB.D,canBlock=b.y>=FGY&&back&&(b.st==="idle"||b.st==="walk"||b.st==="crouch"||b.st==="block")&&!o.unb,lowOk=o.l!=="l"||crouch,highOk=o.l!=="h"||!crouch;
 if(canBlock&&lowOk&&highOk){b.st="block";b.t=0;b.hs=o.h?Math.round(o.h*.7):12;b.vx=-b.f*(o.k||3)*.9;const ch=Math.round(dmg*(o.chip?.18:.02));b.hp=Math.max(1,b.hp-ch);b.sp=Math.min(100,b.sp+4);fgEv("block",(a.x+b.x)/2,b.y-(o.l==="l"?20:80));FG.hst=4;return true}
 if(b.armor>0&&!o.big){b.hp-=Math.round(dmg*.5);b.fl=6;fgEv("hit",(a.x+b.x)/2,b.y-80,{big:0});FG.hst=4;return true}
 const ci=a.side,cm=FG.cmb[ci];if(b.st==="hit"||b.st==="air")cm.n++;else cm.n=1;cm.t=90;const sc=Math.max(.4,1-.12*(cm.n-1)),D=Math.round(dmg*sc);b.hp=Math.max(0,b.hp-D);b.fl=8;a.sp=Math.min(100,a.sp+D/9);b.sp=Math.min(100,b.sp+D/14);
 const big=o.big||D>=90;fgEv("hit",(a.x+b.x)/2+b.f*-6,b.y-(o.l==="l"?20:o.y||84),{big:big?1:0,c:a.C.col,d:D});FG.hst=big?9:6;FG.shk=big?10:5;
 if(b.hp<=0){b.st="ko";b.t=0;b.vy=-9;b.vx=-b.f*5;b.y-=1;FG.slow=90;fgEv("ko",b.x,b.y-60);return true}
 if(o.kd||o.launch||b.y<FGY){b.st="air";b.t=0;b.vy=o.launch?-12:-7;b.vx=-b.f*(o.k||4)*1.2;b.y-=1;b.kd=1}else{b.st="hit";b.t=0;b.hs=o.h||15;b.vx=-b.f*(o.k||3)}
 b.mv=null;return true}
function fgStep(inputs){const F=FG;F.ev=[];F.frame++;
 if(F.ph==="intro"){F.pt++;if(F.pt===1)fgEv("ann",0,0,{s:"ROUND "+F.rd});if(F.pt===70)fgEv("ann",0,0,{s:"¡PELEA!"});if(F.pt>=100){F.ph="fight";F.pt=0}F.p.forEach(p=>{p.t++;p.st="idle"});return}
 if(F.cin){F.cin.t--;if(F.cin.t<=0)F.cin=null;return}
 if(F.hst>0){F.hst--;F.p.forEach(p=>{if(p.fl>0)p.fl--});return}
 if(F.slow>0){F.slow--;if(F.slow%3)return}
 if(F.shk>0)F.shk--;
 F.p.forEach((p,i)=>{p.pm=p.m;p.m=F.ph==="fight"?inputs[i]:0});
 F.p.forEach(p=>fgFighter(p));
 F.pr.forEach(q=>{q.x+=q.vx;q.vy+=q.g;q.y+=q.vy;q.life--;if(q.y>FGY-10&&q.g){q.life=0;fgEv("dust",q.x,FGY)}const t=F.p[q.own?0:1];if(q.life>0&&fgOv({x:q.x-q.w/2,y:q.y-q.h/2,w:q.w,h:q.h},fgHurt(t))){const a=F.p[q.own];if(fgApply(a,t,q.d,{h:18,k:5,chip:1,kd:q.kd,big:q.big,y:80}))q.life=0}if(q.x<-80||q.x>FGW+80)q.life=0;if(q.life<=0)F.p[q.own].proj=0});
 for(let i=0;i<F.pr.length;i++)for(let j=i+1;j<F.pr.length;j++){const a=F.pr[i],b=F.pr[j];if(a.own!==b.own&&a.life>0&&b.life>0&&Math.abs(a.x-b.x)<30&&Math.abs(a.y-b.y)<30){a.life=b.life=0;F.p[a.own].proj=F.p[b.own].proj=0;fgEv("hit",(a.x+b.x)/2,a.y,{big:1,c:"#fff"})}}
 F.pr=F.pr.filter(q=>q.life>0);
 const A=F.p[0],B=F.p[1];if(A.y>=FGY&&B.y>=FGY&&Math.abs(A.x-B.x)<40&&A.st!=="ko"&&B.st!=="ko"){const o=(40-Math.abs(A.x-B.x))/2,d=A.x<B.x?-1:1;A.x+=d*o;B.x-=d*o}
 F.p.forEach(p=>{p.x=Math.max(36,Math.min(FGW-36,p.x))});
 F.cmb.forEach(c=>{if(c.t>0)c.t--});
 if(F.ph==="fight"){F.rt--;const ko=A.hp<=0||B.hp<=0;if(ko||F.rt<=0){F.ph="ko";F.pt=0;let w=A.hp>B.hp?0:B.hp>A.hp?1:-1;F.rw=w;if(w>=0)F.p[w].wins++;if(!ko)fgEv("ann",0,0,{s:"¡TIEMPO!"});else fgEv("ann",0,0,{s:"K.O.!"})}}
 else if(F.ph==="ko"){F.pt++;if(F.pt===110&&F.rw>=0){const w=F.p[F.rw];w.st="win";w.t=0;fgEv("ann",0,0,{s:w.C.n+" GANA"})}if(F.pt>=220){if(A.wins>=2||B.wins>=2){F.ph="end";F.res={w:A.wins>=2?0:1};fgEv("end",0,0)}else{F.rd++;fgRound()}}}}
function fgFighter(p){const o=p.opp,C=p.C,m=p.m,pr=m&~p.pm,F=FG;p.t++;if(p.fl>0)p.fl--;if(p.inv>0)p.inv--;if(p.armor>0)p.armor--;
 const grounded=p.y>=FGY;
 if(p.st==="ko"){p.vy+=.7;p.y+=p.vy;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.y>=FGY){p.y=FGY;p.vy=0;p.vx*=.8}return}
 if(p.st==="win"){return}
 if(F.ph!=="fight"){p.vx*=.8;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.y<FGY){p.vy+=.8;p.y=Math.min(FGY,p.y+p.vy)}if(p.st!=="win"&&p.st!=="ko"&&p.y>=FGY&&p.t>2)p.st="idle";return}
 if(p.st==="hit"||p.st==="block"){p.x+=p.vx;p.vx*=.82;if(--p.hs<=0){p.st="idle";p.t=0}return}
 if(p.st==="air"){p.vy+=.75;p.y+=p.vy;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.y>=FGY){p.y=FGY;p.vy=0;p.st="down";p.t=0;p.vx=0;fgEv("dust",p.x,FGY,{big:1})}return}
 if(p.st==="down"){if(p.t>46){p.st="idle";p.t=0;p.inv=20}return}
 if(p.st!=="atk"&&p.st!=="spec"&&p.st!=="super"&&grounded)p.f=o.x>=p.x?1:-1;
 if(p.st==="atk"){const mv=p.mv,act=p.t>mv.s&&p.t<=mv.s+mv.a;if(act&&!p.hit){const hb=fgBox(p,mv),hu=fgHurt(o);if(fgOv(hb,hu)){p.hit=1;fgApply(p,o,Math.round(mv.d*C.pw),{h:mv.h,k:mv.k,l:mv.l,kd:mv.kd})}}
  if(!grounded){p.vy+=.8;p.y+=p.vy;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.y>=FGY){p.y=FGY;p.vy=0;p.st="idle";p.t=0;p.mv=null;fgEv("dust",p.x,FGY);return}}
  if((mv.id==="P"||mv.id==="P2")&&p.hit&&p.t>mv.s+1){if(pr&FB.K){fgAtk(p,"K");return}if((pr&FB.P)&&mv.id==="P"){fgAtk(p,"P2");return}if(pr&FB.S){fgSpec(p);return}}
  if(mv.id==="K"&&p.hit&&(pr&FB.S)){fgSpec(p);return}
  if(grounded&&p.t>=mv.s+mv.a+mv.r){p.st=(m&FB.D)?"crouch":"idle";p.t=0;p.mv=null}return}
 if(p.st==="spec"){const k=p.mv.k,t=p.t;
  if(k==="wave"||k==="fire"||k==="star"||k==="book"){if(t===12&&!p.proj)fgProj(p,k==="book"?"book":k);if(t>=34){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="charge"){if(t>8&&t<26){p.vx=p.f*9;p.armor=2}else p.vx*=.7;p.x+=p.vx;if(t>8&&t<26&&!p.hit&&fgOv(fgBox(p,{x:[0,58],y:[-110,-30]}),fgHurt(o))){p.hit=1;fgApply(p,o,Math.round(110*C.pw),{h:22,k:9,kd:1})}if(t>=44){p.st="idle";p.t=0;p.vx=0}}
  else if(k==="dash"){if(t>5&&t<18){p.vx=p.f*13}else p.vx*=.6;p.x+=p.vx;if(t>5&&t<20&&!p.hit&&fgOv(fgBox(p,{x:[0,70],y:[-80,-30]}),fgHurt(o))){p.hit=1;fgApply(p,o,Math.round(100*C.pw),{h:20,k:7,kd:1})}if(t>=36){p.st="idle";p.t=0;p.vx=0}}
  else if(k==="tele"){if(t===10){p.x=Math.max(40,Math.min(FGW-40,o.x-o.f*56));p.f=o.x>=p.x?1:-1;fgEv("smoke",p.x,p.y-60)}if(t>=14&&t<20&&!p.hit&&fgOv(fgBox(p,{x:[0,70],y:[-100,-40]}),fgHurt(o))){p.hit=1;fgApply(p,o,Math.round(90*C.pw),{h:20,k:6})}if(t>=38){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="upper"){if(t<7)p.inv=2;if(t===4){p.vy=-12}if(t>3){p.vy+=.8;p.y+=p.vy;p.x+=p.f*2;if(!p.hit&&t<18&&fgOv(fgBox(p,{x:[0,56],y:[-150,-50]}),fgHurt(o))){p.hit=1;fgApply(p,o,Math.round(120*C.pw),{launch:1,k:3,big:1})}if(p.y>=FGY&&t>6){p.y=FGY;p.vy=0;p.st="idle";p.t=0}}}
  return}
 if(p.st==="super"){const k=p.mv.k,t=p.t;
  if(k==="multi"){if(t<40&&!p.hit){p.vx=p.f*14;p.x+=p.vx;if(fgOv(fgBox(p,{x:[0,64],y:[-110,-20]}),fgHurt(o))){p.hit=1;p.mv.hits=0;p.mv.ht=t}}if(p.hit){p.vx=0;const ht=t-p.mv.ht;if(ht%5===0&&p.mv.hits<7){p.mv.hits++;o.x=Math.max(40,Math.min(FGW-40,p.x+p.f*48));fgApply(p,o,Math.round(42*C.pw),{h:12,k:1,unb:1,big:p.mv.hits===7,kd:p.mv.hits===7})}if(ht>40){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}else if(t>=50){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="beam"){if(t>10&&t<52&&t%6===0){const hb={x:p.f>0?p.x+20:0,y:p.y-110*C.sc,w:p.f>0?FGW-p.x:p.x-20,h:64};if(fgOv(hb,fgHurt(o)))fgApply(p,o,Math.round(40*C.pw),{h:10,k:1,chip:1,big:t>=48})}if(t>=64){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="quake"){if(t===16){fgEv("quake",p.x,FGY);if(o.y>=FGY-4)fgApply(p,o,Math.round(260*C.pw),{kd:1,big:1,unb:1,k:6})}if(t>=44){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="rocket"){if(t===14)fgProj(p,"rocket");if(t>=40){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  else if(k==="meteor"){if(t>8&&t<58&&t%8===0){FG.pr.push({own:p.side,x:o.x+(Math.random()-.5)*120,y:-30,vx:0,vy:9,g:.1,k:"star",d:Math.round(48*C.pw),w:30,h:30,life:120});}if(t>=70){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
  return}
 // control libre
 if(!grounded){p.vy+=.8;p.y+=p.vy;p.x+=p.vx;if(p.y>=FGY){p.y=FGY;p.vy=0;p.st="idle";p.t=0;fgEv("dust",p.x,FGY)}if(pr&FB.K){fgAtk(p,"JK");p.vy=p.vy}else if(pr&FB.P)fgAtk(p,"JP");return}
 if((pr&FB.X)&&p.sp>=100){fgSuper(p);return}
 if(pr&FB.S){fgSpec(p);return}
 const crouch=m&FB.D;
 if(pr&FB.P){fgAtk(p,crouch?"CP":"P");return}
 if(pr&FB.K){fgAtk(p,crouch?"CK":"K");return}
 if((pr&FB.U)&&!crouch){p.vy=-15.5*C.jmp;p.vx=((m&FB.R)?1:(m&FB.L)?-1:0)*4.2*C.spd;p.y-=1;p.st="jump";p.t=0;fgEv("jump",p.x,FGY);return}
 if(crouch){p.st="crouch";p.vx=0;return}
 const dir=((m&FB.R)?1:0)-((m&FB.L)?1:0);if(dir){const back=dir!==p.f;p.x+=dir*3.4*C.spd*(back?.75:1);if(p.st!=="walk"){p.st="walk";p.t=0}}else if(p.st!=="idle"){p.st="idle";p.t=0}}
// ---------- IA
function fgAI0(p,lv){const o=p.opp,A=p.aim,d=Math.abs(o.x-p.x),fw=o.x>p.x?FB.R:FB.L,bk=o.x>p.x?FB.L:FB.R,R=Math.random,react=[.25,.45,.65,.85,.95][lv],agg=[.25,.4,.55,.7,.85][lv];
 if(p.st!=="idle"&&p.st!=="walk"&&p.st!=="crouch"&&p.st!=="block"&&p.st!=="jump")return A.m&(FB.L|FB.R|FB.D);
 const threat=(o.st==="atk"||o.st==="spec"||o.st==="super")&&d<180||FG.pr.some(q=>q.own!==p.side&&Math.abs(q.x-p.x)<150);
 if(threat&&R()<react){const low=o.mv&&o.mv.id==="CK";if(o.mv&&o.mv.k==="quake"&&R()<react)return FB.U|fw;A.m=bk|(low?FB.D:0);return A.m}
 if(--A.cd>0)return A.m;A.cd=Math.max(3,Math.round(14-lv*2+R()*8));
 const C=p.C,proj=["wave","fire","star","book"].includes(C.sp);
 if(p.sp>=100&&d<(C.su==="beam"||C.su==="quake"||C.su==="meteor"||C.su==="rocket"?600:160)&&R()<.5+lv*.1){A.m=FB.X;return A.m}
 if(d>230){if(proj&&!p.proj&&R()<.35+lv*.08){A.m=FB.S;return A.m}if(R()<.15*agg){A.m=FB.U|fw;return A.m}A.m=fw;return A.m}
 if(d>95){const r=R();if(r<.18*agg)A.m=FB.U|fw;else if(r<.3&&(C.sp==="dash"||C.sp==="charge"||C.sp==="tele"))A.m=FB.S;else A.m=R()<agg?fw:(R()<.5?bk:0);return A.m}
 const r=R();if(r<agg*.55)A.m=FB.P;else if(r<agg*.8)A.m=FB.K;else if(r<agg*.9)A.m=FB.D|FB.K;else if(r<agg*.97&&(C.sp==="upper"||C.sp==="tele"||C.sp==="dash"))A.m=FB.S;else A.m=R()<.5?bk:FB.D|bk;
 if(o.y<FGY-40&&C.sp==="upper"&&R()<react)A.m=FB.S;return A.m}
function fgAI(p,lv){const r=fgAI0(p,lv);p.aim.m&=~(FB.P|FB.K|FB.S|FB.X|FB.U);return r}
// ---------- dibujo del luchador (esqueleto animado)
const FPZ={idle:{lean:.12,hip:54,aF1:.55,aF2:2.5,aB1:.25,aB2:2.3,lF1:.3,lF2:-.05,lB1:-.35,lB2:.05,hd:0}};
function fgPose(p){const t=p.t,st=p.st,C=p.C,id=p.mv&&p.mv.id,b=Math.sin((FG.frame+p.ph*20)*.12);let P=Object.assign({},FPZ.idle);P.hip+=b*1.5;P.aF2+=b*.06;
 if(st==="walk"){const w=Math.sin(t*.28);P.lF1=.3+w*.45;P.lF2=-.05-Math.max(0,-w)*.6;P.lB1=-.35-w*.45;P.lB2=.05-Math.max(0,w)*.6;P.hip=52+Math.abs(w)*2}
 else if(st==="crouch"){Object.assign(P,{hip:34,lean:.3,lF1:1.2,lF2:-.6,lB1:-.2,lB2:-1.4,aF1:.9,aF2:2.4})}
 else if(st==="jump"||(st==="atk"&&p.y<FGY)){Object.assign(P,{hip:56,lF1:1.1,lF2:-.9,lB1:.5,lB2:-1.2,aF1:1,aF2:2.3});if(id==="JK")Object.assign(P,{lF1:1.35,lF2:1.4,lean:-.2});if(id==="JP")Object.assign(P,{aF1:1.9,aF2:1.9})}
 else if(st==="atk"){const mv=p.mv,ph=t<=mv.s?t/mv.s:t<=mv.s+mv.a?1:Math.max(0,1-(t-mv.s-mv.a)/mv.r),e=ph;
  if(id==="P"||id==="P2"){P.aF1=.55+(1.57-.55)*e;P.aF2=2.5+(1.57-2.5)*e;P.lean=.12+.18*e;if(id==="P2"){P.aB1=.25+1.3*e;P.aB2=2.3-.7*e;P.aF1=.55;P.aF2=2.5}}
  else if(id==="K"){P.lF1=.3+(1.45-.3)*e;P.lF2=-.05+(1.5+.05)*e;P.lean=.12-.45*e;P.lB1=-.2;P.lB2=0;P.hip=56}
  else if(id==="CP"){Object.assign(P,{hip:34,lean:.35,lF1:1.2,lF2:-.6,lB1:-.2,lB2:-1.4});P.aF1=.9+.7*e;P.aF2=2.4-.8*e}
  else if(id==="CK"){Object.assign(P,{hip:28,lean:-.1,lB1:-.3,lB2:-1.5,aF1:.4,aF2:1.8,aB1:-.4,aB2:.2});P.lF1=.6+.95*e;P.lF2=.6+1*e}}
 else if(st==="spec"||st==="super"){const k=p.mv.k;Object.assign(P,{lean:.25,aF1:1.55,aF2:1.55,aB1:1.4,aB2:1.6,lF1:.6,lF2:-.2,lB1:-.6,lB2:.1});if(k==="upper"&&p.t>3)Object.assign(P,{aF1:3,aF2:3.05,lean:.1,lF1:1.2,lF2:-1});if(k==="charge"||k==="dash"||k==="multi")Object.assign(P,{lean:.7,aF1:1.2,aF2:1.9,lF1:1,lF2:-.4,lB1:-.9,lB2:-.2});if(k==="quake")Object.assign(P,p.t<16?{aF1:3,aF2:3,aB1:3,aB2:3,hip:58}:{aF1:.6,aF2:.6,aB1:.6,aB2:.6,hip:34,lean:.6});if(k==="tele"&&p.t<12)P.vanish=1}
 else if(st==="hit"){Object.assign(P,{lean:-.45,aF1:-.6,aF2:-.2,aB1:-.8,aB2:-.4,hd:-.4,lF1:.2,lB1:-.5})}
 else if(st==="block"){Object.assign(P,{lean:-.08,aF1:1.3,aF2:2.8,aB1:1.15,aB2:2.9,hd:.2})}
 else if(st==="air"||st==="ko"){Object.assign(P,{lean:-.8,aF1:-1.2,aF2:-1,aB1:-1.6,aB2:-1.3,lF1:.8,lF2:.2,lB1:.4,lB2:.5,hd:-.5});P.rot=st==="ko"&&p.y>=FGY?-1.45:-.5}
 else if(st==="down"){Object.assign(P,{rot:-1.5,hip:18,lean:-.2,aF1:-1,aF2:-1,aB1:-1.3,aB2:-1,lF1:.5,lF2:0,lB1:.3,lB2:.2});if(p.t>30)P.rot=-1.5+(p.t-30)/16*1.5}
 else if(st==="win"){const w=Math.sin(p.t*.15);Object.assign(P,{lean:-.05,aF1:2.95+w*.1,aF2:3.05,aB1:.4,aB2:1.4,hip:54+Math.abs(w)*6})}
 return P}
function fgLerp(a,b,r){const o={};for(const k in b)o[k]=typeof b[k]==="number"&&typeof a[k]==="number"?a[k]+(b[k]-a[k])*r:b[k];return o}
function fgHead(p,hx,hy,r,f){const C=p.C,a=C.acc,t=FG.frame;
 g.fillStyle=a==="shadow"?"#09090b":"#f8fafc";g.strokeStyle="#0b0b12";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy,r,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();
 const ex=hx+f*r*.45,ey=hy-r*.1;
 if(a==="shadow"){g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.shadowColor="#ef4444";g.shadowBlur=12;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(ex,ey,r*.22,r*.12,0,0,7);g.ellipse(hx-f*r*.05,ey,r*.18,r*.1,0,0,7);g.fill();g.shadowBlur=0;return}
 if(a!=="ninja"&&a!=="glasses"){g.fillStyle="#0b0b12";g.beginPath();g.arc(ex,ey,r*.14,0,7);g.fill()}
 if(a==="band"||a==="band2"){g.fillStyle=a==="band"?"#ef4444":"#facc15";g.fillRect(hx-r,hy-r*.62,r*2,r*.36);g.strokeStyle=g.fillStyle;g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx-f*r,hy-r*.45);g.quadraticCurveTo(hx-f*r*2,hy-r*.3+Math.sin(t*.3)*4,hx-f*r*2.8,hy+Math.sin(t*.25)*6);g.stroke()}
 if(a==="horn"){g.fillStyle="#fde68a";[-1,1].forEach(s=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx+s*r*.5,hy-r*.7);g.lineTo(hx+s*r*.9,hy-r*1.8);g.lineTo(hx+s*r*.1,hy-r*.85);g.fill()});g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.fillRect(hx-r*.9,hy+r*.25,r*1.8,r*.25)}
 if(a==="crown"){g.fillStyle="#facc15";g.strokeStyle="#92400e";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx-r*.8,hy-r*.75);g.lineTo(hx-r*.8,hy-r*1.6);g.lineTo(hx-r*.4,hy-r*1.15);g.lineTo(hx,hy-r*1.75);g.lineTo(hx+r*.4,hy-r*1.15);g.lineTo(hx+r*.8,hy-r*1.6);g.lineTo(hx+r*.8,hy-r*.75);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()}
 if(a==="ninja"){g.fillStyle="#111827";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy,r,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#f8fafc";g.fillRect(hx-r*.2+f*r*.2,hy-r*.3,r*1.1*f>0?r*1.1:r*1.1,r*.28);g.fillStyle="#0b0b12";g.fillRect(ex-1,hy-r*.28,3,r*.24);g.strokeStyle="#dc2626";g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx-f*r*.6,hy+r*.6);g.quadraticCurveTo(hx-f*r*2,hy+r*.4+Math.sin(t*.3)*5,hx-f*r*3.4,hy+r*.9+Math.sin(t*.22)*8);g.stroke()}
 if(a==="glasses"){g.fillStyle="#3b2412";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy-r*.25,r*1.02,Math.PI*1.05,Math.PI*1.95);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#111";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(hx+f*r*.55,hy,r*.32,0,7);g.moveTo(hx-f*r*.05+r*.32,hy);g.arc(hx-f*r*.05,hy,r*.32,0,7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#38bdf855";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx+f*r*.55,hy,r*.3,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#0b0b12";g.fillRect(hx+f*r*.55-1,hy-1,2.4,2.4)}
 if(a==="wizard"){g.fillStyle="#6d28d9";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx-r*1.2,hy-r*.6);g.lineTo(hx-f*r*.6,hy-r*2.9);g.lineTo(hx+r*1.2,hy-r*.6);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx-f*r*.1,hy-r*1.5,r*.2,0,7);g.fill()}
 if(a==="helmet"){g.fillStyle="#6b7280";g.beginPath();g.arc(hx,hy,r*1.06,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillRect(hx-r*1.06,hy-r*.1,r*2.12,r*.4);g.fillStyle="#111";g.fillRect(hx+f*r*.1-(f<0?r*.8:0),hy-r*.2,r*.8,r*.18);g.fillStyle="#dc2626";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hx,hy-r);g.quadraticCurveTo(hx-f*r*1.4,hy-r*2.2,hx-f*r*2,hy-r*1.2);g.lineTo(hx,hy-r*.9);g.fill()}}
function fgDrawP(p){const C=p.C,s=C.sc,f=p.f,T=fgPose(p);p.pz=p.pz?fgLerp(p.pz,T,p.st==="atk"?.55:.3):T;const P=p.pz;if(T.vanish){fgEv&&0;return}
 g.save();g.translate(p.x,p.y);if(P.rot)g.rotate(P.rot*f);g.scale(s,s);
 g.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.35)";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,0,34,7,0,0,7);g.fill();
 if(C.acc==="shadow"||p.st==="super"){g.shadowColor=C.acc==="shadow"?"#7f1d1d":C.col;g.shadowBlur=18}
 const L=(x,y,a,l)=>[x+Math.sin(a)*l*f,y+Math.cos(a)*l],hip=[0,-P.hip],sh=[Math.sin(P.lean)*40*f,-P.hip-Math.cos(P.lean)*40],hd=[sh[0]+Math.sin(P.lean+P.hd)*17*f,sh[1]-Math.cos(P.lean+P.hd)*17];
 const kF=L(hip[0],hip[1],P.lF1,28),fF=L(kF[0],kF[1],P.lF2,28),kB=L(hip[0],hip[1],P.lB1,28),fB=L(kB[0],kB[1],P.lB2,28),eF=L(sh[0],sh[1],P.aF1,23),hF=L(eF[0],eF[1],P.aF2,22),eB=L(sh[0],sh[1],P.aB1,23),hB=L(eB[0],eB[1],P.aB2,22);
 const col=p.fl>0&&p.fl%2?"#ffffff":C.col,dark=p.fl>0&&p.fl%2?"#ffffff":fgShade(C.col),seg=(pts,c,w)=>{g.strokeStyle="#0b0b12";g.lineWidth=w+4;g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(pts[0][0],pts[0][1]);for(let i=1;i<pts.length;i++)g.lineTo(pts[i][0],pts[i][1]);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle=c;g.lineWidth=w;g.stroke()};
 seg([hip,kB,fB],dark,8);seg([sh,eB,hB],dark,7);seg([hip,sh],col,10);seg([hip,kF,fF],col,8);
 fgHead(p,hd[0],hd[1],13,f);seg([sh,eF,hF],col,7);
 g.fillStyle=col;g.strokeStyle="#0b0b12";g.lineWidth=2.5;[hF,hB].forEach(h=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(h[0],h[1],5.5,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke()});
 if(C.acc==="band"&&(p.st==="atk"||p.st==="super")){g.strokeStyle="#cbd5e1";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(hF[0],hF[1]);g.lineTo(hF[0]+f*34,hF[1]-16);g.stroke()}
 g.shadowBlur=0;g.restore()}
function fgShade(c){const n=parseInt(c.slice(1),16),r=(n>>16)*.62|0,gg=((n>>8)&255)*.62|0,b=(n&255)*.62|0;return"rgb("+r+","+gg+","+b+")"}

// ---- STICKMAN FIGHTER: escenarios, efectos, HUD, menús, online
let FGD=null;const fgK={};let fgTP=0;
const fgSave=()=>{try{localStorage.setItem("stk_fgt",JSON.stringify(FGD))}catch(e){}};
function fgLoad(){try{FGD=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("stk_fgt")||"null")}catch(e){FGD=null}if(!FGD){FGD={boss:0,tw:0,wins:0};fgSave()}}
function fgOpen(){fgLoad();FG={pg:"menu",frame:0,fx:[],ev:[],pr:[],p:[],sel:0,mode:"",dif:1,ladder:null,ann:null};S="fgt"}
// ---------- efectos y sonidos de eventos
function fgFxEv(e){const F=FG,P=(x,y,c,n,v,s,k)=>{for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const a=Math.random()*6.28,sp=v*(.3+Math.random());F.fx.push({x,y,vx:Math.cos(a)*sp,vy:Math.sin(a)*sp-(k==="dust"?1:2),t:20+Math.random()*16,T:36,c,s:s*(.6+Math.random()*.6),k:k||"p"})}};
 if(e.k==="hit"){P(e.x,e.y,e.big?"#fde047":"#fff",e.big?26:14,e.big?9:6,e.big?4:3);P(e.x,e.y,e.c||"#fb923c",10,5,3);F.fx.push({k:"ring",x:e.x,y:e.y,t:14,T:14,c:"#fff",r:e.big?60:34});if(e.d)F.fx.push({k:"num",x:e.x,y:e.y-20,t:40,T:40,v:e.d});sfx(e.big?70:130,e.big?.28:.12,e.big?"sawtooth":"square",e.big?.16:.1,-60);sfx(2400,.05,"square",.03,-1800)}
 else if(e.k==="block"){P(e.x,e.y,"#7dd3fc",12,5,3);F.fx.push({k:"ring",x:e.x,y:e.y,t:10,T:10,c:"#7dd3fc",r:30});sfx(1600,.06,"square",.05,-900)}
 else if(e.k==="swish")sfx(e.v?240:360,.07,"triangle",.035,-220);
 else if(e.k==="spec"){P(e.x,e.y,e.c,16,4,3);sfx(420,.22,"sawtooth",.06,500)}
 else if(e.k==="super"){F.flash=12;sfx(180,.9,"sawtooth",.1,900);sfx(90,1,"square",.06,200)}
 else if(e.k==="ko"){F.flash=20;sfx(55,1.4,"sawtooth",.18,-25);sfx(900,.6,"square",.05,-700)}
 else if(e.k==="ann"){F.ann={s:e.s,t:70};sfx(e.s==="¡PELEA!"?660:e.s==="K.O.!"?220:440,.35,"square",.07,e.s==="¡PELEA!"?600:0)}
 else if(e.k==="dust"||e.k==="jump")P(e.x,e.y-4,"rgba(214,211,209,.8)",e.big?14:6,3,5,"dust");
 else if(e.k==="smoke"){P(e.x,e.y,"#475569",20,4,7,"dust");sfx(800,.15,"sine",.05,-600)}
 else if(e.k==="quake"){F.shkV=26;for(let i=0;i<16;i++)F.fx.push({k:"rock",x:Math.random()*FGW,y:FGY,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*3,vy:-6-Math.random()*8,t:50,T:50,c:"#78716c",s:6});sfx(40,1,"sine",.3,-10)}}
// ---------- escenarios
function fgStage(i,fr){const S0=FSTG[i]||FSTG[0],gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,FGY);gr.addColorStop(0,S0.a);gr.addColorStop(1,S0.b);g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);const t=fr/60;
 if(i===0){g.fillStyle="#fef3c7";for(let k=0;k<5;k++){const x=40+k*160;g.fillRect(x,120,120,200);g.strokeStyle="#7c2d12";g.lineWidth=4;g.strokeRect(x,120,120,200);g.lineWidth=2;for(let a=1;a<4;a++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+a*30,120);g.lineTo(x+a*30,320);g.stroke()}for(let b=1;b<5;b++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,120+b*40);g.lineTo(x+120,120+b*40);g.stroke()}}g.fillStyle="#451a03";g.fillRect(0,96,W,24);g.fillRect(0,318,W,12);[150,400,650].forEach((x,k)=>{const sw=Math.sin(t*1.6+k)*.12;g.save();g.translate(x,96);g.rotate(sw);g.strokeStyle="#451a03";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,0);g.lineTo(0,30);g.stroke();const lg=g.createRadialGradient(0,52,4,0,52,40);lg.addColorStop(0,"rgba(254,215,170,.7)");lg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(254,215,170,0)");g.fillStyle=lg;g.fillRect(-40,12,80,80);g.fillStyle="#dc2626";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,52,15,22,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.fillRect(-15,50,30,4);g.restore()});g.fillStyle="#b91c1c";g.fillRect(380,130,40,120);g.fillStyle="#fde68a";g.font="bold 26px serif";g.textAlign="center";g.fillText("武",400,180);g.fillText("道",400,220)}
 else if(i===1){g.fillStyle="#0c0a09";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(120,FGY);g.lineTo(330,90);g.lineTo(470,90);g.lineTo(680,FGY);g.fill();const lg=g.createLinearGradient(0,90,0,200);lg.addColorStop(0,"#f97316");lg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(249,115,22,0)");g.fillStyle=lg;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(330,90);g.lineTo(470,90);g.lineTo(430,200);g.lineTo(380,160);g.fill();for(let k=0;k<30;k++){const x=(k*97+t*20*(k%3+1))%W,y=FGY-((t*40*(1+k%4)+k*53)%FGY);g.fillStyle=k%2?"#fb923c":"#fde047";g.fillRect(x,y,2,2)}g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.globalAlpha=.25+.1*Math.sin(t*3);g.fillRect(0,FGY-30,W,30);g.globalAlpha=1}
 else if(i===2){for(let k=0;k<18;k++){const x=k*48-10,h=80+(k*67)%170;g.fillStyle="#111827";g.fillRect(x,FGY-h-40,44,h+40);for(let y=FGY-h-30;y<FGY-50;y+=14)for(let xx=x+6;xx<x+40;xx+=10)if((xx*3+y+k)%7<3){g.fillStyle=(xx+y)%5?"#fde68a":"#22d3ee";g.fillRect(xx,y,4,6)}}const fl=Math.sin(t*9)>-.7;g.save();g.shadowBlur=20;g.shadowColor="#e879f9";g.strokeStyle=fl?"#e879f9":"#6b21a8";g.lineWidth=4;g.strokeRect(90,110,150,50);g.fillStyle=g.strokeStyle;g.font="bold 24px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillText("FIGHT",165,144);g.shadowColor="#22d3ee";g.strokeStyle="#22d3ee";g.strokeRect(560,90,160,44);g.fillStyle="#22d3ee";g.fillText("STICK",640,121);g.restore();g.strokeStyle="rgba(148,163,184,.35)";g.lineWidth=1;for(let k=0;k<60;k++){const x=(k*53+t*120)%W,y=(k*37+t*600)%H;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y);g.lineTo(x-3,y+12);g.stroke()}}
 else if(i===3){for(let k=0;k<70;k++){g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,"+(.3+.6*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*1.5+k)))+")";g.fillRect((k*113)%W,(k*47)%260,2,2)}const mg=g.createRadialGradient(400,120,20,400,120,140);mg.addColorStop(0,"#fefce8");mg.addColorStop(.4,"rgba(254,249,195,.5)");mg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(254,249,195,0)");g.fillStyle=mg;g.fillRect(260,0,280,280);g.fillStyle="#fefce8";g.beginPath();g.arc(400,120,58,0,7);g.fill();[60,200,600,740].forEach(x=>{g.fillStyle="#4c1d95";g.fillRect(x-22,150,44,FGY-150);g.fillStyle="#5b21b6";g.fillRect(x-30,140,60,14);g.fillRect(x-30,FGY-14,60,14)})}
 else{for(let k=0;k<14;k++){const x=(k*131)%W,y=60+(k*71)%200,o=Math.sin(t*2+k)>.85;if(!o){g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.shadowColor="#ef4444";g.shadowBlur=10;g.fillRect(x,y,8,3);g.fillRect(x+16,y,8,3);g.shadowBlur=0}}g.fillStyle="rgba(127,29,29,"+(.25+.1*Math.sin(t))+")";g.fillRect(0,FGY-60,W,60)}
 const fg2=g.createLinearGradient(0,FGY,0,H);fg2.addColorStop(0,S0.fl);fg2.addColorStop(1,"#000");g.fillStyle=fg2;g.fillRect(0,FGY,W,H-FGY);g.strokeStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.08)";g.lineWidth=1;for(let k=-10;k<=10;k++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(400+k*40,FGY);g.lineTo(400+k*120,H);g.stroke()}g.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.12)";g.fillRect(0,FGY,W,2)}
// ---------- proyectiles y dibujo de pelea
function fgDrawPr(q){g.save();g.translate(q.x,q.y);const t=FG.frame;
 if(q.k==="fire"){for(let i=1;i<6;i++){g.fillStyle="rgba(249,115,22,"+(.5-i*.08)+")";g.beginPath();g.arc(-Math.sign(q.vx)*i*9,Math.sin(t*.5+i)*3,16-i*2,0,7);g.fill()}const rg=g.createRadialGradient(0,0,2,0,0,20);rg.addColorStop(0,"#fff7d6");rg.addColorStop(.5,"#fbbf24");rg.addColorStop(1,"#ef4444");g.fillStyle=rg;g.shadowColor="#f97316";g.shadowBlur=20;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,17,0,7);g.fill()}
 else if(q.k==="wave"){g.scale(Math.sign(q.vx)||1,1);g.shadowColor="#38bdf8";g.shadowBlur=16;g.strokeStyle="#e0f2fe";g.lineWidth=6;g.beginPath();g.arc(-10,0,22,-1.2,1.2);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#38bdf8";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(-16,0,18,-1.1,1.1);g.stroke()}
 else if(q.k==="star"){g.rotate(t*.3);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.shadowColor="#fde047";g.shadowBlur=16;g.beginPath();for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const r=i%2?7:17,a=i*Math.PI/5;g.lineTo(Math.cos(a)*r,Math.sin(a)*r)}g.fill()}
 else if(q.k==="book"){g.rotate(t*.35);g.fillStyle="#2563eb";g.fillRect(-13,-10,26,20);g.fillStyle="#f8fafc";g.fillRect(-11,-8,22,3);g.fillRect(-11,5,22,3);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(-2,-10,4,20)}
 else if(q.k==="rocket"){g.scale(Math.sign(q.vx)||1,1);for(let i=0;i<6;i++){g.fillStyle=i%2?"#fde047":"#f97316";g.beginPath();g.arc(-40-i*10,Math.sin(t+i)*4,10-i,0,7);g.fill()}g.fillStyle="#e5e7eb";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-32,-16);g.lineTo(18,-16);g.quadraticCurveTo(40,0,18,16);g.lineTo(-32,16);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.fillRect(-32,-22,12,8);g.fillRect(-32,14,12,8);g.fillStyle="#38bdf8";g.beginPath();g.arc(6,0,6,0,7);g.fill()}
 g.restore()}
function fgSuperFx(p){if(p.st!=="super")return;const k=p.mv.k,t=p.t,C=p.C;
 if(k==="beam"&&t>10&&t<56){const w=1-Math.abs((t-33)/23),y=p.y-110*C.sc+32,x0=p.x+p.f*40;g.save();g.shadowColor=C.col;g.shadowBlur=30;const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,y-34*w,0,y+34*w);gr.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,0)");gr.addColorStop(.3,C.col);gr.addColorStop(.5,"#fff");gr.addColorStop(.7,C.col);gr.addColorStop(1,"rgba(255,255,255,0)");g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(p.f>0?x0:0,y-34*w,p.f>0?W-x0:x0,68*w);g.restore()}
 if((k==="multi")&&t<50){g.save();g.globalAlpha=.25;for(let i=1;i<4;i++){const ox=p.x-p.f*i*24;g.translate(ox-p.x,0);fgDrawP(p);g.translate(p.x-ox,0)}g.restore()}}
function fgHUD(){const F=FG;F.p.forEach((p,i)=>{const L=i===0,x=L?20:450,w=330;p.dh+=(p.hp-p.dh)*.06;if(p.dh<p.hp)p.dh=p.hp;

function fgLocal(){let m=0;const k=fgK;if(k.a||k.arrowleft||inp.l)m|=FB.L;if(k.d||k.arrowright||inp.r)m|=FB.R;if(k.w||k.arrowup||k[" "]||inp.j)m|=FB.U;if(k.s||k.arrowdown)m|=FB.D;if(k.j||inp.at)m|=FB.P;if(k.k)m|=FB.K;if(k.l)m|=FB.S;if(k.i||k.u)m|=FB.X;m|=fgTP;return m}

 const C=FCH[FG.sel];g.fillStyle="#0b1020cc";rrect(14,54,272,330,14);g.fill();fgMini(FG.sel,150,300,1.35,"idle",1);txt(C.n,150,84,22,C.col==="#18181b"?"#ef4444":C.col,"center");txt(C.d,150,104,10,"#e5e7eb","center");
 [["VIDA",C.hp/1300],["FUERZA",C.pw/1.25],["VELOCIDAD",C.spd/1.3]].forEach((s,i)=>{txt(s[0],30,330+i*18,10,"#cbd5e1");g.fillStyle="#334155";g.fillRect(110,322+i*18,160,8);g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.fillRect(110,322+i*18,160*s[1],8)});txt("Súper: "+C.sn,150,384,10,"#fde047","center");

 button(14,396,130,36,"VOLVER",()=>{if(FG.mode==="online")fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"});button(150,396,136,36,FG.mode==="online"?(FG.net&&FG.net.myPick!=null?"ESPERANDO…":"¡LISTO!"):"¡PELEAR!",ok,true,FG.mode==="online"&&FG.net&&FG.net.myPick!=null);

"+(i===L.l.length-1&&!done&&!cur?"???":FCH[c].n),x-106,y-4,13,cur?"#fde047":done?"#86efac":"#cbd5e1");if(done)txt("✓",x+124,y-4,14,"#4ade80","right")});

 if(won&&!F.net)txt("+"+(F.opt.tower?60:40)+" monedas"+(F.towerDone?"  ·  ¡TORRE COMPLETA! +300 y SOMBRA desbloqueado":""),400,190,12,"#fbbf24","center");
 if(F.net){if(F.net.lost){txt("Tu amigo se desconectó",400,240,14,"#f87171","center");button(300,300,200,40,"MENÚ",()=>{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"});return}button(200,300,190,42,F.net.again?"ESPERANDO…":"REVANCHA",()=>{F.net.again=1;fgSend({t:"again"});if(F.net.role==="host"&&F.net.oagain)fgNetStart()},true);button(410,300,190,42,"SALIR",()=>{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"});if(F.net.oagain)txt("Tu amigo quiere revancha",400,270,12,"#4ade80","center");return}
 if(F.opt.tower){if(F.towerDone)button(300,300,200,42,"MENÚ",()=>{FG.pg="menu"},true);else if(won)button(300,300,200,42,"SIGUIENTE",()=>{FG.pg="ladder"},true);else{button(200,300,190,42,"REINTENTAR",()=>{const L=FG.ladder,o=L.l[L.i];fgMatch(L.me,o,o===8?4:L.i%4,F.opt)},true);button(410,300,190,42,"MENÚ",()=>{FG.pg="menu"})}return}
 button(200,300,190,42,"REVANCHA",()=>{fgMatch(F.p[0].ci,F.p[1].ci,Math.floor(Math.random()*4),F.opt)},true);button(410,300,190,42,"MENÚ",()=>{FG.pg="menu"})}
// ---------- online P2P (WebRTC, códigos copiados)
const FICE={iceServers:[{urls:["stun:stun.l.google.com:19302","stun:stun1.l.google.com:19302","stun:stun.cloudflare.com:3478"]}]};
async function fgEnc(d){const s=JSON.stringify({t:d.type,s:d.sdp});try{if(window.CompressionStream){const cs=new Blob([s]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream("deflate-raw"));const b=new Uint8Array(await new Response(cs).arrayBuffer());let bin="";b.forEach(x=>bin+=String.fromCharCode(x));return"SF1."+btoa(bin)}}catch(e){}return"SF0."+btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(s)))}
async function fgDec(c){c=(c||"").trim().replace(/\s+/g,"");let s;if(c.startsWith("SF1.")){const bin=atob(c.slice(4)),b=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)b[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);s=await new Response(new Blob([b]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream("deflate-raw"))).text()}else if(c.startsWith("SF0."))s=decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(c.slice(4))));else throw new Error("código inválido");const o=JSON.parse(s);return{type:o.t,sdp:o.s}}
const fgIce=pc=>new Promise(r=>{if(pc.iceGatheringState==="complete")return r();const f=()=>{if(pc.iceGatheringState==="complete")r()};pc.addEventListener("icegatheringstatechange",f);setTimeout(r,3500)});
function fgSend(o){const N=FG.net;if(!N)return;const ch=o.t==="s"||o.t==="i"?N.fast:N.ctl;try{if(ch&&ch.readyState==="open")ch.send(JSON.stringify(o))}catch(e){}}
function fgNetWire(){const N=FG.net,on=e=>{let o;try{o=JSON.parse(e.data)}catch(x){return}fgNetMsg(o)};[N.ctl,N.fast].forEach(c=>{if(!c)return;c.onmessage=on});if(N.ctl){N.ctl.onopen=()=>{N.ok=1;fgUIHide();FG.pg="sel";N.myPick=null;N.opPick=null;sfx(880,.3,"triangle",.07,300)};N.ctl.onclose=()=>{N.lost=1;if(FG.pg!=="fight")FG.pg="online",fgNetUI("lost")}}}
function fgNetMsg(o){const N=FG.net,F=FG;if(!N)return;
 if(o.t==="pick"){N.opPick=o.c;if(N.role==="host"&&N.myPick!=null)fgNetStart()}
 else if(o.t==="start"){fgMatch(o.c1,o.c2,o.st,{online:1});N.again=0;N.oagain=0}
 else if(o.t==="i"){N.rin=o.m}
 else if(o.t==="again"){N.oagain=1;if(N.role==="host"&&N.again)fgNetStart()}
 else if(o.t==="s"&&N.role==="guest"&&F.pg==="fight"){fgApplySnap(o.d)}}
function fgNetPick(c){const N=FG.net;N.myPick=c;fgSend({t:"pick",c});if(N.role==="host"&&N.opPick!=null)fgNetStart()}
function fgNetStart(){const N=FG.net,st=Math.floor(Math.random()*4);const m={t:"start",c1:N.myPick,c2:N.opPick,st};fgSend(m);fgMatch(m.c1,m.c2,st,{online:1});N.again=0;N.oagain=0}
async function fgHost(){const N=FG.net={role:"host",rin:0};try{N.pc=new RTCPeerConnection(FICE);N.ctl=N.pc.createDataChannel("ctl");N.fast=N.pc.createDataChannel("st",{ordered:false,maxRetransmits:0});fgNetWire();const of=await N.pc.createOffer();await N.pc.setLocalDescription(of);fgNetUI("hostwait");await fgIce(N.pc);N.code=await fgEnc(N.pc.localDescription);fgNetUI("hostcode")}catch(e){N.err=""+e;fgNetUI("err")}}
async function fgHostAns(c){const N=FG.net;try{await N.pc.setRemoteDescription(await fgDec(c));fgNetUI("connecting")}catch(e){N.err="Código de respuesta inválido";fgNetUI("hostcode")}}
async function fgJoin(c){const N=FG.net={role:"guest",rin:0};try{N.pc=new RTCPeerConnection(FICE);N.pc.ondatachannel=e=>{if(e.channel.label==="ctl")N.ctl=e.channel;else N.fast=e.channel;fgNetWire();if(e.channel.label==="ctl"&&e.channel.readyState==="open")e.channel.onopen()};await N.pc.setRemoteDescription(await fgDec(c));const a=await N.pc.createAnswer();await N.pc.setLocalDescription(a);fgNetUI("guestwait");await fgIce(N.pc);N.code=await fgEnc(N.pc.localDescription);fgNetUI("guestcode")}catch(e){FG.net={role:"guest",err:"Código inválido. Revisa que lo copiaste completo."};fgNetUI("join")}}
function fgNetClose(){const N=FG.net;if(N){try{N.ctl&&N.ctl.close();N.pc&&N.pc.close()}catch(e){}}FG.net=null;fgUIHide()}
// snapshots
function fgSnap(){const F=FG;return{f:F.frame,ph:F.ph,pt:F.pt,rd:F.rd,rt:F.rt,cin:F.cin,shk:F.shk,res:F.res,cm:F.cmb.map(c=>[c.n,c.t]),p:F.p.map(p=>[Math.round(p.x),Math.round(p.y),p.f,p.hp,Math.round(p.sp),p.st,p.t,p.mv?p.mv.id:"",p.mv?p.mv.k||"":"",p.fl,p.wins]),pr:F.pr.map(q=>[Math.round(q.x),Math.round(q.y),q.k,q.vx,q.own]),ev:F.evq||[]}}
function fgApplySnap(d){const F=FG;F.frame=d.f;F.ph=d.ph;F.pt=d.pt;F.rd=d.rd;F.rt=d.rt;F.cin=d.cin;F.shk=d.shk;F.res=d.res;d.cm.forEach((c,i)=>{F.cmb[i].n=c[0];F.cmb[i].t=c[1]});d.p.forEach((a,i)=>{const p=F.p[i];p.x=a[0];p.y=a[1];p.f=a[2];p.hp=a[3];p.sp=a[4];if(p.st!==a[5])p.pz=p.pz;p.st=a[5];p.t=a[6];p.mv=a[7]?Object.assign({id:a[7],k:a[8]},FM[a[7]]||{}):null;p.fl=a[9];p.wins=a[10]});F.pr=d.pr.map(a=>({x:a[0],y:a[1],k:a[2],vx:a[3],own:a[4]}));(d.ev||[]).forEach(fgFxEv)}
// panel DOM para códigos
function fgUIEl(){let d=document.getElementById("fgo");if(!d){d=document.createElement("div");d.id="fgo";d.style.cssText="position:fixed;z-index:60;background:#0f172af2;border:2px solid #fde047;border-radius:14px;padding:14px;color:#fff;font:14px system-ui,sans-serif;box-shadow:0 10px 40px #000a;display:none;box-sizing:border-box";document.body.appendChild(d);["keydown","keyup"].forEach(ev=>d.addEventListener(ev,e=>e.stopPropagation()))}return d}
function fgUIHide(){const d=document.getElementById("fgo");if(d)d.style.display="none"}
function fgUIPos(){const d=fgUIEl(),r=cv.getBoundingClientRect(),w=Math.min(r.width-20,560);d.style.width=w+"px";d.style.left=(r.left+(r.width-w)/2)+"px";d.style.top=(r.top+r.height*.2)+"px";d.style.maxHeight=(r.height*.78)+"px";d.style.overflow="auto"}
function fgNetUI(step){const N=FG.net||{},d=fgUIEl();fgUIPos();d.style.display="block";const B=(id,t,c)=>'<button id="'+id+'" style="margin:6px 6px 0 0;padding:9px 14px;border:0;border-radius:10px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;background:'+(c||"#7c3aed")+';color:#fff">'+t+'</button>',TA=(id,ro,v)=>'<textarea id="'+id+'" '+(ro?"readonly":"")+' style="width:100%;height:76px;margin-top:6px;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #475569;background:#020617;color:#a5f3fc;font:11px monospace;box-sizing:border-box">'+(v||"")+'</textarea>',H=s=>'<div style="font-weight:800;font-size:16px;color:#fde047;margin-bottom:6px">'+s+'</div>',P=s=>'<div style="margin-top:8px;color:#cbd5e1">'+s+'</div>';
 let h="";if(step==="start")h=H("🌐 Online con amigos")+P("Uno de los dos crea la sala y el otro se une. Se pasan dos códigos (por WhatsApp, por ejemplo) y listo.")+'<div style="margin-top:10px">'+B("fgc","CREAR SALA")+B("fgj","UNIRME","#0ea5e9")+B("fgx","CANCELAR","#475569")+"</div>"+P('<small>Necesitan internet. En algunas redes muy cerradas (colegio, ciertos datos móviles) puede no conectar.</small>');
 else if(step==="hostwait"||step==="guestwait")h=H("Preparando código…")+P("Un momento ⏳");
 else if(step==="hostcode")h=H("Sala creada ✅")+P("<b>1.</b> Copia este código y mándaselo a tu amigo:")+TA("fgt1",1,N.code)+B("fgcp","📋 COPIAR")+P("<b>2.</b> Pega aquí el código de RESPUESTA que te mande:")+TA("fgt2",0)+'<div>'+B("fgok","CONECTAR","#16a34a")+B("fgx","CANCELAR","#475569")+"</div>"+(N.err?P('<span style="color:#f87171">'+N.err+"</span>"):"");
 else if(step==="join")h=H("Unirme a una sala")+P("Pega el código que te mandó tu amigo:")+TA("fgt2",0)+'<div>'+B("fgok2","GENERAR RESPUESTA","#16a34a")+B("fgx","CANCELAR","#475569")+"</div>"+(N.err?P('<span style="color:#f87171">'+N.err+"</span>"):"");
 else if(step==="guestcode")h=H("Respuesta lista ✅")+P("Copia esta respuesta y mándasela a tu amigo. Cuando la pegue, se conectan solos:")+TA("fgt1",1,N.code)+B("fgcp","📋 COPIAR")+B("fgx","CANCELAR","#475569")+P("Esperando conexión… ⏳");
 else if(step==="connecting")h=H("Conectando… ⏳")+P("Si tarda más de 20 segundos, puede que sus redes no permitan conexión directa. Prueben con otra red.")+B("fgx","CANCELAR","#475569");
 else if(step==="lost")h=H("Conexión perdida")+P("Tu amigo se desconectó.")+B("fgx","VOLVER","#475569");
 else if(step==="err")h=H("No se pudo crear la sala")+P(N.err||"")+B("fgx","VOLVER","#475569");
 d.innerHTML=h;const $$=id=>document.getElementById(id);
 if($$("fgc"))$$("fgc").onclick=()=>fgHost();if($$("fgj"))$$("fgj").onclick=()=>{FG.net={role:"guest"};fgNetUI("join")};if($$("fgx"))$$("fgx").onclick=()=>{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"};
 if($$("fgcp"))$$("fgcp").onclick=()=>{const t=$$("fgt1");t.select();try{navigator.clipboard.writeText(t.value).then(()=>{$$("fgcp").textContent="✅ COPIADO"},()=>{document.execCommand("copy");$$("fgcp").textContent="✅ COPIADO"})}catch(e){document.execCommand("copy")}};
 if($$("fgok"))$$("fgok").onclick=()=>fgHostAns($$("fgt2").value);if($$("fgok2"))$$("fgok2").onclick=()=>fgJoin($$("fgt2").value);
 if(FG.net&&FG.net.pc&&step==="connecting"){const pc=FG.net.pc;pc.onconnectionstatechange=()=>{if(pc.connectionState==="failed"&&FG.net){FG.net.err="No se pudo conectar. Prueben otra red o creen una sala nueva.";fgNetUI("err")}}}}
// ---------- bucle principal
function drawFgt(mt){const F=FG;F.frame++;if(F.pg!=="fight"&&F.pg!=="online")fgUIHide();
 if(F.pg==="menu")fgMenu();else if(F.pg==="ctl")fgCtl();else if(F.pg==="sel")fgSel();else if(F.pg==="ladder")fgLadder();else if(F.pg==="online"){fgBG();txt("ONLINE CON AMIGOS",400,60,24,"#fde047","center");fgMini(3,140,380,1.2,"idle",1);fgMini(6,660,380,1.2,"idle",-1);if(!document.getElementById("fgo")||document.getElementById("fgo").style.display==="none")button(300,380,200,36,"VOLVER",()=>{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"});else fgUIPos()}
 else if(F.pg==="fight"){F.frame--;const now=performance.now();let dt=Math.min(.1,(now-F.last)/1000);F.last=now;const N=F.net;
  if(N&&N.role==="guest"){fgSend({t:"i",m:fgLocal()});if(fgTPt>0&&--fgTPt<=0)fgTP=0}
  else if(!F.pause){F.acc+=dt;let n=0;while(F.acc>=1/60&&n<4){F.acc-=1/60;n++;const i0=fgLocal();if(fgTPt>0&&--fgTPt<=0)fgTP=0;const i1=N?N.rin||0:fgAI(F.p[1],F.opt.lv||0);fgStep([i0,i1]);F.ev.forEach(fgFxEv);if(N){F.evq=F.ev;fgSend({t:"s",d:fgSnap()});F.evq=[]}}}
  fgRender();
  if(F.ph==="end"&&F.res){fgEndUI();return}
  if(!window.__vid)[["K",FB.K,"#2563eb"],["★",FB.S,"#7c3aed"],["SÚPER",FB.X,"#dc2626"]].forEach((b,i)=>{const x=W-226+i*74,y=H-58;g.globalAlpha=.75;g.fillStyle=b[2];rrect(x,y,66,40,10);g.fill();g.globalAlpha=1;txt(b[0],x+33,y+26,b[0].length>2?12:18,"#fff","center");hb(x,y,66,40,()=>fgPulse(b[1]))});
  if(!N&&!window.__vid)button(W-66,74,56,26,"II",()=>{F.pause=1});
  if(F.pause){g.fillStyle="#000b";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);txt("PAUSA",400,140,36,"#fff","center");button(300,170,200,44,"▶ CONTINUAR",()=>{F.pause=0;F.last=performance.now()});button(300,224,200,44,"✕ SALIR",()=>{F.pause=0;FG.pg="menu"})}
  if(N&&N.lost){g.fillStyle="#000b";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);txt("Tu amigo se desconectó",400,190,22,"#f87171","center");button(300,230,200,42,"MENÚ",()=>{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"})}}}
addEventListener("keydown",e=>{if(S!=="fgt")return;const tg=e.target;if(tg&&(tg.tagName==="TEXTAREA"||tg.tagName==="INPUT"))return;const k=e.key.toLowerCase();e.stopImmediatePropagation();if(k==="escape"){if(FG.pg==="fight"&&!FG.net&&FG.ph!=="end"){FG.pause=FG.pause?0:1;FG.last=performance.now()}else if(FG.pg!=="fight"){if(FG.pg==="menu")S="games";else{fgNetClose();FG.pg="menu"}}return}if(FG.pause&&k==="enter"){FG.pause=0;FG.last=performance.now();return}fgK[k]=1;if([" ","arrowup","arrowdown","arrowleft","arrowright"].includes(k))e.preventDefault()},true);
addEventListener("keyup",e=>{fgK[e.key.toLowerCase()]=0});addEventListener("blur",()=>{for(const k in fgK)fgK[k]=0});

  g.fillStyle="#fff";g.strokeStyle="#6b7280";g.lineWidth=1.6;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,-37,8,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillRect(-5,-32,10,4);g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-3.2,-38,2.4,3,0,0,7);g.ellipse(3.2,-38,2.4,3,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ef4444";g.shadowColor="#ef4444";g.shadowBlur=5;g.fillRect(-3.8,-38.6,1.4,1.4);g.fillRect(2.6,-38.6,1.4,1.4);g.shadowBlur=0}
 else if(e.t===9){ // calabaza saltarina
  const sq=clamp(-e.vy/800,-.25,.3);g.scale(1-sq*.5,1+sq);const gr=g.createRadialGradient(-5,-18,2,0,-12,17);gr.addColorStop(0,"#fdba74");gr.addColorStop(1,"#c2410c");g.fillStyle=gr;g.strokeStyle="#7c2d12";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,-13,17,13,0,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-8,-13,6,12.4,0,0,7);g.ellipse(8,-13,6,12.4,0,0,7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#166534";g.fillRect(-2,-29,4,6);g.fillStyle="#22c55e";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(2,-27);g.quadraticCurveTo(10,-30,9,-22);g.quadraticCurveTo(5,-25,2,-24);g.fill();
  g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.shadowColor="#fde047";g.shadowBlur=8;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-10,-14);g.lineTo(-4,-14);g.lineTo(-7,-20);g.moveTo(4,-14);g.lineTo(10,-14);g.lineTo(7,-20);g.moveTo(-9,-8);g.lineTo(-6,-5);g.lineTo(-3,-8);g.lineTo(0,-5);g.lineTo(3,-8);g.lineTo(6,-5);g.lineTo(9,-8);g.lineTo(6,-10);g.lineTo(0,-8);g.lineTo(-6,-10);g.closePath();g.fill();g.shadowBlur=0}
 else if(e.t===10){ // escarabajo blindado
  g.strokeStyle="#450a0a";g.lineWidth=2.4;for(let i=-1;i<=1;i++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(i*7,-6);g.lineTo(i*9+Math.sin(t*12+i)*3,0);g.stroke()}
  const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,-24,0,-4);gr.addColorStop(0,"#ef4444");gr.addColorStop(1,"#7f1d1d");g.fillStyle=gr;g.strokeStyle="#450a0a";g.lineWidth=2.2;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(0,-11,17,12,0,Math.PI,0);g.lineTo(17,-6);g.lineTo(-17,-6);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-23);g.lineTo(0,-6);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#d1d5db";[-12,-5,5,12].forEach((a,i)=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a-3,-(i%3?15:13));g.lineTo(a,-(i%3?24:22)-3);g.lineTo(a+3,-(i%3?15:13));g.fill();g.stroke()});
  g.fillStyle="#1f2937";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-16,-8,6,5,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(-17,-9,2,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#7f1d1d";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-20,-6);g.lineTo(-26,-1);g.moveTo(-20,-10);g.lineTo(-26,-13);g.stroke()}
 else{ // cañón calavera
  g.fillStyle="#44403c";g.strokeStyle="#1c1917";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(-11,-5,5,0,7);g.arc(11,-5,5,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#57534e";g.fillRect(-16,-14,32,9);g.strokeRect(-16,-14,32,9);
  g.save();g.translate(0,-22);g.rotate(clamp((p.y-(y-14))/180,-.5,.6));g.fillStyle="#292524";g.fillRect(2,-5,22,10);g.strokeRect(2,-5,22,10);g.fillStyle="#fb923c";if(Math.sin(t*9)>.3){g.shadowColor="#f97316";g.shadowBlur=9;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(24,-3);g.lineTo(30+Math.random()*3,0);g.lineTo(24,3);g.fill();g.shadowBlur=0}g.restore();
  g.fillStyle="#f5f5f4";g.beginPath();g.arc(-2,-22,10,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.fillRect(-7,-16,10,5);g.fillStyle="#111";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(-6,-23,2.6,3.2,0,0,7);g.ellipse(2,-23,2.6,3.2,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.fillRect(-6.8,-23.6,1.6,1.6);g.fillRect(1.2,-23.6,1.6,1.6)}
 g.restore()};

// ---- niveles más difíciles: conversión a monstruos nuevos + más enemigos
function monsHard(){if(lvl===99||dif===3||typeof AR==="undefined")return;const n=gi();if(n<1)return;
 const lim=(AR-2)*T,pc=Math.min(.7,.12+n*.03);
 enemies.forEach(e=>{if(e.gone||e.x>lim||e.x<12*T||R()>pc)return;const m={0:[8,10],1:[9,9],2:[7,7],3:[10,10]}[e.t];if(!m)return;const k=m[R()<.5?0:1],hh=[0,0,0,0,0,0,0,24,34,26,22][k],ww=[0,0,0,0,0,0,0,28,24,30,34][k];e.t=k;e.h=hh;e.w=ww;e.y=k===7?12*T-100:12*T-hh;e.y0=12*T-100;if(k===7)e.vx=-50;e.ph=e.ph||0});
 const pool=n<4?[8,9,7,0,1]:n<8?[8,9,7,10,2,4]:[8,9,7,10,11,2,4,11];let extra=Math.min(28,Math.floor(3+n*1.1)),tries=0,i=300;
 while(extra>0&&tries++<400){const c=16+Math.floor(R()*(AR-24));if(!(G[12][c]===1&&G[12][c-1]===1&&G[12][c+1]===1&&!G[11][c]&&!G[11][c-1]&&!G[11][c+1]))continue;if(enemies.some(e=>!e.gone&&Math.abs(e.x/T-c)<3))continue;mkE(c,pool[Math.floor(R()*pool.length)],i++);extra--}}

CHARS.push({id:"joaquin",n:"JOAQUÍN",t:"Chiquito y con lentes",ab:"Pequeño genio: +15% velocidad y +12% salto",c:450,ex:1,hint:"Disponible en la tienda"});
UPD.push({k:"xj",n:"Botas de resorte",d:"+10% de salto",max:3,cost:[60,100,150]},{k:"xs",n:"Zapatillas pro",d:"+8% de velocidad",max:3,cost:[80,130,190]},{k:"xw",n:"Filo de dragón",d:"+10% de alcance",max:3,cost:[90,140,200]},{k:"xh",n:"Armadura ligera",d:"+1 corazón extra",max:2,cost:[200,350]},{k:"xl",n:"Amuleto de vida",d:"+1 vida extra al empezar",max:2,cost:[100,200]},{k:"xp",n:"Escudo del héroe",d:"Empiezas con 3 s de escudo",max:1,cost:[120]});

// ---- jefes: render realista (luz, volumen, textura, borde de luz, aura y sombra de contacto)
const BOB=document.createElement("canvas");BOB.width=520;BOB.height=420;const BOX=BOB.getContext("2d");
const BTM=document.createElement("canvas");BTM.width=520;BTM.height=420;const BTX=BTM.getContext("2d");
const BNZ=(()=>{const c=document.createElement("canvas");c.width=c.height=96;const x=c.getContext("2d"),im=x.createImageData(96,96);for(let i=0;i<96*96;i++){const v=Math.random()<.5?0:255;im.data[i*4]=im.data[i*4+1]=im.data[i*4+2]=v;im.data[i*4+3]=Math.random()*55}x.putImageData(im,0,0);return c})();
const _drawBoss0=drawBoss;
drawBoss=function(){const b=boss;if(!b.active&&!b.dead&&b.x-cam>W+120)return;if(b.dead&&wonT>0)return;
 const sx=b.x+b.w/2-cam,sy=b.y+b.h;if(sx<-260||sx>W+260)return;
 BOX.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);BOX.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";BOX.globalAlpha=1;BOX.shadowBlur=0;BOX.clearRect(0,0,520,420);BOX.setTransform(1,0,0,1,260-sx,360-sy);
 const gs=g;g=BOX;try{_drawBoss0()}finally{g=gs}
 BOX.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);BOX.shadowBlur=0;BOX.globalAlpha=1;BOX.globalCompositeOperation="source-atop";
 const L=BOX.createLinearGradient(0,190,0,362);L.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,.2)");L.addColorStop(.45,"rgba(255,255,255,0)");L.addColorStop(1,"rgba(0,0,0,.45)");BOX.fillStyle=L;BOX.fillRect(0,0,520,420);
 const Rg=BOX.createRadialGradient(228,250,8,262,270,135);Rg.addColorStop(0,"rgba(255,255,255,.16)");Rg.addColorStop(.55,"rgba(0,0,0,0)");Rg.addColorStop(1,"rgba(0,0,0,.42)");BOX.fillStyle=Rg;BOX.fillRect(0,0,520,420);
 BOX.globalAlpha=.5;BOX.fillStyle=BOX.createPattern(BNZ,"repeat");BOX.fillRect(0,0,520,420);BOX.globalAlpha=1;
 BTX.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);BTX.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";BTX.clearRect(0,0,520,420);BTX.drawImage(BOB,0,0);BTX.globalCompositeOperation="destination-out";BTX.drawImage(BOB,3,4);BTX.globalCompositeOperation="source-in";BTX.fillStyle="rgba(255,255,255,.8)";BTX.fillRect(0,0,520,420);BTX.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";
 BOX.globalCompositeOperation="source-over";BOX.drawImage(BTM,0,0);
 const hue=(b.k*47+20)%360,br=1+Math.sin(time*2.2)*.012;
 g.save();g.fillStyle="rgba(0,0,0,.38)";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(sx,sy+1,b.w*.66,7,0,0,7);g.fill();
 const mg=g.createRadialGradient(sx,sy,4,sx,sy,b.w*.9);mg.addColorStop(0,"hsla("+hue+",70%,70%,.22)");mg.addColorStop(1,"hsla("+hue+",70%,70%,0)");g.fillStyle=mg;g.fillRect(sx-b.w,sy-26,b.w*2,40);
 const D=bDance(b,sx,sy);g.translate(sx+D.x,sy+D.y);g.rotate(D.r);g.scale(D.sx,br*D.sy);g.shadowColor="hsla("+hue+",90%,60%,.65)";g.shadowBlur=16+(D.dz?10:0);g.drawImage(BOB,-260,-360);g.restore();bNotesDraw(sx,sy,b,D)};
let bN=[],bPI=0,bTa=0,bA0=-1,bDz=0;
function bDance(b,sx,sy){const t=time,O={x:0,y:0,r:0,sx:1,sy:1,dz:0};
 if(b.active&&bA0<0)bA0=t;if(!b.active&&!b.dead)bA0=-1;
 if(p&&p.inv>bPI+.3&&!b.dead)bTa=1.3;bPI=p?p.inv:0;if(bTa>0)bTa-=1/60;
 if(b.dead){const k=Math.min(1,(b.dt0=(b.dt0||0)+1/60)/1.4);O.r=k*Math.PI*2*(b.f||1);O.sx=O.sy=1-k*.4;O.y=-Math.sin(k*Math.PI)*40;return O}
 O.r=Math.sin(t*3.2)*.05;O.y=-Math.abs(Math.sin(t*6.4))*5;const sq=Math.sin(t*12.8)*.03;O.sy=1+sq;O.sx=1-sq;
 if(b.hurt>0){O.sx*=1.12;O.sy*=.88}
 const it=bA0>=0?t-bA0:99;if(it<2.4){O.sx*=Math.cos(it*7.85);O.y-=Math.abs(Math.sin(it*6))*26;O.dz=1;if(Math.random()<.25)bN.push({x:sx+(Math.random()-.5)*b.w,y:sy-b.h,vy:-1-Math.random(),vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.4,t:1.6,c:["#f472b6","#fde047","#22d3ee","#a3e635"][Math.floor(Math.random()*4)],ch:Math.random()<.5?"♪":"♫"})}
 else{const ph=(t+(b.k||0)*1.3)%9;if(ph<2.6&&b.active&&!(b.hurt>0)){O.dz=1;const beat=Math.sin(ph*Math.PI*2.4);O.r=Math.sign(beat)*.16;O.x=Math.sin(ph*Math.PI*1.2)*14;O.y-=Math.abs(beat)*16;if(ph>1.6&&ph<2.2)O.sx*=Math.cos((ph-1.6)*Math.PI*3.4);if(Math.random()<.2)bN.push({x:sx+(Math.random()-.5)*b.w,y:sy-b.h-10,vy:-1.2-Math.random(),vx:(Math.random()-.5)*1.6,t:1.5,c:["#f472b6","#fde047","#22d3ee","#a3e635"][Math.floor(Math.random()*4)],ch:Math.random()<.5?"♪":"♫"})}}
 if(bTa>0){O.y+=Math.sin(t*40)*2;O.r+=Math.sin(t*20)*.04}
 return O}
function bNotesDraw(sx,sy,b,D){bN.forEach(n=>{n.t-=1/60;n.x+=n.vx;n.y+=n.vy;g.globalAlpha=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,n.t));g.font="bold 22px sans-serif";g.textAlign="center";g.fillStyle=n.c;g.fillText(n.ch,n.x,n.y);g.globalAlpha=1});bN=bN.filter(n=>n.t>0);
 if(D.dz&&!b.dead){g.save();g.globalCompositeOperation="lighter";["#f472b6","#22d3ee","#fde047"].forEach((c,i)=>{const a=time*2+i*2.1,lx=sx+Math.cos(a)*b.w*.7,ly=sy-8;const rg=g.createRadialGradient(lx,ly,2,lx,ly,40);rg.addColorStop(0,c+"88");rg.addColorStop(1,c+"00");g.fillStyle=rg;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(lx,ly,44,12,0,0,7);g.fill()});g.restore()}
 if(bTa>0&&!b.dead){const q=Math.min(1,(1.3-bTa)*6);g.save();g.translate(sx,sy-b.h-34);g.scale(q,q);txt("¡JA JA JA!",0,0,18,"#fde047","center");g.restore()}}

function swordArt(id,s0,s1,px,py){if(id==="no")return;const C=SWD[id]||SWD.cl,t=time;g.save();g.lineCap="round";if(C.glow){g.shadowColor=C.glow;g.shadowBlur=10}
 const col=id==="ar"?"hsl("+((t*200)%360|0)+",95%,60%)":C.b,L=(a,b,w,c)=>{g.lineWidth=w;g.strokeStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a[0],a[1]);g.lineTo(b[0],b[1]);g.stroke()};
 L(s0,s1,C.w+3,C.o);L(s0,s1,C.w,col);
 if(C.core)L(s0,s1,C.w*.4,"#fff");else L([s0[0]+px*C.w*.2,s0[1]+py*C.w*.2],[s1[0]+px*C.w*.2,s1[1]+py*C.w*.2],1.3,C.e);
 if(C.teeth){g.fillStyle=C.e;for(let i=1;i<=5;i++){const a=i/6,bx=s0[0]+(s1[0]-s0[0])*a,by=s0[1]+(s1[1]-s0[1])*a;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(bx-px*2,by-py*2);g.lineTo(bx+px*7,by+py*7);g.lineTo(bx+(s1[0]-s0[0])*.07-px*2,by+(s1[1]-s0[1])*.07-py*2);g.fill()}}
 if(id==="fi"){for(let i=0;i<4;i++){const a=(i+((t*5)%1))/4,bx=s0[0]+(s1[0]-s0[0])*a,by=s0[1]+(s1[1]-s0[1])*a-Math.sin(t*20+i)*2;g.fillStyle=i%2?"#fde047":"#f97316";g.beginPath();g.arc(bx+px*3,by+py*3-3*(1-a),2.2*(1-a)+1,0,7);g.fill()}}
 if(id==="hi"||id==="mg"){g.fillStyle="#fff";for(let i=0;i<3;i++){const a=((t*.8+i/3)%1),bx=s0[0]+(s1[0]-s0[0])*a,by=s0[1]+(s1[1]-s0[1])*a;g.globalAlpha=1-a;g.fillRect(bx+px*5-1,by+py*5-1,2.4,2.4)}g.globalAlpha=1}
 g.restore()}

function drawPlayer(){if(p.inv>0&&Math.floor(p.inv*14)%2)return;
 const k=clamp(Math.abs(p.vx)/RUN,0,1),air=!p.ground,q=p.atk>0?1-p.atk/.34:-1,ph=p.ph,bob=air?0:(k>.1?Math.abs(Math.sin(ph))*2.6:Math.sin(time*3)*.7),cx=p.x+p.w/2-cam,fy=p.y+p.h+1;
 const lean=k*4+(air?(p.vy<0?-1.5:2):0),hip=[0,-21-bob],sh=[lean,-38-bob],hd=[lean+2,-48-bob],L=10.5,sqx=1+p.sq*.5,sqy=1-p.sq*.5;
 const line=(a,b,c)=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(a[0],a[1]);g.lineTo(b[0],b[1]);if(c)g.lineTo(c[0],c[1]);g.stroke()};
 const SK=skinNow(),EQ={back:eqId("back"),body:eqId("body"),hat:eqId("hat"),face:eqId("face"),sword:eqId("sword")};g.save();g.globalAlpha*=SK.alpha;if(SK.glow){g.shadowColor=SK.glow;g.shadowBlur=9}g.translate(cx,fy);g.scale(p.f*sqx,sqy);g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.strokeStyle=SK.col;g.lineWidth=4.6;
 const leg=i=>{if(sit){const kn=[hip[0]+9,hip[1]+2],ft=[kn[0]+1-i*3,kn[1]+9];line(hip,kn,ft);line(ft,[ft[0]+5,ft[1]]);return}const f=ph+i*Math.PI;let a1,a2;if(air){a1=i?-.55:.6;a2=a1-(i?.5:.9)+(p.vy>0?.5:0)}else{a1=Math.sin(f)*.9*k;a2=a1-Math.max(0,Math.sin(f+.9))*1.15*k-.08*(1-k)}
  const kn=[hip[0]+Math.sin(a1)*L,hip[1]+Math.cos(a1)*L],ft=[kn[0]+Math.sin(a2)*L,kn[1]+Math.cos(a2)*L];line(hip,kn,ft);line(ft,[ft[0]+5,ft[1]])};
 const arm=b=>{const e=[sh[0]+Math.sin(b)*10,sh[1]+Math.cos(b)*10],h=[e[0]+Math.sin(b+.6)*10,e[1]+Math.cos(b+.6)*10];line(sh,e,h)};
 COSF.back(EQ.back,hip,sh,hd,k);leg(1);arm(air?-1:-Math.sin(ph)*.9*k+.15);line(hip,sh);leg(0);COSF.body(EQ.body,hip,sh,hd,k);
 if(ups.sc){g.strokeStyle="#e11d48";g.lineWidth=5;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(sh[0]+2,sh[1]+3);for(let i=1;i<=6;i++)g.lineTo(sh[0]-i*4-k*5,sh[1]+3+Math.sin(time*10+i)*(2+k*3)*(i/6)+i*.6);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle=SK.col;g.lineWidth=4.6}g.restore();const HEAD=()=>{g.save();g.globalAlpha*=SK.alpha;if(SK.glow){g.shadowColor=SK.glow;g.shadowBlur=9}g.translate(cx,fy);g.scale(p.f*sqx,sqy);g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.strokeStyle=SK.col;g.lineWidth=4.6;g.fillStyle=SK.hd;g.beginPath();g.arc(hd[0],hd[1],9.5,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();COSF.head(EQ.hat,EQ.face,hip,sh,hd,k);g.restore()};
 // brazo y espada en coordenadas del mundo: apuntan libremente
 const shx=cx+p.f*sh[0]*sqx,shy=fy+sh[1]*sqy,a=q>=0?swingA(p.th,q):p.as+Math.sin(ph*2)*.08*k,dx=Math.cos(a),dy=Math.sin(a),px=-dy,py=dx,hand=[shx+dx*13,shy+dy*13],K=(p.bg>0?1.7:1)*(1+.15*ups.sw);
 g.save();g.globalAlpha*=SK.alpha;g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.strokeStyle=SK.col;g.lineWidth=4.6;line([shx,shy],hand);
 if(q>=0){const a0=swingA(p.th,0),cc=Math.cos(p.th)<0;g.strokeStyle="rgba("+(ups.fl?"255,140,30":"255,255,255")+","+(1-q)*.9+")";g.lineWidth=6;g.beginPath();g.arc(shx,shy,54*K,a0,a,cc);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="rgba("+(ups.fl?"255,230,80":"120,220,255")+","+(1-q)*.7+")";g.lineWidth=3;g.beginPath();g.arc(shx,shy,60*K,a0,a,cc);g.stroke()}
 g.lineWidth=5;g.strokeStyle="#444";line([hand[0]-dx*5,hand[1]-dy*5],[hand[0]+dx*5,hand[1]+dy*5]);
 g.lineWidth=3.2;g.strokeStyle="#d4a017";line([hand[0]+dx*6+px*6,hand[1]+dy*6+py*6],[hand[0]+dx*6-px*6,hand[1]+dy*6-py*6]);
 const s0=[hand[0]+dx*7,hand[1]+dy*7],s1=[hand[0]+dx*31*K,hand[1]+dy*31*K];swordArt(EQ.sword,s0,s1,px,py);g.restore();HEAD()}
function drawItem(it){const x=it.x-cam+11,y=it.y+11+Math.sin(time*5+it.x)*2,c=ITC[it.k];g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.fillStyle=c+"44";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,17,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#1e1b4b";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,12,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle=c;g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();g.fillStyle=c;g.lineWidth=3;g.lineCap="round";
 if(it.k===0)heart(0,2,true);else if(it.k===1){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,-6);g.lineTo(6,-6);g.lineTo(6,1);g.quadraticCurveTo(6,7,0,9);g.quadraticCurveTo(-6,7,-6,1);g.closePath();g.fill()}
 else if(it.k===2){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,0);g.lineTo(0,-6);g.lineTo(6,0);g.moveTo(-6,6);g.lineTo(0,0);g.lineTo(6,6);g.stroke()}
 else if(it.k===3){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,6);g.lineTo(6,-6);g.moveTo(-6,0);g.lineTo(0,6);g.stroke()}
 else if(it.k>=5)itemIcon(it.k);else{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(2,-8);g.lineTo(-5,1);g.lineTo(0,1);g.lineTo(-2,8);g.lineTo(5,-2);g.lineTo(0,-2);g.closePath();g.fill()}g.restore()}
function heart(x,y,on){g.fillStyle=on?"#ff3355":"#3a3a55";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x,y+6);g.bezierCurveTo(x-12,y-4,x-6,y-14,x,y-6);g.bezierCurveTo(x+6,y-14,x+12,y-4,x,y+6);g.fill()}
function txt(s,x,y,sz,col,al){g.font="bold "+sz+"px monospace";g.textAlign=al||"left";g.fillStyle="#000a";g.fillText(s,x+2,y+2);g.fillStyle=col||"#fff";g.fillText(s,x,y)}
function button(x,y,w,h,label,fn,sel,dis){const hov=!dis&&ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+w&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+h;btns.push({x,y,w,h,fn,dis});if(hov)cv.style.cursor="pointer";
 const cc=dis?["#2a2a35","#1c1c26"]:hov?["#ff7ac0","#db2777"]:sel?["#8b5cf6","#5b21b6"]:["#4c3a9a","#2a1f66"],gr=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+h);gr.addColorStop(0,cc[0]);gr.addColorStop(1,cc[1]);g.fillStyle=gr;g.beginPath();if(g.roundRect)g.roundRect(x,y,w,h,12);else g.rect(x,y,w,h);g.fill();g.strokeStyle=sel?"#ffd166":"#fff3";g.lineWidth=3;g.stroke();txt(label,x+w/2,y+h/2+Math.round(h*.17),Math.min(18,Math.round(h*.44)),dis?"#777":"#fff","center")}
function bg(cx,t){const L=th||LV[0];g.fillStyle=L.sky;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 if(L.night){g.fillStyle="#fff";for(let i=0;i<50;i++){const x=((i*173-cx*.1)%W+W)%W,y=(i*97)%250;g.globalAlpha=.4+.5*Math.abs(Math.sin(t*2+i));g.fillRect(x,y,2,2)}g.globalAlpha=1}
 g.fillStyle=L.cl;for(let i=0;i<9;i++){const x=((i*260-cx*.25)%(W+260)+W+260)%(W+260)-120,y=40+(i*53%110);g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,20,0,7);g.arc(x+24,y-8,26,0,7);g.arc(x+52,y,20,0,7);g.fill()}
 g.fillStyle=L.hill;for(let i=0;i<7;i++){const x=((i*330-cx*.5)%(W+330)+W+330)%(W+330)-160;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,12*T,120+i%3*30,70+i%2*25,0,Math.PI,0);g.fill()}
 if(L.lava){g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.fillRect(0,H-16+Math.sin(t*3)*2,W,30);g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.fillRect(0,H-16+Math.sin(t*3)*2,W,3)}if(L.cb)L.cb(cx,t);if(L.fx)fxDraw(L.fx,cx,t)}

function drawX(){
 btns=[];uiMode();if(S==="roy"){drawRoy(performance.now()/1000);return}if(S==="fgt"){drawFgt(performance.now()/1000);return}if(S==="mz3"){drawMz3(performance.now()/1000);return}if(S==="mzlv"){drawMzLv(performance.now()/1000);return}cv.style.cursor=S==="play"?"crosshair":"default";
 if(S==="talk"&&talk&&talk.back==="menu"){const mt=performance.now()/1000;th=LV[Math.floor(mt/12)%LV.length];bg(mt*40,mt);S="menu";drawMenu(mt);S="talk";return}
 if(S==="menu"||S==="levels"||S==="diff"||S==="shop"||S==="games"||S==="settings"||S==="maze"){const mt=performance.now()/1000;th=LV[Math.floor(mt/12)%LV.length];bg(mt*40,mt);drawMenu(mt);return}
 g.save();bg(cam+bgOff,time);if(shake>0&&CFG.shk)g.translate((Math.random()-.5)*8,(Math.random()-.5)*8);
 plants.forEach(drawPlant);
 const c0=Math.floor(cam/T),c1=Math.min(COLS-1,c0+Math.ceil(W/T)+1);
 for(let r=0;r<ROWS;r++)for(let c=c0;c<=c1;c++){const t=G[r][c];if(t)tile(t,c,r,c*T-cam,r*T)}
 g.fillStyle="#ffd166";coins.forEach(c=>{if(c.got)return;const x=c.x-cam,w=Math.abs(Math.sin(time*5+c.x))*7+2;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,c.y,w,10,0,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#b9770e";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke()});
 cps.forEach(x=>{g.fillStyle="#ddd";g.fillRect(x-cam+12,12*T-70,4,70);g.fillStyle=cp>=x?"#2fb34b":"#e63946";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-cam+16,12*T-70);g.lineTo(x-cam+46,12*T-58);g.lineTo(x-cam+16,12*T-46);g.fill()});
 if(lvl===99){const x=90*T-cam;g.fillStyle="#ddd";g.fillRect(x,12*T-130,5,130);g.fillStyle="#22c55e";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+5,12*T-130);g.lineTo(x+60,12*T-110);g.lineTo(x+5,12*T-90);g.fill();txt("META",x+30,12*T-140,14,"#fff","center")}
 items.forEach(drawItem);enemies.forEach(e=>{if(!e.gone&&e.x-cam>-40&&e.x-cam<W+40)drawEnemy(e)});drawBoss();if(dif===3&&lvl!==99)drawMinis();drawNpcs();drawRide();if(p.star>0)starAura();if(p.frz>0)frzFX();flashFX();
 if(p.sh>0&&(p.sh>2||Math.floor(p.sh*6)%2)){g.strokeStyle="#22d3ee";g.lineWidth=3;g.globalAlpha=.7;g.beginPath();g.arc(p.x+p.w/2-cam,p.y+p.h/2,38+Math.sin(time*8)*2,0,7);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#22d3ee22";g.fill();g.globalAlpha=1}
 balls.forEach(f=>{const x=f.x-cam,sx=Math.sign(f.vx)||1,c=f.c||"#a855f7";
  if(f.k==="z"){const x0=(AR+1)*T-cam,x1=(COLS-2)*T-cam,y=f.y;if(f.warn>0){g.strokeStyle="#ef4444aa";g.lineWidth=3;g.setLineDash([12,10]);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x0,y);g.lineTo(x1,y);g.stroke();g.setLineDash([])}else{g.fillStyle="#ff3355aa";g.fillRect(x0,y-10,x1-x0,20);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(x0,y-4,x1-x0,8)}return}
  if(f.k==="sb"){g.fillStyle="#fff";g.beginPath();g.arc(x,f.y,23,0,7);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#bae6fd";g.lineWidth=3;g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#bae6fd";g.beginPath();g.arc(x-6+Math.cos(time*6)*4,f.y-5,5,0,7);g.fill();return}
  if(f.k==="t"){g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(x-35,f.y-7,70,14);g.fillStyle="#fbcfe8";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+Math.sign(f.vx)*35,f.y,9,0,7);g.fill();return}
  if(f.k==="s"){const gy=12*T;if(f.warn>0){g.fillStyle="#ef444488";for(let i=-1;i<=1;i++){g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x+i*10-6,gy);g.lineTo(x+i*10,gy-14-Math.sin(time*30)*3);g.lineTo(x+i*10+6,gy);g.fill()}}else{g.fillStyle="#7dd3fc";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-16,gy);g.lineTo(x-6,gy-70);g.lineTo(x,gy-30);g.lineTo(x+8,gy-60);g.lineTo(x+16,gy);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#e0f2fe";g.fillRect(x-2,gy-50,3,40)}return}
  if(f.k==="w"){g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(x-22,f.y+12);g.lineTo(x-12,f.y-12);g.lineTo(x,f.y+4);g.lineTo(x+12,f.y-12);g.lineTo(x+22,f.y+12);g.closePath();g.fill()}
  else if(f.k==="c"){g.save();g.translate(x,f.y);g.rotate(time*8);g.fillStyle=c;g.fillRect(-8,-8,16,16);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(-3,-3,6,6);g.restore()}
  else if(f.k==="b"){g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x,f.y,8,4,0,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(x-sx*3,f.y-1,4,2)}
  else{g.fillStyle=c+"66";g.beginPath();g.arc(x-sx*12,f.y-(f.vy||0)*.05,7,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,f.y,10,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fff8";g.beginPath();g.arc(x,f.y,5,0,7);g.fill()}});
 parts.forEach(q=>{g.fillStyle=q.c;g.fillRect(q.x-cam,q.y,6,6)});pops.forEach(o=>txt(o.t,o.x-cam,o.y,16,"#fff","center"));if(th&&th.fx)fxFront(th.fx,cam+bgOff,time);
 g.restore();
 if(S==="play"&&ms.pt==="mouse"&&performance.now()-ms.t<4000){g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.beginPath();g.arc(ms.x,ms.y,9,0,7);g.moveTo(ms.x-14,ms.y);g.lineTo(ms.x-5,ms.y);g.moveTo(ms.x+5,ms.y);g.lineTo(ms.x+14,ms.y);g.stroke()}
 for(let i=0;i<HPM();i++)heart(26+i*28,26,i<p.hp);txt(lvl===99?"TUTORIAL":"NIVEL "+(gi()+1)+" · "+DIFS[dif].n,14,52,13,"#fff");
 [["sh","#22d3ee","ESCUDO"],["dj","#7ae582","SALTO x2"],["bg","#e2e8f0","ESPADA+"],["spd","#ffd166","VELOZ"],["star","#f472b6","ESTRELLA"],["frz","#7dd3fc","HIELO"],["mag","#f87171","IMÁN"]].filter(a=>p[a[0]]>0).forEach((a,i)=>{g.fillStyle="#000a";g.fillRect(12,62+i*20,112,17);g.fillStyle=a[1];g.fillRect(12,62+i*20,112*Math.min(1,p[a[0]]/(({sh:8,dj:20,bg:12,star:9,frz:10,mag:12})[a[0]]||10)),17);txt(a[2]+" "+Math.ceil(p[a[0]]),18,76+i*20,12,"#000")});
 if(lvl===99&&S==="play"){const s=TUT.filter(a=>p.x>=a[0]*T).pop()||TUT[0];g.fillStyle="#000c";g.fillRect(W/2-250,66,500,50);g.strokeStyle="#ffd166";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(W/2-250,66,500,50);txt(s[1],W/2,88,18,"#ffd166","center");txt(s[2],W/2,108,12,"#fff","center")}
 txt("● "+coinsN+"   ♥ x"+lives,W/2,28,18,"#ffd166","center");if(dif===3&&lvl!==99)txt("∞ "+distM()+" m · PELIGRO "+(lap+1),W/2,50,14,"#ff9ff3","center");txt("PUNTOS "+String(score).padStart(6,"0"),W-14,28,18,"#fff","right");
 if(boss.active&&!boss.dead){g.fillStyle="#000a";g.fillRect(W/2-142,H-34,284,20);g.fillStyle=BC[boss.k];g.fillRect(W/2-138,H-30,276*boss.hp/boss.max,12);txt(bnw(boss.k),W/2,H-38,14,"#fff","center")}
 if(S==="over"||S==="win"){const w=S==="win";
  if(R3){anim3D(endT,w);R3.render(SC3,CAM3);g.drawImage(R3.domElement,0,0,W,H)}else{g.fillStyle=w?"#000a":"#000c";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H)}
  const gr=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,150);gr.addColorStop(0,"#000d");gr.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(0,0,W,150);
  const gb=g.createLinearGradient(0,H-90,0,H);gb.addColorStop(0,"#0000");gb.addColorStop(1,"#000c");g.fillStyle=gb;g.fillRect(0,H-90,W,90);
  if(!w){if(dif===3&&lvl!==99){const m=distM();if(m>bestE){bestE=m;try{localStorage.setItem("stk_e",bestE)}catch(e){}}txt("ENDLESS: "+m+" m · Récord "+bestE+" m",W/2,124,16,"#ff9ff3","center")}txt("FIN DEL JUEGO",W/2,56,44,"#ff3355","center");txt("Puntos: "+score+"  ·  ¡Inténtalo de nuevo!",W/2,92,20,"#fff","center");button(210,396,170,40,"REINTENTAR",act.retry);button(420,396,170,40,"MENÚ",toMenu)}
  else if(lvl===99){txt("¡TUTORIAL COMPLETADO!",W/2,56,38,"#ffd166","center");txt("¡Ya sabes jugar! Ganaste una corona",W/2,92,18,"#fff","center");button(210,396,170,40,"JUGAR",()=>newGame(0));button(420,396,170,40,"MENÚ",toMenu)}
  else{txt(gi()<23?"¡NIVEL COMPLETADO!":"¡JUEGO TERMINADO!",W/2,56,42,"#ffd166","center");txt("Derrotaste "+BW[boss.k]+" · ¡Ganaste la corona!",W/2,90,18,"#fff","center");txt("Puntos: "+score+"   Monedas: "+coinsN,W/2,116,16,"#7ef0ff","center");txt("♪ BAILE: "+DANCES[lvl].n+" ♪",W/2,142,15,"#ff9ff3","center");
   if(gi()<23){button(210,396,170,40,"SIGUIENTE",act.next);button(420,396,170,40,"MENÚ",toMenu)}else button(315,396,170,40,"MENÚ",toMenu)}}

 P.grad=grad;P.skyW=grad(["#5fb8ff","#ffd9a0","#ffb36b"]);P.skyL=grad(["#070b16","#1c2740","#3a4a63"]);
 P.hemi=new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xffffff,0x555577,.9);SC3.add(P.hemi);P.pl=new THREE.PointLight(0xffffff,1.2,40);P.pl.position.set(3,7,5);SC3.add(P.pl);
 P.fl=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(18,18,.2,48),M3(0x7ed957));P.fl.position.y=-.1;SC3.add(P.fl);
 P.st=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(3.4,3.4,.25,40),M3(0x9be58a));P.st.position.y=-.02;SC3.add(P.st);

 P.lm=[M3(0x2fb34b),M3(0xff7eb6),M3(0xffd166)];P.trk=M3(0x6b4226);
 for(let i=0;i<18;i++){const a=i*.35+.15,rr=7.5+(i*5)%5*1.3,g3=new THREE.Group(),lm=P.lm[i%3];const tr=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.18,.25,1.4,8),P.trk);tr.position.y=.7;g3.add(tr);
  const c1=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1,2.2,8),lm);c1.position.y=2.3;g3.add(c1);const c2=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.75,1.6,8),lm);c2.position.y=3.4;g3.add(c2);g3.position.set(Math.cos(a)*rr,0,Math.sin(a)*rr);SC3.add(g3)}
 P.cm=M3(0xffffff,{emissive:0x222222});P.cg=new THREE.Group();SC3.add(P.cg);
 for(let i=0;i<10;i++){const g3=new THREE.Group();[[0,0,1.6],[1.6,-.2,1.2],[-1.6,-.2,1.2],[.6,.7,1.1]].forEach(a=>{const s=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(a[2],12,10),P.cm);s.position.set(a[0],a[1],0);g3.add(s)});const an=i*.65,rr=10+(i%4)*2.5;g3.position.set(Math.cos(an)*rr,8+(i%3)*1.3,Math.sin(an)*rr);g3.rotation.y=-an;P.cg.add(g3)}
 P.sun=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(1.8,16,12),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0xfff2a0}));P.sun.position.set(-14,10,-24);SC3.add(P.sun);
 P.pd=[];for(let i=0;i<8;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CircleGeometry(.45+(i*3)%4*.25,20),M3(0x1d3557,{metalness:.9,roughness:.1,emissive:0x0a1a30}));m.rotation.x=-Math.PI/2;const a=i*1.3,rr=1.6+(i%3)*1.1;m.position.set(Math.cos(a)*rr,.14,Math.sin(a)*rr);SC3.add(m);P.pd.push(m)}
 const N=480;P.rp=new Float32Array(N*6);P.rd=[];for(let i=0;i<N;i++)P.rd.push({x:(Math.random()-.5)*24,z:(Math.random()-.5)*24-2,y:Math.random()*11,s:9+Math.random()*5});
 P.rg=new THREE.BufferGeometry();P.rg.setAttribute("position",new THREE.BufferAttribute(P.rp,3));P.rl=new THREE.LineSegments(P.rg,new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({color:0x9bd1ff,transparent:true,opacity:.65}));P.rl.frustumCulled=false;SC3.add(P.rl);
 const blk=M3(0x111111,{roughness:.8}),wh=M3(0xffffff,{roughness:.4}),cyl=(len,rd)=>{const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(rd,rd,len,10),blk);m.position.y=-len/2;return m};
 const G=new THREE.Group();SC3.add(G);P.G=G;const bodyG=new THREE.Group();bodyG.position.y=1.2;G.add(bodyG);P.bodyG=bodyG;
 const torso=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.07,.07,.9,10),blk);torso.position.y=.45;bodyG.add(torso);
 const headG=new THREE.Group();headG.position.y=1.5;bodyG.add(headG);P.headG=headG;
 headG.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.46,28,20),wh));headG.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.51,28,20),new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({color:0x111111,side:THREE.BackSide})));
 [-1,1].forEach(s=>{const e=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05,10,8),blk);e.position.set(s*.16,.07,.42);headG.add(e)});
 P.mouth=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(.13,.025,8,16,Math.PI),blk);P.mouth.position.set(0,-.12,.42);headG.add(P.mouth);
 const arm=()=>{const sh=new THREE.Group();sh.position.set(0,.85,0);bodyG.add(sh);sh.add(cyl(.5,.06));const el=new THREE.Group();el.position.y=-.5;sh.add(el);el.add(cyl(.5,.06));return{sh,el}};P.aL=arm();P.aR=arm();
 const leg=()=>{const h=new THREE.Group();G.add(h);h.position.y=1.2;h.add(cyl(.6,.07));const kn=new THREE.Group();kn.position.y=-.6;h.add(kn);kn.add(cyl(.6,.07));return{h,kn}};P.lL=leg();P.lR=leg();
 const sw=new THREE.Group(),bl=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.07,1.3,.02),M3(0xe6f4ff,{metalness:.8,roughness:.2,emissive:0x335577}));bl.position.y=.8;sw.add(bl);
 const gd=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.4,.07,.07),M3(0xd4a017));gd.position.y=.15;sw.add(gd);sw.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.04,.04,.3,8),blk));P.sw=sw;
 const cr=new THREE.Group(),gold=M3(0xffd24a,{metalness:.7,roughness:.25,emissive:0x664400});cr.add(new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.CylinderGeometry(.4,.36,.22,20,1,true),gold));
 for(let i=0;i<5;i++){const a=i/5*Math.PI*2,c=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(.1,.3,8),gold);c.position.set(Math.cos(a)*.37,.25,Math.sin(a)*.37);cr.add(c);const gm=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05,8,6),M3(i%2?0xff3355:0x22d3ee,{emissive:i%2?0x550011:0x004455}));gm.position.set(Math.cos(a)*.38,.43,Math.sin(a)*.38);cr.add(gm)}P.cr=cr;
 P.cf=[];for(let i=0;i<70;i++){const m=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(.12,.04,.12),M3([0xff4fa3,0xffd166,0x22d3ee,0x7ae582][i%4]));m.userData={x:(Math.random()-.5)*8,z:(Math.random()-.5)*8,s:.6+Math.random(),o:Math.random()*9};SC3.add(m);P.cf.push(m)}
 P.tear=new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.SphereGeometry(.05,8,6),M3(0x7dd3fc,{emissive:0x2266aa}));headG.add(P.tear);
 }catch(e){R3=null}}
function set3D(w){init3D();if(!R3)return;try{const P=P3;if(P.sw.parent)P.sw.parent.remove(P.sw);if(P.cr.parent)P.cr.parent.remove(P.cr);P.lt=null;
 SC3.background=w?P.skyW:P.skyL;SC3.fog=w?new THREE.Fog(0xffd9a0,20,70):new THREE.Fog(0x2a364d,10,40);
 P.fl.material.color.setHex(w?0x7ed957:0x27402f);P.st.material.color.setHex(w?0x9be58a:0x3a4a45);P.hm[0].color.setHex(w?0x6fcf7c:0x1f3b34);P.hm[1].color.setHex(w?0x52c44f:0x183029);
 [[0x2fb34b,0x14352c],[0xff7eb6,0x1d3f45],[0xffd166,0x2a3b2f]].forEach((c,i)=>P.lm[i].color.setHex(w?c[0]:c[1]));P.cm.color.setHex(w?0xffffff:0x3a4252);P.cm.emissive.setHex(w?0x222222:0x000000);
 P.hemi.color.setHex(w?0xffffff:0x9db4d8);P.hemi.groundColor.setHex(w?0x555577:0x1a2433);P.hemi.intensity=w?.95:.45;P.pl.color.setHex(w?0xffffff:0x8fb0ff);P.pl.intensity=w?1.2:.6;
 P.sun.visible=w;P.cf.forEach(m=>m.visible=w);P.pd.forEach(m=>m.visible=!w);P.rl.visible=!w;P.tear.visible=!w;
 if(w){P.aR.el.add(P.sw);P.sw.position.set(0,-.5,0);P.sw.rotation.set(Math.PI,0,0);SC3.add(P.cr)}else{SC3.add(P.sw);P.sw.position.set(1.6,.05,.7);P.sw.rotation.set(Math.PI/2,0,.5)}P.G.rotation.set(0,0,0);P.G.position.set(0,0,0);P.bodyG.rotation.set(0,0,0);if(w&&lvl!==99&&LV[gi()]&&P.grad){const L=LV[gi()];P.skyX=P.skyX||{};SC3.background=P.skyX[gi()]||(P.skyX[gi()]=P.grad([L.sky,L.cl,L.hill]));SC3.fog=new THREE.Fog(new THREE.Color(L.cl),20,70);P.fl.material.color.set(L.gt);P.st.material.color.set(L.br)}}catch(e){R3=null}}
function anim3D(t,w){if(!R3)return;const P=P3,dt=Math.max(0,Math.min(.05,t-(P.lt==null?t:P.lt)));P.lt=t;P.cg.rotation.y=t*.03;
 if(w){const j=Math.abs(Math.sin(t*4))*.35;P.G.position.y=j;P.bodyG.rotation.x=0;P.headG.rotation.set(0,0,Math.sin(t*5)*.1);P.mouth.rotation.z=Math.PI;P.mouth.position.y=-.12;
  P.aL.sh.rotation.set(0,0,-2.6+Math.sin(t*8)*.35);P.aL.el.rotation.z=-.3;P.aR.sh.rotation.set(0,0,2.75+Math.sin(t*6)*.12);P.aR.el.rotation.z=.2;
  P.lL.h.rotation.z=.22;P.lR.h.rotation.z=-.22;P.lL.kn.rotation.x=0;P.lR.kn.rotation.x=0;danceFX(P,t,lvl===99?0:lvl);
  const ct=clamp((t-.4)/.9,0,1),top=3.18+j;let cy=7*(1-ct*ct)+top*ct*ct;if(t>1.3)cy=top+Math.abs(Math.sin((t-1.3)*9))*.28*Math.exp(-(t-1.3)*2.2);
  P.cr.position.set(0,cy,0);P.cr.rotation.set(Math.sin(t*3)*.08,t*1.5,Math.sin(t*2.3)*.08);
  P.cf.forEach(m=>{const u=m.userData,y=(((6-(t*u.s*1.6+u.o))%6)+6)%6;m.position.set(u.x,y,u.z);m.rotation.set(t*u.s*3,t*2,0)});
  const a=t*.55+.3;CAM3.position.set(Math.sin(a)*6.6,2.7+Math.sin(t*.5)*.3,Math.cos(a)*6.6);CAM3.lookAt(0,2,0);P.pl.color.setHSL((t*.2)%1,.6,.85)}
 else{const sag=Math.min(1,t/.9);P.G.position.y=-.15*sag;P.bodyG.rotation.x=.38*sag;P.headG.rotation.set(.45*sag,0,.1*sag);P.mouth.rotation.z=0;P.mouth.position.y=-.2;
  P.aL.sh.rotation.set(.2*sag,0,.18);P.aR.sh.rotation.set(.2*sag,0,-.18);P.aL.el.rotation.z=.1;P.aR.el.rotation.z=-.1;
  P.lL.h.rotation.z=.12;P.lR.h.rotation.z=-.12;P.lL.kn.rotation.x=-.25*sag;P.lR.kn.rotation.x=-.25*sag;
  P.tear.position.set(.16,-((t*.9)%1)*.55,.45);
  P.rd.forEach((d,i)=>{d.y-=d.s*dt;if(d.y<0)d.y=10+Math.random()*2;const k=i*6;P.rp[k]=d.x;P.rp[k+1]=d.y;P.rp[k+2]=d.z;P.rp[k+3]=d.x-.12;P.rp[k+4]=d.y+.55;P.rp[k+5]=d.z});P.rg.attributes.position.needsUpdate=true;
  const ph=t%5,fl=ph<.1?1:(ph>.22&&ph<.3?.7:0);P.hemi.intensity=.45+fl*1.8;P.pl.intensity=.6+fl*3;
  const a=Math.sin(t*.4)*.3,r=7-1.6*Math.min(1,t/1.2);CAM3.position.set(Math.sin(a)*r,2.2,Math.cos(a)*r);CAM3.lookAt(0,1.9,0)}}
function buy(i){const u=UPD[i],l=ups[u.k]||0;if(l>=u.max)return;const c=u.cost[l];if(wallet>=c){wallet-=c;ups[u.k]=(ups[u.k]||0)+1;save();sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06)}else sfx(150,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-50)}
const mp={x:0,y:0,w:24,h:56,vx:0,vy:0,f:1,ground:true,ph:0,sq:0,atk:0,th:0,as:-.85,inv:0,sh:0,dj:0,bg:0,spd:0,air:0};
let mLast=0,mparts=[],mCyc=-1;
function menuScene(mt){const dt=Math.min(.05,Math.max(0,mt-mLast));mLast=mt;const u=mt%14,cyc=Math.floor(mt/14),L=th||LV[0],gy=392;
 for(let i=0;i<36;i++){const x=((i*97+mt*(8+i%7))%W+W)%W,y=70+(i*53)%300+Math.sin(mt*.8+i)*14;g.globalAlpha=.25+.55*Math.abs(Math.sin(mt*1.5+i));g.fillStyle=i%3?"#fff":L.st;g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y,1.5+(i%3),0,7);g.fill()}g.globalAlpha=1;
 g.fillStyle=L.gb;g.fillRect(0,gy,430,60);g.fillStyle=L.gt;g.fillRect(0,gy,430,10);g.fillStyle="#00000030";g.fillRect(0,gy+10,430,3);
 for(let i=0;i<9;i++){g.fillStyle=["#ff7eb6","#ffd166","#fff"][i%3];g.beginPath();g.arc(28+i*46,gy-3+Math.sin(mt*2+i),3,0,7);g.fill()}
 let X=130,jump=0,vx=0,f=1,g0=true,atk=0,as=-.85;
 if(u<2){X=130}else if(u<4.5){X=130+100*(u-2)/2.5;vx=RUN*.55}else if(u<5.3){X=230;jump=Math.sin((u-4.5)/.8*Math.PI)*24;g0=false}
 else if(u<7.4){X=230;for(const s0 of[5.6,6.4])if(u>=s0&&u<s0+.34)atk=.34-(u-s0)}
 else if(u<10.4){X=230-100*(u-7.4)/3;f=-1;vx=-RUN*.6;as=Math.PI+.85}else{X=130;as=-1.3+Math.sin(mt*4)*.1;jump=u>11&&u<11.6?Math.sin((u-11)/.6*Math.PI)*18:0;g0=jump===0}
 if(f>0&&u<7.4)as=-.85;
 mp.f=f;mp.vx=vx;mp.ground=g0;mp.vy=g0?0:(u<4.9?-300:300);mp.atk=atk;mp.th=-.3;mp.as=as;mp.sq=(u>=5.3&&u<5.45)?.22:0;if(g0)mp.ph+=Math.abs(vx)*dt*.075;
 mp.x=-mp.w/2;mp.y=-mp.h-1-jump;
 // robotin que aparece y es derrotado
 const rx=u>=2&&u<6.5?410-(u-2)*25:u>=6.5&&u<7.1?410-4.5*25:-99;
 if(u>=6.5&&mCyc!==cyc){mCyc=cyc;for(let i=0;i<16;i++)mparts.push({x:rx,y:gy-30,vx:(Math.random()-.5)*300,vy:-Math.random()*280,l:.8,c:i%2?"#94a3b8":"#fde047"})}
 if(rx>-50){const sc=cam,st=time;cam=0;time=mt;g.save();g.translate(rx,gy);g.scale(2,2);drawEnemy({t:0,x:-14,y:-26,w:28,h:26,dead:u>=6.5?.3:0,ph:0});g.restore();cam=sc;time=st}
 const sp=p,sc2=cam,st2=time;p=mp;cam=0;time=mt;g.save();g.translate(X,gy);g.scale(2.4,2.4);drawPlayer();g.restore();p=sp;cam=sc2;time=st2;
 mparts.forEach(q=>{q.x+=q.vx*dt;q.y+=q.vy*dt;q.vy+=600*dt;q.l-=dt;g.fillStyle=q.c;g.fillRect(q.x,q.y,6,6)});mparts=mparts.filter(q=>q.l>0)}
