const rrect=(x,y,w,h,r)=>{g.beginPath();if(g.roundRect)g.roundRect(x,y,w,h,r);else g.rect(x,y,w,h)};
const pv={x:-12,y:-57,w:24,h:56,vx:0,vy:0,f:1,ground:true,ph:0,sq:0,atk:0,th:0,as:-.85,inv:0,sh:0,dj:0,bg:0,spd:0,air:0};
function drawChar(X,Y,s,t,as){const sp=p,sc=cam,st=time;p=pv;cam=0;time=t;pv.x=-pv.w/2;pv.y=-pv.h-1;pv.as=as!=null?as:-.85+Math.sin(t*2)*.12;g.save();g.translate(X,Y);g.scale(s,s);drawPlayer();g.restore();p=sp;cam=sc;time=st}
function miniIcon(slot,id,x,y,s){g.save();rrect(x,y,s,s,12);g.clip();const gr=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x,y+s);gr.addColorStop(0,"#2d2d66");gr.addColorStop(1,"#14142e");g.fillStyle=gr;g.fillRect(x,y,s,s);
 if(slot==="sword"){swordArt(id,[x+13,y+s-13],[x+s-13,y+13],.7,.7);g.restore();return}const sv=tryOn;tryOn={slot,id,only:true};const Z={skin:[.95,28],hat:[2.3,48],face:[2.8,48],body:[1.8,30],back:[1.8,30]}[slot];drawChar(x+s/2+(slot==="back"?9:slot==="face"?-13:0),y+s/2+Z[1]*Z[0],Z[0],performance.now()/1000,slot==="face"||slot==="hat"?1.9:-1.2);tryOn=sv;g.restore()}
function upIcon(k,x,y){g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.lineWidth=2.6;g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.fillStyle="#ffd166";
 const bolt=c=>{g.fillStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(3,-10);g.lineTo(-6,2);g.lineTo(0,2);g.lineTo(-3,10);g.lineTo(7,-3);g.lineTo(1,-3);g.closePath();g.fill()},sword=c=>{g.strokeStyle=c;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-8,8);g.lineTo(8,-8);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#d4a017";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,2);g.lineTo(-2,9);g.stroke()};
 const I={dj:()=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-8,1);g.lineTo(0,-6);g.lineTo(8,1);g.moveTo(-8,9);g.lineTo(0,2);g.lineTo(8,9);g.stroke()},
 hp:()=>heart(0,2,true),lf:()=>{heart(-2,3,true);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillRect(4,-9,8,2.6);g.fillRect(6.7,-11.7,2.6,8)},
 sw:()=>sword("#fff"),dm:()=>sword("#ff6b6b"),spd:()=>bolt("#ffd166"),ps:()=>bolt("#7dd3fc"),
 mg:()=>{g.strokeStyle="#ef4444";g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,7,Math.PI,0);g.lineTo(7,8);g.moveTo(-7,0);g.lineTo(-7,8);g.stroke();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-7,8);g.lineTo(-7,10);g.moveTo(7,8);g.lineTo(7,10);g.stroke()},
 gm:()=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(-3,2,6,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.arc(4,-3,6,0,7);g.fill();g.stroke()},
 si:()=>{g.fillStyle="#22d3ee";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-8,-8);g.lineTo(8,-8);g.lineTo(8,0);g.quadraticCurveTo(8,8,0,11);g.quadraticCurveTo(-8,8,-8,0);g.closePath();g.fill();g.stroke()},
 rv:()=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,8,.6,5.6);g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(4,-9);g.lineTo(9,-7);g.lineTo(5,-2);g.stroke()},
 jh:()=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,9);g.lineTo(0,-8);g.moveTo(-6,-2);g.lineTo(0,-9);g.lineTo(6,-2);g.stroke()},
 pw:()=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,9,0,7);g.stroke();g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-5);g.lineTo(0,0);g.lineTo(5,3);g.stroke()},
 sc:()=>{g.strokeStyle="#e11d48";g.lineWidth=4;g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,0);g.quadraticCurveTo(-4,-8,0,0);g.quadraticCurveTo(4,8,9,0);g.stroke()},
 fl:()=>{g.fillStyle="#fb923c";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(0,-11);g.quadraticCurveTo(10,-1,5,7);g.quadraticCurveTo(0,11,-5,7);g.quadraticCurveTo(-9,0,0,-11);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,5,3.5,0,7);g.fill()},
 rg:()=>{g.fillStyle="#4ade80";g.fillRect(-3,-9,6,18);g.fillRect(-9,-3,18,6)},
 bl:()=>{g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-9,6);g.lineTo(-9,-6);g.lineTo(-4,0);g.lineTo(0,-8);g.lineTo(4,0);g.lineTo(9,-6);g.lineTo(9,6);g.closePath();g.fill()},
 pb:()=>{g.beginPath();g.moveTo(-6,9);g.lineTo(6,5);g.lineTo(-6,1);g.lineTo(6,-3);g.lineTo(-6,-7);g.stroke()}};
 (I[k]||(()=>{g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,8,0,7);g.fill()}))();g.restore()}
function cosAct(slot,it){if(isOwned(slot,it.id)){cos.eq[slot]=it.id;saveCos();sfx(660,.1,"square",.06);return}
 if(wallet>=it.c){wallet-=it.c;cos.own[slot+":"+it.id]=1;cos.eq[slot]=it.id;save();saveCos();sfx(988,.1,"square",.06);sfx(1319,.2,"square",.06);noticeTxt="¡Compraste "+it.n+"!";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2500}
 else{sfx(150,.2,"sawtooth",.06,-50);noticeTxt="Te faltan "+(it.c-wallet)+" monedas";noticeUntil=performance.now()+2200}}
function drawShop(mt){
 g.fillStyle="#070714e8";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);const gl=g.createLinearGradient(0,0,0,H);gl.addColorStop(0,"#2d1f7a66");gl.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=gl;g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 for(let i=0;i<26;i++){g.fillStyle="rgba(255,230,150,"+(.15+.3*Math.abs(Math.sin(mt*1.3+i)))+")";g.fillRect((i*131)%W,(i*71+mt*6)%H,2,2)}
 g.strokeStyle="#ffd16688";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(8,8,W-16,H-16,18);g.stroke();
 txt("TIENDA",26,48,34,"#ffd166");
 g.fillStyle="#000a";rrect(W-196,18,176,38,19);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffd166";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#ffd166";g.beginPath();g.arc(W-172,37,10,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#b9770e";g.fillRect(W-175,31,6,12);txt(""+wallet,W-152,44,20,"#ffd166");
 const TB=["MEJORAS","SKINS","CABEZA","ARMADURA","ESPALDA","CARA","ESPADAS","OFERTAS"],SL=[null,"skin","hat","body","back","face","sword"];
 TB.forEach((n,i)=>button(12+i*98,64,95,30,n,()=>{shopTab=i;shopPg=0},i===shopTab||(i===1&&shopTab>=8)));skinNav();if(shopTab===7){drawCombos(mt);return}if(shopTab===8){drawChars(mt);return}if(shopTab===9){drawPets(mt);return}
 const slot=SL[shopTab],items=slot?COS[slot]:UPD,per=6,pages=Math.ceil(items.length/per);shopPg=clamp(shopPg,0,pages-1);
 const pg=items.slice(shopPg*per,shopPg*per+per);let hov=null;
 pg.forEach((it,j)=>{const x=228+(j%2)*280,y=104+Math.floor(j/2)*92;if(ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+270&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+82)hov=it});
 tryOn=(slot&&hov)?{slot,id:hov.id}:null;
 g.fillStyle="#0e0e2a";rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();const pgd=g.createRadialGradient(114,250,10,114,250,130);pgd.addColorStop(0,"#6d4aff55");pgd.addColorStop(1,"#0000");g.fillStyle=pgd;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffffff22";g.lineWidth=2;rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.stroke();
 g.fillStyle="#0007";g.beginPath();g.ellipse(114,338,52,8,0,0,7);g.fill();
 g.save();rrect(14,104,200,266,16);g.clip();drawChar(104,338,2.55,mt,-.6+Math.sin(mt*2)*.1);g.restore();
 txt(tryOn?"PROBANDO…":"TU ESTILO",114,128,13,tryOn?"#ff9ff3":"#ffd166","center");
 tryOn=null;
 pg.forEach((it,j)=>{const x=228+(j%2)*280,y=104+Math.floor(j/2)*92,w=270,h=82;
  if(!slot){const i=UPD.indexOf(it),l=ups[it.k]||0,mx=l>=it.max,c=mx?0:it.cost[l],can=wallet>=c;
   button(x,y,w,h,"",()=>buy(i),mx);
   g.fillStyle="#0008";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+36,y+41,24,0,7);g.fill();upIcon(it.k,x+36,y+41);
   txt(it.n,x+70,y+26,15,"#fff");txt(it.d,x+70,y+46,11,"#cbd5e1");
   for(let q=0;q<it.max;q++){g.fillStyle=q<l?"#ffd166":"#ffffff33";g.beginPath();g.arc(x+74+q*15,y+66,5,0,7);g.fill()}
   txt(mx?"MÁXIMO":"● "+c,x+w-12,y+70,16,mx?"#7ae582":can?"#ffd166":"#ef4444","right")}
  else{const own=isOwned(slot,it.id),eq=cos.eq[slot]===it.id;
   button(x,y,w,h,"",()=>cosAct(slot,it),eq);miniIcon(slot,it.id,x+8,y+8,66);
   txt(it.n,x+84,y+32,14,"#fff");
   if(eq)txt("EQUIPADO ✓",x+84,y+62,15,"#7ae582");else if(own)txt("EQUIPAR",x+84,y+62,15,"#7dd3fc");else txt("● "+it.c,x+84,y+62,16,wallet>=it.c?"#ffd166":"#ef4444")}});
 if(pages>1){button(228,388,90,36,"◀",()=>shopPg=Math.max(0,shopPg-1),false,shopPg===0);txt((shopPg+1)+" / "+pages,W/2+90,412,15,"#fff","center");button(440,388,90,36,"▶",()=>shopPg=Math.min(pages-1,shopPg+1),false,shopPg===pages-1)}

function drawLevels(mt){
 txt("NIVELES",W/2,44,34,"#ffd166","center");if(dif===3)txt("∞ ENDLESS",W-20,40,15,"#ff9ff3","right");
 const per=8,pages=Math.ceil(LV.length/per);lvPg=clamp(lvPg,0,pages-1);
 for(let j=0;j<per;j++){const i=lvPg*per+j;if(i>=LV.length)break;const x=18+(j%4)*194,y=60+Math.floor(j/4)*156,L=LV[i],lk=i>=unlocked,w=180;
  g.save();rrect(x,y,w,74,10);g.clip();g.fillStyle=L.sky;g.fillRect(x,y,w,74);
  if(L.night){g.fillStyle="#fff";for(let q=0;q<9;q++)g.fillRect(x+(q*37)%w,y+(q*23)%40,2,2)}
  g.fillStyle=L.hill;g.beginPath();g.ellipse(x+90,y+64,98,34,0,Math.PI,0);g.fill();g.fillStyle=L.gt;g.fillRect(x,y+58,w,16);g.fillStyle=L.gb;g.fillRect(x,y+64,w,10);if(L.lava){g.fillStyle="#f97316";g.fillRect(x,y+70,w,4)}
  if(lk){g.fillStyle="#000b";g.fillRect(x,y,w,74);txt("BLOQUEADO",x+w/2,y+44,16,"#aaa","center")}g.restore();
  g.strokeStyle=i===gi()?"#ffd166":"#fff4";g.lineWidth=3;rrect(x,y,w,74,10);g.stroke();
  txt((i+1)+" · "+L.n,x+w/2,y+92,13,"#fff","center");
  txt((i>=14?"Mundo 2 ★"+(i-13):i<3?"Clásico":i<6?"Difícil "+"★".repeat(i-2):"Extremo ★"+(i-5))+" · "+BN[i],x+w/2,y+108,9,"#ffd166","center");
  button(x+14,y+114,w-28,26,lk?"BLOQUEADO":"JUGAR",()=>{wld=i>=14?1:0;lvl=i-14*wld;act.play()},i===gi(),lk)}
 button(18,404,120,34,"◀",()=>lvPg=Math.max(0,lvPg-1),false,lvPg===0);txt((lvPg+1)+" / "+pages,W/2,428,16,"#fff","center");button(W-138,404,120,34,"▶",()=>lvPg=Math.min(pages-1,lvPg+1),false,lvPg===pages-1);
 button(300,404,200,34,"VOLVER",()=>S="menu")}
function gear(x,y,r,a){g.save();g.translate(x,y);g.rotate(a);g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";for(let i=0;i<8;i++){g.rotate(Math.PI/4);g.fillRect(-1.5,-r-2,3,4)}g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,r,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#101030";g.beginPath();g.arc(0,0,r*.4,0,7);g.fill();g.restore()}
function gamesBtn(x,y,w,h,mt){const hov=ms.x>=x&&ms.x<=x+w&&ms.y>=y&&ms.y<=y+h;btns.push({x,y,w,h,fn:()=>S="games"});if(hov)cv.style.cursor="pointer";
 const gr=g.createLinearGradient(x,y,x+w,y);gr.addColorStop(0,hov?"#fde047":"#fbbf24");gr.addColorStop(1,hov?"#fb923c":"#f97316");g.save();g.shadowColor="#ffb300";g.shadowBlur=10+6*Math.sin(mt*3);g.fillStyle=gr;rrect(x,y,w,h,12);g.fill();g.restore();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2.5;rrect(x,y,w,h,12);g.stroke();
 g.fillStyle="#7c2d12";rrect(x+14,y+7,28,16,6);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#fde68a";g.fillRect(x+19,y+13.5,9,2.6);g.fillRect(x+22,y+10.5,2.6,8.6);g.beginPath();g.arc(x+34,y+12.5,2,0,7);g.arc(x+37,y+17,2,0,7);g.fill();
 g.font="bold 17px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillStyle="#4a1d00";g.fillText("JUEGOS",x+w/2+16,y+h/2+6)}

 g.fillStyle="#070714ec";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);

 const ix=x+10,iy=y+10;g.save();g.translate(ix+iw*.673,iy+ih*.877);g.rotate(-.07);g.fillStyle="#dc2626";rrect(-86,-27,172,54,12);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=4;g.stroke();g.font="bold 26px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.lineWidth=5;g.strokeStyle="#450a0a";g.strokeText("24 JEFES",0,9);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.fillText("24 JEFES",0,9);g.restore();
 g.fillStyle="#fff";rrect(ix+4,iy+ih*.875,iw*.4,ih*.115,10);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#0f172a";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();g.font="bold 12px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillStyle="#0f172a";g.fillText("SOLO PC · v32.0",ix+4+iw*.2,iy+ih*.875+ih*.078);
 g.fillStyle="#16a34a";rrect(x+w-146,y-11,128,22,11);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();txt("● DISPONIBLE",x+w-82,y+4,11,"#fff","center");

 DEV.forEach((d,i)=>{const cx=432,cy=64+i*108;
  g.fillStyle="#101030";rrect(cx,cy,344,98,14);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#ffffff2a";g.lineWidth=2;g.stroke();
  g.save();rrect(cx+8,cy+8,120,82,10);g.clip();const gg=g.createLinearGradient(cx,cy,cx+128,cy+90);gg.addColorStop(0,["#134e4a","#7c2d12","#7f1d1d"][i]);gg.addColorStop(1,"#0b0b22");g.fillStyle=gg;g.fillRect(cx+8,cy+8,120,82);
  g.strokeStyle="#ffffff99";g.lineWidth=3;g.lineCap="round";g.lineJoin="round";
  if(i===0){g.strokeRect(cx+18,cy+16,100,66);g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx+18,cy+38);g.lineTo(cx+78,cy+38);g.lineTo(cx+78,cy+58);g.lineTo(cx+46,cy+58);g.moveTo(cx+46,cy+82);g.lineTo(cx+46,cy+66);g.moveTo(cx+98,cy+16);g.lineTo(cx+98,cy+44);g.moveTo(cx+98,cy+60);g.lineTo(cx+98,cy+82);g.moveTo(cx+62,cy+16);g.lineTo(cx+62,cy+28);g.stroke();g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.beginPath();g.arc(cx+30,cy+28,5,0,7);g.fill();g.fillStyle="#4ade80";g.fillRect(cx+104,cy+66,8,8)}
  else if(i===1){g.fillStyle="#fbbf24";g.beginPath();g.moveTo(cx+44,cy+38);g.lineTo(cx+48,cy+16);g.lineTo(cx+58,cy+28);g.lineTo(cx+68,cy+12);g.lineTo(cx+78,cy+28);g.lineTo(cx+88,cy+16);g.lineTo(cx+92,cy+38);g.closePath();g.fill();g.fillStyle="#ffffffaa";[[22,54],[90,54]].forEach(t=>{g.fillRect(cx+t[0],cy+t[1],24,30);for(let q=0;q<3;q++)g.fillRect(cx+t[0]+q*10,cy+t[1]-6,5,6)});g.fillStyle="#60a5fa";g.fillRect(cx+56,cy+52,24,32);g.strokeStyle="#fff";g.lineWidth=2;g.strokeRect(cx+56,cy+52,24,32);g.fillStyle="#fff";g.beginPath();g.arc(cx+68,cy+68,5,0,7);g.fill()}
  else{g.lineWidth=4;[[46,"#ef4444",1],[92,"#38bdf8",-1]].forEach(a=>{const x=cx+a[0],y=cy+80,f=a[2];g.strokeStyle=a[1];g.beginPath();g.arc(x,y-48,7,0,7);g.moveTo(x,y-41);g.lineTo(x,y-20);g.lineTo(x-8,y);g.moveTo(x,y-20);g.lineTo(x+8,y);g.moveTo(x,y-36);g.lineTo(x+f*18,y-38);g.moveTo(x,y-34);g.lineTo(x-f*8,y-24);g.stroke()});g.fillStyle="#fde047";g.font="bold 20px monospace";g.textAlign="center";g.fillText("VS",cx+69,cy+30);g.fillStyle="#fff";[[cx+66,cy+40]].forEach(s=>{for(let k=0;k<6;k++){const a=k*1.05;g.fillRect(s[0]+Math.cos(a)*10,s[1]+Math.sin(a)*10,3,3)}})}
  g.fillStyle="#0006";g.fillRect(cx+8,cy+8,120,82);g.restore();
  txt(d[0],cx+142,cy+34,16,"#fff");txt(d[1],cx+142,cy+54,10,"#9ca3af");
  if(i===0||(i===1&&typeof royOpen==="function")||i===2){g.fillStyle="#16a34a";rrect(cx+266,cy-8,70,18,9);g.fill();txt("● ABIERTO",cx+301,cy+5,9,"#fff","center");button(cx+142,cy+62,172,28,"ABRIR ▶",()=>{if(i===0)S="maze";else if(i===1){if(typeof royOpen==="function")royOpen()}else fgOpen()})}else{g.fillStyle="#f59e0b22";rrect(cx+142,cy+64,172,24,12);g.fill();g.strokeStyle="#f59e0b";g.lineWidth=1.5;g.stroke();gear(cx+158,cy+76,7,mt*1.5);txt("EN DESARROLLO",cx+172,cy+81,11,"#fbbf24")}});

 g.fillStyle=S==="menu"?"#0002":"#0007";g.fillRect(0,0,W,H);
 if(S==="menu"){menuScene(mt);
  const ty=Math.sin(mt*1.6)*3;g.save();g.textAlign="center";g.shadowColor="#ffb300";g.shadowBlur=22;const tg=g.createLinearGradient(0,30,0,104);tg.addColorStop(0,"#fff4b0");tg.addColorStop(1,"#ff9f1c");g.fillStyle=tg;g.strokeStyle="#4a1d00";g.lineWidth=5;g.font="bold 46px monospace";

  button(10,10,138,28,"⚙ AJUSTES",()=>S="settings");button(470,154,200,30,"JUGAR",act.play);button(470,192,200,30,"NIVELES",()=>S="levels");button(470,230,200,30,"DIFICULTAD: "+DIFS[dif].n,()=>S="diff");button(470,268,200,30,tutDone?"TUTORIAL":"TUTORIAL ★",()=>newGame(99));button(470,306,200,30,"TIENDA  ● "+wallet,()=>S="shop");gamesBtn(470,344,200,30,mt);
  txt("v32.0",W-12,H-12,13,"#ffffffaa","right");txt("Partidas con mejoras: "+plays+"/15 · se reinician al completar 15",W/2,H-30,12,plays>=14?"#fbbf24":"#e2e8f0","center");if(performance.now()<noticeUntil){g.fillStyle="#7f1d1dee";g.fillRect(30,6,740,34);txt(noticeTxt,W/2,28,14,"#fff","center")}
  txt("Clic izquierdo = espada · Enter = jugar · M = música",W/2,H-12,12,"#fffa","center")}
 if(S==="levels")drawLevels(mt);if(S==="shop")drawShop(mt);if(S==="settings")drawSettings(mt);if(S==="games")drawGames(mt);if(S==="maze")drawMaze(mt);
 if(S==="diff"){txt("DIFICULTAD",W/2,70,44,"#ffd166","center");
  DIFS.forEach((d,i)=>{button(200,90+i*64,400,56,(i===3?"∞ ":"")+d.n+" · "+d.d,()=>{dif=i;save()},i===dif)});button(300,360,200,44,"VOLVER",()=>S="menu")}
}
build();S="menu";let last=performance.now(),acc=0;
function loop(n){acc+=Math.min(.1,(n-last)/1000);last=n;while(acc>=1/60){update(1/60);acc-=1/60}draw();requestAnimationFrame(loop)}
requestAnimationFrame(n=>{last=n;loop(n)});
}catch(e){document.body.innerHTML="<p style=padding:20px>No se pudo iniciar el juego: "+e.message+"</p>";console.error(e)}})();
</script></body></html>
